abhiṣekonāma tṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ vajrācāryādisarva karmaprasarasādhanālakṣaṇamahoddeśaḥ ||
namaḥ śrīkālacakrāya ||
dattaṃ yena hayādikaṃ daśavidhaṃ dānaṃ ca dānārthine puṇyajñānabalena tena mahatā mārādayo dhvaṃsitāḥ
siktvā śrīmati dharmadhātuvimale vāgīśvare maṇḍale viśvaṃ vyākṛtamekaśāstṛviṣaye buddhāya tasmai namaḥ ||
praṇamyaivaṃ trikāyāgraṃ kālacakraṃ mahāsukham
trimaṇḍalatrivajrāgraṃ ṭīkā'bhiṣekapaṭale ghoṣavajramanakṣaram ||
mūlatantrāvabodhataḥ'|
likhyate'tra mayā tantre puṇyajñānaphalāptaye ||
ih śrīmati kalāpagrāmadakṣiṇe' malayodyāne kālacakramaṇḍala gṛhapūrva dvārāvasāne ' mahāmaṇiratnamaṇḍape mahāmaṇiratnasiṃhāsanasthena yaśorājñā nirmitakāyena mañjuśriyā sūryarathādhyeṣitena tathāgatavyākṛtena paramādibuddhāt sucandrādhyeṣaṇārthapratipādakaṃ laghu- tantre'bhiṣekapaṭale prathamavṛttaṃ deśitam
tadeva mayā lokeśvareṇa nirmitakāyena puṇḍarī- keṇa tathāgatavyākṛtena mañjuśriyā coditena mahoddeśa ṭīkayā vitanyate dehe viśvasya mānamityādinā - dehe viśvasya mānaṃ dinaniśisamayo māsasaṃkrāntibhedā nāḍīnāṃ sūkṣmasaṃkhyā prakṛtiṣu puruṣastīthikānāṃ mataṃ ca
veda: (de) kartā ( ) dibhedaḥ śrutamiti hi mayā maṇḍalaṃ deśanīyaṃ śrutvā socandravākyaṃ pravadati sugato maṇḍalaṃ kālacakram ||
1||
iha dehe viśvasya mānamityādinā maṇḍalaṃ deśanīyamiti paryantaṃ sucandrādhyeṣaṇam
tataḥ śrutvā saucandravākyaṃ pravadati sugato maṇḍalaṃ kālacakramityādi samastābhiṣeka- paṭalavṛtteṣu tathāgataprati vacanaṃ punaradhyeṣaṇā'bhāvaḥ paṭalāntaṃ yāvaditi
atra sucandra āha—- iha dehe bhagavan !
yad bhagavatoktamadhyātmapaṭale - 'viśvasya mānaṃ dinaniśisamayaṃ māsasaṃkrāntibhedāt ' [ ityā]di 'vede' kartrādibhevaḥ' iti paryantaṃ śrutaṃ mayā, sarva jñāta- mityarthaḥ
tadidānīṃ sattvānāṃ puṇyajñānalābhāya bhagavatā maṇḍalaṃ deśanīyam śiṣyāṇāṃ sekadānāya pratimādīnāṃ pratiṣṭhākaraṇāya daśatattvasaṃyuktalaukikasiddhisādha- vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ abhiṣeka- nāyākaniṣṭha bhuvanaparyantaṃ laukika satyeneti
paramārthasatyena rajomaṇḍalālekhanaṃ nāsti, bhagavataḥ pratiṣedhāt
tathāha bhagavān ādibuddhe- pātanaṃ vajrasūtrāṇāṃ rajaso'pi nipātanam
na kuryānmantratattvena kurvato bodhi durlabhaḥ ||
iti
iha yadi mantratattvena, mantramiti jñānam, manastrāṇabhūtatvāt tena mantratattvena yadi mahāmudrāsiddhayarthaṃ sūtrapātanādikaṃ karoti vajrācārya:, tadā tasya kurvato bodhi (dhiḥ ) durlabhā bhavatīti tathāgataniyamaḥ
tena kāraṇenedaṃ sucandrādhyeṣaṇaṃ laukikasiddhisādhanārthaṃ puṇpasambhārārtham, na mahāmudrāsiddhisādhanārthaṃ jñānasambhārārthamiti
atra jñānasādhanāyāparaṃ maṇḍalatrayaṃ bhagavatoktam
tadyathā- kāyendriyaṃ bhagaścittaṃ maṇḍalaṃ trividhaṃ bhavet
kāyavākcittavajrāṇāṃ nāparaṃ pañcaraṅgikam ||
iti
ato mahāsukhasādhanāya rajomaṇḍalaṃ na bhavati, uttarābhiṣekadānāya ceti sucandrādhyeṣaṇam
tadevādhyeṣaṇāvacanaṃ saucandravākyaṃ śrutvā prakarṣeṇa vadati sugato maṇḍalaṃ kālacakraṃ sarvaṃ deśayatītyarthaḥ
sarvaṃ vakṣyamāṇakramata iti deśakādhyeṣaka 'vacana- saṃgrahaḥ ||
1 ||
idānīṃ vajrācāryaparīkṣāṃ gurvārādhanāya dvitīyavṛttenāha ādāvityādinā - ādī saṃsevanīyo gururapi samayī vajrayānādhirūḍha- stattvadhyāyī śvalubdho vyapagata kaluṣaḥ kṣāntiśīlo'dhvavartī
śiṣyāṇāṃ mārgadātā narakabhayaharastattvato brahmacārī mārāṇāṃ vajradaṇḍaḥ sa ca dharaṇitale vajrasattvaḥ prasiddhaḥ ||
2 ||
iha mantranaye prathamaṃ laukikalokottarasiddhikāṅkṣibhiḥ śiṣyairguruḥ sevanīyaḥ, taṃ ca samyak parīkṣayitvā vajrācāryaparīkṣoktavidhinā
anyathā parīkṣālakṣaṇa- rahitasya gurorārādhanena śiṣyāṇāṃ dharmaviparyāso bhavatīti, dharmaviparyāsannarakagamanaṃ bhavati
[ 162b] ata āha—- ādau saṃsevanīyo gururapi samayoti
iha samayo dvividho bāhya ādhyātmiko neyanītārthenāvagantavyo vakṣyamāṇe ( ṇaḥ), samayo'syāstīti samayī gururādau samyak prakāreṇa sevanīyaḥ, putrakalatrādibhirārādhanīya ityarthaḥ

iha vajrayānaṃ samyak saṃbuddhayānam, tīrthika śrāvaka- pratyekabuddhayānānāmabhedyatvāt
vajraṃ mokṣo yāyate'neneti vajrayānam, tasminnadhirūḍho vajrayānādhirūḍha iti
tattvadhyāyī
iha tattvaṃ dvidhā - laukika siddhisādhakaṃ samyak- saṃbuddhatvasādhakamiti vakṣyamāṇe vaktavyaṃ paramākṣarajñānasiddhau pañcame 'jñānapaṭale
tadeva tattvaṃ dhyātuṃ śīlamasyetyarthaḥ
alubdha iti sarvaputrakalatrādisvaśarīranirapekṣa iti
vyapagatakaluṣa iti

kṣāntiśīla iti
kṣāntau phalanirapekṣā svābhāvikī pravṛttirasya
adhyavartī, adhvā' samyaksambuddhamārgaḥ, tatra vartata iti
asau gururārādhitaḥ san śiṣyāṇāṃ mārgadātā narakabhayaharo bhavati
tattvato brahmacārī yaḥ paramākṣarasukhaprāpto mārāṇāṃ skandhakleśamṛtyudevaputramārāṇāṃ caturṇāṃ vajradaṇḍa iva vajradaṇḍaḥ
sa ca dharaṇitale vajrasattvaḥ prasiddha nirmitakāyeneti vajrācārya sevā- niyamaḥ ||
2 ||
idānīṃ duṣṭācārya doṣaparīkṣārthamiha tṛtīyavṛttenaivāha mānītyādinā - mānī krodhābhibhūtaḥ samayavirahito dravyalubdho'śrutaśca śiṣyāṇāṃ vañcanārthī paramasukhapade naṣṭacitto na siktaḥ
bhogāsaktaḥ pramattaḥ sakaṭukavacanaḥ kāmukaścendriyārthaṃ śiṣyaiḥ sambodhihetorna rakamiva budhairvarjanīyaḥ sa eva ||
3 ||
iha mantranaye mānādidoṣasahito gururyaḥ sa guruḥ śiṣyairvarjanīyaḥ, kṛto'pi guru: samyaksambodhihetornarakamiva budhaiḥ paṇḍitairiti
māno'syāstīti mānī
māno'pya- nekadhā - paṇḍitābhimānaḥ, dravyaiśvaryābhimānaḥ, daśatattvaparijñānamārga rūpādyabhimānaḥ, sa yasyāsti sa varjanīyaḥ
adho'dhaḥ sattvān paśyanniti mānī, uttamottama - sattvān paśyan mānarahito bhavati samyak mārgavetteti, tena mānī karuṇārahito varjanīyaḥ, tathā krodhenābhibhūtaḥ
samayavirahita iti lokajugupsitairgṛhya samayaiḥ prakaṭenā caritaiḥ samayavirahito bhavati, so'pi varjanīyaḥ
dravyalubdho'pi sāṃghikastaupikādiguru dravyopa- bhoktā dravyalubdha:, tathā saṃsārabhogārthaṃ dravyasañcayakāraka iti
aśrutaśca iti mūrkha : sanmārgopadeśa rahita iti
tathā sacchiṣyāṇāṃ vañcanārthī mithyāvādīti varjanīyaḥ
paramasukhapade naṣṭacitto na sikta iti
abhiṣekaṃ vinā tantradeśaka iti varjanīyaḥ
bhogāsakto bāhyasāṃsārikabhogeṣu ā samantāt prakāreṇa saṃsakta iti
pramatto madya- vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ abhiṣeka- pānena, varjanīyo'samāhita ityarthaḥ

vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ abhiṣeka- iha trikālaṃ bhikṣubhiḥ kāṣāyadhāribhirvajrācāryo vandyate, na gṛhī, na' navaka: saddharma vyākhyānena vinā

iha yadi gṛhī navako'pi vā bhikṣurvajrācāryeṇa tulyo bha[vati, tadā kimasatkarmapādaprakṣālanādikaṃ pañcāṅgavandanāṃ tyaktvā svasthāne gurorāgatasyārthādi- gauravaṃ kartavyam

iha lokāvadhyānaṃ yad gṛhasthacellakānāṃ tatkauśīdyatvenottaraliṅgāgrahaṇāt prātimokṣāśrutaparijñānena
yadi kauśīdyābhimāno nāsti, tadā kimarthaṃ prāga bhikṣusaṃvara jñātvā paścād mahāyānaṃ jñātavyamiti heṣajjādike bhagavato vākyaṃ na kurvanti ? tasmād gṛhasthācāryā bhikṣubhirnārādhanīyā bhikṣau vajradhare sati rājñā punaḥ sarvaprakāreṇa nārā- dhanīyā iti
tathā ācāryaparīkṣāyām- bhikṣayā raktavastreṇa lajjā yasya durātmanaḥ | vandyaḥ pūjyaḥ sa raṇḍānāṃ bauddhānāṃ naṣṭamāgiṇām || ' iti bhavati sambandhaḥ
tathā - raktāmbaraṃ yadā dṛṣṭvā dveṣaṃ gacchanti pāpinaḥ | mlecchadharmaratā bauddhāstathā śvetāmbarapriyāḥ || iti
iha bauddhadarśanaṃ sarvadā na śuklapaṭam

] vajrācāryādisarva karmaprasarasāghanālakṣaṇa mahoddeśaḥ satyapi bhikṣuvajradhare
tasmāt sarvaprakāreṇa parīkṣayitvā guruḥ sevanīyo doṣarahitaḥ, doṣayukto varjanīya ityācārya parīkṣā 'prakathananiyamaḥ || || idānīṃ prajñā' jñānābhiṣekārthaṃ sacchiṣyalakṣaṇamucyate gambhīra ityādinā - gambhīrodāracitto guruniyamaratastyāgaśīlo guṇajño mokṣārthī tantrabhakto'pyacapala hṛdayo labdha tattve'tiguptaḥ | duṣṭānāṃ saṅganaṣṭaḥ sunipuṇaguruṇā grāhyaśiṣyaḥ sa eva prajñāsekādihetorapara iti punarmadhyamaḥ puṇyahetoḥ || || iha mantranaye śiṣyo dvidhā - eko mahāmudrāsiddhisādhanārthī, dvitīyo laukika- siddhisādhanārthī
yo mahāmu [drāsiddhisādhanārthī sa śūnyatāmārgabhāvanārthaṃ sekena saṃgrāhyaḥ 'kalaśaguhyādikena
yo'sau laukikasiddhisādhanārthī sa mantramudrāmaṇḍala- cakrabhāvanārthaṃ saptābhiṣekeṇa saṃgrāhyo madhyamaḥ puṇyahetoriti
adhamo'bhiṣekeṇa
saṃgrāhyo na bhavati, sa upāsaka śikṣayā saṃgrāhya iti niyamaḥ
iha gambhīrodāradharme śūnyatākaruṇātmake cittaṃ yasya sa gambhīrodāracitta iti śiṣyottamaḥ
guruniyama- rataścaturdaśamūlāpattirahitaḥ, daśakuśaladharmarata iti
tyāgaśīla iti sarvasaṅgavivarjito dravyādinirapekṣaka iti
guṇajña iti ratnatraye śrāddhaḥ
mokṣārthīti laukika siddhinira- pekṣaka iti
tantrabhakta iti tantroktasaṃvaraparipālaka iti
acapalahṛdaya iti
laukikamārgehṛdayaṃ na cātyate yasyāsāvacapalahṛdaya iti
labdhatatve'tigupta iti labdhe tattve yāvat svato'nubhavo na bhavati, tāvad gupto'tigupta iti
duṣṭānāṃ saṅganaṣṭa iti
iha dhanārthino ye gṛhasthācāryāḥ, tathā tapasvino'pyekapudgalena maṭhavihāropa- bhoginaste duṣṭāḥ teṣāṃ saṅgī daśākuśalapathaḥ, sa naṣṭo yasyāsau duṣṭānāṃ saṅganaṣṭa iti
itthaṃbhūto mahāśiṣyaḥ sunipuṇaguruṇā tattvavidā prajñājñāna sekāvihetoḥ saṃgrāhyaḥ
ādi- śabdāccaturthābhiṣekahetoḥ sa eva
aparo madhyamaḥ punaḥ puṇyahetoḥ saṃgrāhyo madhyama- guṇairyuktaḥ saptābhiṣekahetoḥ
adhamaḥ punaḥ pañcaśikṣāpadahetoḥ saṃgrāhyo yadi gurvārādhanaṃ karoti, na viheṭhayatīti bhagavato niyamaḥ
iti śiṣyaparīkṣāniyamaḥ ||||

vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ abhiṣeka- idānīṃ vajrācāryasya maṇḍalavartanāyā bhiṣekadānāya tantradeśanārthaṃ yoginīnāṃ pūjākaraṇāya śukla parvaniyamo bhagavatokto vitanyate caitrāta ityādinā- caitrānte śvetaparve parahitaguruṇā maṇḍalaṃ vartayitvā deyāḥ saptābhiṣekāḥ kaluṣamalaharāḥ puṇyahetoḥ sutānām
pūjā ve yoginīnāṃ sakalaguṇanidherdeśanāyā nimittaṃ pūjābhāve'bdamekaṃ nahi bhavati gurordeśanā tantrarāje || 5 ||
ihārya viṣaye śākyamunirbhagavān vaiśākha pūrṇimāyā maruṇodaye'bhisaṃbuddhaḥ
śuklapratipadādipañcadaśakalāvamāne kṛṣṇapratipada praveśe tato dharmacakraṃ pravartayitvā yānatrayadeśanāṃ kṛtvā dvādaśame māse caitra pūrṇimāyāṃ śrīdhānyakaṭake dharmadhātuvāgīśvara- maṇḍalaṃ ṣoḍaśakalāvibhāgalakṣaṇaṃ tadupari śrīmā nnakṣatramaṇḍalaṃ ṣaḍvibhāgikamādi- buddhaṃ maṇḍalaivisphāritami ti
kathaṃ nakṣatramaṇḍala miti jñāyate ?
ucyate - iha caityabāhye yenāṣṭāviṃśannakṣatra viśuddhayā aṣṭāviṃśatstambhāścaturṣu diśāsu saptaśalākā- bhedenā' varopitāḥ - pūrve viṣṇunā'nītā: , dakṣiṇe kārtikeyena, paścime brahmaṇā, uttare śaṅkareṇānītā iti
tena caityabāhye nakṣatravibhaktena caityagarbhamaṇḍalaṃ śrīmā nnakṣatramaṇḍalamiti
evaṃ caturviṃśatikakṣapakṣakalābhedenāparaṃ pātālamaṇḍalaṃ ṣoḍaśa- vibhāgikaṃ kāyavākcittamaṇḍalātmakaṃ ṣoḍaśaśūnyatā karuṇāviśuddhayā
iti maṇḍala- sthānaniyamaḥ
tatra sthāne tasminneva dine buddhābhiṣekaṃ dattvā devāsurādayo buddhatve vyākṛtāstathā- gatenānāgate'dhvani
tenāsyāṃ pūrṇimāyāṃ tathāgataniyamaḥ kālacakre vajrācāryāṇāṃ babhūveti
caitrānte śvetaparve parahitaguruṇā maṇḍalaṃ vartayitvā deyāḥ saptābhiṣekāḥ kaluṣamalaharāḥ puṇyahetoḥ sutānā miti
iha prathamaṃ saptābhiṣekadānapravṛttyarthaṃ mantrajāpamaṇḍala”bhāvanārtham tena puṇyasaṃbhāraḥ, tasmāt puṇyasambhāra hetoḥ saptābhiṣekā deyā iti , ahiṃsādipañcaviṃśadvratāni deyāni tato jñānasambhārārthamuttarābhiṣekatrayaṃ deyam
svamāṃsā dīṣṭataradānādyarthaṃ sarvākāravaropetaśūnyatābhāvanārthaṃ bodhicittā- ga. vartamānāya

ca. nārthaṃ
paṭale, ślo. ] vajrācāryādisarva karmaprasarasāghanālakṣaṇamahoddeśaḥ 'kṣarārthaṃ tena jñānasambhāraḥ, tasmājjñāna-sambhārahetoruttarābhiṣekā deyāḥ, te ca caturthābhiṣekeṇa sahitāḥ
caturtha upadeśena vaktavya iti bhagavato'bhiṣeka' niyamaḥ
evaṃ caitra pūrṇimāṃ* mukhataḥ kṛtvā dvādaśapūrṇimāsu vajrācāryeṇābhiṣekā deyāḥ, pratimādīnāṃ pratiṣṭhā kartavyā, tathānyasminnapi dine śubhanakṣatrayogasahite kartavya iti sekādiniyamaḥ
idānīṃ kālacakra deśanārthaṃ yoginīpūjāniyama ucyate
caitra pūrṇimāyāṃ maṇḍalaṃ vartayitvā yoginīnāṃ khānapānādinā pūjā kartavyā, maṇḍalālekhanābhāve'pi vā sakala- guṇanidhestantrarājasya deśanārtham
a thā'sāmagrīvaśāt pūjā'bhāvo bhavati caitra pūrṇimāyām, tadābdamekaṃ deśanābhāvastantrarāje gurorvajrācāryasyeti
atha pūjā'bhāve jāte sati kaścidyogyaḥ śiṣyaḥ śrutārthī, tadā sa dvādaśapūrṇimāsu yoginīpūjāṃ kṛtvā śṛṇotīti tasya puṇyasambhāreṇānye'pi śṛṇvanti
iha yoginīpūjā bāhyavighnopa- śamanārthaṃ sadāṣṭamyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ yathāvibhavataḥ kartavyā, bhūtādīnāṃ pratyahaṃ balirdātavyaḥ, buddhapūjāmaṇḍalādikaṃ kartavyamiti gṛhasthānāṃ dhanināṃ niyamaḥ
avadhūtaśiṣyāṇāṃ punaḥ pūjāniyamo nāsti, tebhyo deśanāṃ prati gurorapi niyamo nāsti
yasmādā - śayagrāhakā buddhā vajrayoginyaśca na pūjādivastugrāhakā iti tantradeśanā pūjā- niyamaḥ || 5 ||
idānīṃ sekārthaṃ bhūmyādilakṣaṇamucyate- sekārthaṃ bhūparīkṣāṃ vanapura nigame grāmake digvibhāgaṃ jñātvācāryaḥ samastaṃ tvaśubhaśubhaphale śāntikādyaṃ prakuryāt
kuṇḍānāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ vai sakalaśa rajasāṃ homakīlādikānāṃ śiṣyāṇāṃ saṃgrahaṃ yatparamajinapatermaṇḍalā lekhanaṃ ca || 6 ||
iha vṛtte yad bhūmyādikaṃ gṛhītam, vakṣyamāṇe vaktavyam, tat samastaṃ śubhāśubhakarma- phalārtha śāntikādyaṃ kuryāditi vajrācāryaḥ śiṣyāṇāṃ saṃgrahaṃ yat maṇḍalālekhanaṃ ca jñātvā kuryāditi niyamaḥ
parijñānābhāvāt kurvato duṣṭācāryasya narakagamanaṃ bhavati, dravyalobhena paravañcakasyetyācāryānuśāsanaṃ vṛttam
sekārthaṃ bhūparīkṣāmityādi subodham || 6 ||

bho. Gan sMos Pa ( yaduktaṃ )
ga. ghamiti, ca. ghameva
idānīṃ bhūmilakṣaṇamucyate- vimalaprabhāyāṃ bhūmerjātiścaturdhā bhavati guṇavaśācchūdraviḍrājaviprā kṛṣṇā pītā ca raktā śaśadharadhavalā varṇato veditavyā
pratikṣārābjagandhā bhavati vasumatī divyagandhā krameṇa amlā kṣārā ca śūdrī samadhurakaṭuke viḍnṛpe'nyo dvijātiḥ ||7||
bhūmerityādinā
iha lokavyavahāreṇa vastūnāṃ jātiścaturṣā, sā 'caturvarṇato veditavyā śūdrādinā kṛṣṇavarṇādinā iti
atra lokasaṃvṛtyā kṛṣṇavarṇā bhūmiḥ śūdrī, pītā vaiśyā, raktā kṣatriṇī, śvetā brāhmaṇo jātiḥ
tathā gandhataḥ pūtigandhā śūdrī, kṣāragandhā vaiśyā, padmagandhā kṣatriṇī, divyagandhā brāhmaṇī jātiḥ krameṇa
tathā rasataḥ amlakṣārāsvādena śūdro, samadhurakaṭuketi madhurā- svādena viḍjātiḥ kaṭukāsvādena nṛpa iti kṣatriṇī, anyo rasastiktaḥ kaṣāyo dvijātiriti svādato jātiniyamaḥ || 7 ||
idānīṃ śāntyādyarthaṃ bhūmimāha - śvetā śāntī ca puṣṭo bhavati ghananibhā māraṇoccāṭane ca raktākṛṣṭau ca vaśye varakanakanibhā stambhane mohane ca
sarvasmin karmabhāge bhavati hi haritā pañcamī cāntyajātiḥ sarvasvādā ca gandhā sakalaguṇanidhiryoginā veditavyā || 8 ||
śvetetyādi
iha śāntike śvetā bhūmiḥ puṣṭau ca kṛṣṇavarṇā māraṇe uccāṭane ca, raktā ākṛṣṭau vaśye ca, pītā stambhane mohane syāditi
cakārānirvirṣādike'pi yathākrameṇa niyamaḥ
iha sarvasmin karmabhāge haritā bhūmiḥ sarvakarmakarī bhavati
pañcamī cāntya- jātiḥ
sarvasvādā sarvagandhā sakalaguṇa nidhiḥ sākāśadhātulakṣaṇā yoginā veditavyeti bhūmilakṣaṇaniyamaḥ
iha yadīdṛśo bhūmi lakṣaṇaniyamaḥ śāntikādyarthaṃ tadā na sarvatra vanapurādidigvibhāgeṣvebhirlakṣaṇairyuktā bhūmirbhavati
tena mūlatantre bhagavatoktam - iha yatra maṇḍalādikarma kartavyaṃ tatra yadi karmānurūpato bhūmirna bhavati, tadā khāni khanitvā udakāntaṃ śilāntaṃ vā khanet, tato'paramṛttikayā khāni pūrayet karmānurūpataḥ
atra 'bhūgandhārthaṃ śvetamṛttikāṃ candanodakena bhāvayet, raktamṛttikāṃ padmodakena raktacanda nodakena bhāvayet, pītamṛttikāṃ kharāśvamanuṣya mūtreṇa bhāvayet
kṛṣṇamṛttikāṃ pūtimāṃsa- dvijajāti
vajrācāryādisarva karmaprasarasāghanālakṣaṇamahoddeśaḥ toyena bhāvayet, rasāsvādanārthaṃ kṛṣṇāyāṃ lavaṇāmbu' kṣepayet, pītāyāṃ guḍam, raktāyāṃ trikaṭukam, śvetāyāṃ tiktaṃ kaṣāyaṃ ceti haritāyāṃ sarvakarmakaratvānna kiñcit kṣepaṇīyam
tathā bhūmigarbhe nidhāpanīyam
kṛṣṇāyāṃ mānuṣāsthi māraṇe, kākapicchānyu- ccāṭane, vidveṣe kharāsthīni pītāyāṃ kīlane meṣaśṛṅgam, stambhane haritālam, mohane sarpam, raktāyāṃ vaśye gorocanam, ākṛṣṭau hiṅgulam, stobhe manaḥśilām, śvetāyāṃ śāntau sphaṭikam, puṣṭau śaṅkham, jvarāpaharaṇe dāhe muktā iti
haritāyāṃ na kiñcidapi kṣepaṇīyamiti
atha haritā sādhāraṇaṃ sārvakāma ke kāryam, tadā pañcaratnāni bhūmigarbhe nidhāpayet
śvetaraktāyāmapi sarvakarmaṇi krūradravyāṇi varjayet
sarvakarmaśabdena śānti- kādivaśyādiṣaṭkarmāṇi na māraṇādikīlanādi ṣaḍiti bhūmiparīkṣākriyāniyamaḥ || 8 ||
idānīṃ śāntikādyarthaṃ digvibhāga ucyate- aiśānyāṃ cottare vai bhavati bhuktile śāntikaṃ pauṣṭikaṃ ca āgneyyāṃ pūrvabhāge prakaṭitaniyataṃ māraṇoccāṭanaṃ ca
naiṛtyāṃ dakṣiṇe ca sphuṭamapi satataṃ vaśyamākarṣaṇaṃ ca vāyavyāṃ paścime vai paramanarapate stambhanaṃ mohanaṃ ca || 9 ||
aiśānyāmityādinā
iha sāmānyagrāme grāmabāhye aiśānyāṃ śāntikaṃ pauṣṭikaṃ jvarāpaharaṇaṃ vā kuryāduttare'pi vā
mahārājadhānyāṃ punaraṣṭadikṣu śavadahanādi - śmaśānāṣṭakam tena śāntikaṃ pauṣṭikaṃ na sidhyati
tena rājagṛhasya aiśānyā - muttareṇa vā śāntikaṃ pauṣṭikaṃ kartavyam
śeṣakarmāṇi grāmavad bāhye rājadhānyāmiti śāntipuṣṭikarmaniyamaḥ
tathāgneyyāṃ māraṇam, pūrve vidveṣoccāṭana, nairṛtyāṃ vaśyam, dakṣiṇe ākṛṣṭiḥ stobhanam vāyavyāṃ mohanam paścime stambhanaṃ kolanaṃ ceti
grāmamadhye sārvakarmika-maṇḍalaṃ kuṇḍaṃ homaṃ ca kuryānmantrīti digbhāge karmakaraṇa- niyamaḥ || 9 ||
idānīṃ śāntyādikuṇḍamucyate- kuṇḍaṃ grāmāṣṭadikṣu prabhavati niyataṃ vartulaṃ cābdhikoṇaṃ arddhenduṃ pañcakoṇaṃ prakṛtiguṇavaśāt saptakoṇaṃ trikoṇam
vimalaprabhāyāṃ ṣaṭkoṇaṃ cāṣṭakoṇaṃ bhavati kulavaśāt garbhacihnaṃ ca teṣāṃ padmaṃ cakraṃ ca karttī tvasiriṣuriti vajrāṅkuśaḥ śṛṅkhalā'hiḥ || 10 ||
kuṇḍaṃ grāmāṣṭadikṣu aiśānyādiṣu yathākrameṇa śāntau vartulaṃ kuṇḍam, puṣṭo caturasram, māraṇe dhanurākāram vidveṣe pañcakoṇam, vaśye saptakoṇam', ākṛṣṭī trikoṇam, mohane ṣaṭkoṇam, stambhane cāṣṭakoṇamiti
eṣāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ ca vakṣyamāṇe homavidhau vistareṇa vaktavyamiti
idānīṃ kuṇḍacihnamucyate - iha vṛttakuṇḍa 'kamalakaṇikāyāṃ cihnaṃ padmam, caturasre cakram dhanurākāre kartī, pañcakoṇe khaṅgaḥ, saptakoṇe bāṇaḥ, trikoṇe vajrāṅkuśaḥ, ṣaṭkoṇe mohane sarpaḥ, aṣṭakoṇe śṛṅkhaleti
atra kuṇḍadvaye cihnaviparyā- sarachandovaśāditi cihna niyamaḥ || 10 ||
eṣāṃ pramāṇamāha- ekadvayarddhaka hastaṃ khayugakhanayanaṃ khāgni khatvaṃṅgulaṃ syā- dardhāṅgā ṣaḍvibhāgādvaruṇaravivibhāgena khāniśca vedī
oṣṭhāścihnāvalī syādupari kulavaśādvedikāyāḥ samantā- dvedī bāhye'bjapatrāṇyapi kuru nṛpate śāntipuṣṭayorna cānye || 11 ||
eketyādi
ekahastaṃ vṛttam, dvihastaṃ caturasram, arddhahastaṃ dhanurākāram, vṛttapramāṇaṃ pañcakoṇam, catvāriṃśadaṅgulaṃ saptakoṇam
viśatyaṅgulaṃ trikoṇam, ṣaṭkoṇaṃ triśadaṅgulam, ṣaṣṭacaṅgulamaṣṭakoṇam, kuṇḍārddhamānā khāniḥ kuṇḍe ṣaḍvibhāgikā vediḥ kuṇḍadvādaśa- bhāgikamoṣṭham, cihnāvalī ca vedyāḥ samam, tadvāhye'bjapatrāṇi śāntipuṣṭayoḥ, śeṣaka' maṃṇi vedabāhye'nyadanyadvakṣyate
vajraṃ vā cihnaṃ sarvakarmaṇi kuṇḍamadhye jñātavyam || 11 ||
vajraṃ vā sarvakarmasvapi bhavati maho, kīlakaṃ cāṣṭabhede - nyagrodhāśvatthakāsthīnyayasakhadirajaṃ cūta dilvā kaṃjaṃ ca
trikoṇa ākṛṣṭau saptakoṇam
trikoṇe bāṇa:, saptakoṇe vajrāṅkuśaḥ
paṭale, ślo
] vajrācāryādisarva karmaprasarasādhanālakṣaṇamahoddeśaḥ evaṃ sphāṭikyakumbhā vararajata mayāḥ śrīkapālāyasāśva tāmrākhyā hemakumbhāḥ prakaṭitaniyatā dārujā mṛṇmayāśca || 12 ||
iha śāntike nyagrodha kīlakāḥ, darśādikkīlanārthaṃ puṣṭau aśvatthāḥ, māraṇe'- sthimayāḥ, uccāṭane āyasāḥ, vaśye 'khadirajāḥ, ākṛṣṭau cūtajāḥ, mohane'rkajāḥ, stambhane vilvajā iti kīlaka niyamaḥ, sārvakarmike udumbarajāḥ
idānīṃ kalaśā ucyante - śāntike sphāṭikakalaśāḥ, puṣṭau raupyā, māraṇe 'mānuṣakapālāḥ, uccāṭane vidveṣe āyasāḥ, vaśye sauvarṇāḥ, ākṛṣṭau tāmrāḥ, stambhane mṛṇmayāḥ, mohane dārujā daśakalaśā iti || 12 ||
atha śāntipuṣṭyarthaṃ ghaṭalakṣaṇamucyate- vṛttā dvaṣṭāṅguloktā dviguṇitadaśakenocchritā dvyaṅguloṣṭhāḥ ṣaḍgrīvāṣṭāṅgulāsyāḥ śaśadharadhavalā : śāntipuṣṭayorna cānye
pūrvāhlādaṣṭayāmā: pratidinasamaye śāntipuṣṭyādike syu- revaṃ tatrārddhayāmedina niśisamaye cāṣṭakarma prakuryāt || 13 || || ||
iha kalaśagarbha vṛttena 'dvaṣṭāṅgulā uktāḥ ṣoḍaśāṅgulā uktāḥ
dviguṇi- tadaśakenocchritā ityadhaḥ kalaśagarbhāt mukhauṣṭhāntā viśatyaṅgulā ucchrayeṇeti
dvagu- loṣṭhā iti dvyaṅgulāvoṣṭau lambamānau yeṣāṃ te dvayaṅguloṣṭhā mukhādavadheḥ ṣaḍaṅgulagrīvā: aṣṭāṅgulāsyāḥ
oṣṭhāntādoṣṭhāntamukhamaṣṭāṅgulam, aṣṭhāṅgulaṃ bhāgatrayaṃ kṛtvā bhāgadvayena kaṇṭharandhraṃ bhāgaikenauṣṭhadvayāyāmaḥ te ca śaśadharadhavalā : śāntipuṣṭayorna cānye ghaṭāḥ syuriti
sphāṭi ka' raupya kalaśānāmabhāve mṛṇmayā akālamūlakalaśāḥ śāli- piṣṭena candanena vā lipya dhavalāḥ kartavyāḥ
tathā mūlatantre'pi bhagavatoktam - māraṇe narakapālāni śmaśānāṅgāracūrṇena naravasayā lipya kṛṣṇāni kārayed iti
uccāṭane vidveṣe dīrghagrīvā aṣṭāṅgulavaktrāḥ kṛṣṇāṅgā vṛttena dvādaśāṅgulāḥ, ucchrayeṇa catu- viśatyaṅgulāḥ, aṅgulaikoṣṭhāḥ ṣaḍaṅgalāsyā iti, vaśyākarṣaṇe yathā śilpinā ghaṭitā lokavyavahāreṇeti, stambhane kharvā vṛttocchrayeṇa tulyāḥ ṣoḍaśāṅgulāścaturaṅgulagrīvāḥ ( vibhede ca )
paṭale, ślo
] vajrācāryādisarva karmaprasarasādhanālakṣaṇ mahoddeśaḥ dūrvā śasyaṃ ca māṃsaṃ saviṣamapi tathā rājikā raktapuṣpaṃ vilvaṃ nirmālyamālāsukanakakusumānyeva pañcādikeṣu || 15 ||
mohane unmattajāḥ stambhane vilvajā iti, arddhahastāḥ sarve dvādaśāṅgalāḥ
kaniṣṭhāṅgulīpramāṇenāṅguṣṭhaṃ yāvat
tadupari ūnādhikā na grāhyā iti samidhaniyamaḥ
idānīṃ homadravyāṇyucyante - kṣīretyādi
iha śāntike gokṣīreṇa durvayā homa:,
puṣṭī ghṛtena pañcaśasyaiḥ, māraṇe raktena māṃsaviṣābhyāṃ saha uccāṭane vidveṣe narabasayā ṣaḍaṅgulāsyāḥ sthūloṣṭhā aṅguṣṭha' dvayeneti kalaśaniyamaḥ
sarvakarmaṇi śāntyādivaśyādi- karmaṇyuktā grāhyā iti niyamaḥ
idānīṃ śāntikādivelocyate - pūrvāhṇetyādinā
iha pratidinaṃ pūrvāhnā- daṣṭayāmāḥ praharāḥ, teṣu pūrvāhna prahare caturthe uccāṭanam pañcame vaśyam, kuryāditi śāntikādipraharāḥ syuḥ
evaṃ tatrārddhayāmairiti pūrvāhlādarddhaprahare śāntikam
aparārddhe pauṣṭikam, evaṃ sarvatrāpi
tathā rātrau pūrvārddha prahare śāntikam, aparārddhe pauṣṭikamapi karmāṇi kuryānmantrīti
yadi vakṣyamāṇāsanaṃ baddhvā praharamekaṃ homaṃ kartuṃ na śaknoti, tadārddhapraharamekaṃ homaṃ kṛtvā sādhayedityubhayakathanam
aparaḥ kālaviśeṣeṇa devī pūjā sādhana niyamaḥ pañcamapaṭale vakṣyamāṇe vaktavya iti || 13 ||
śāntikam, dvitīye pauṣṭikam, tṛtīye māraṇam, ṣaṣṭhe ākarṣaṇam, saptame mohanam, aṣṭame stambhanaṃ varjanīyaṃ idānīṃ śāntipuṣṭyoḥ krūravela lagnapratiṣedha ucyate- madhyāhnaṃ cārddharātraṃ dinaniśisamaye śāntike lagnaṃ krūragrahasthaṃ maraṇabhayakaraṃ tadvadevaṃ prasiddham
sakṣīrāḥ śāntipuṣṭyoḥ śaraśatasamidho māraṇe mānuṣāsthi- vidveṣe kākapicchāyari ca khadirajāḥ kiṃśukā kṛṣṭivaye || 14 |
iha pratidine madhyāhnaṃ cārddharātraṃ ca śāntike puṣṭikārye'pi varjanīyam
tathoditalagnaṃ krūragrahasthaṃ maṅgala śanikālāgnigrahasahitaṃ varjitam, yato maraṇabhayakaram
atha māraṇoccāṭane tu yojanīyaṃ tadeveti niyamaḥ
idānīṃ śāntikādiṣu homasamidha ucyante - sakṣīretyādinā
iha śāntī puṣṭī sakṣīrā samidhaḥ śaraśateti pañcaśatasaṃkhyāḥ, kṣīravṛkṣāṇāmiti udumbarāśvatthanyagrodha- parkaṭīmadhuvṛkṣāṇāmiti
māraṇe mānuṣāsthoni ghaṭitāni kaniṣṭhāṅgulīsaṃsthānānīti pañcaśatāni tathā vidveṣoccāṭane kākapicchāni pañcaśatāni evaṃ vaśye khabirajāḥ, ākṛṣṭau palāśajāḥ || 14||
vilvonmattārdhahastāḥ śaraśatagaṇanā stambhane mohane ca kṣīrājyāsṛgvasāsvedakuliśasalila śleṣma madyādihome
rājikālavaṇābhyāṃ saha vaśye svedena raktakaravīrādipuṣpaiḥ saha ākṛṣṭau mūtreṇa vilva- patreṇa tasya phalaśasyena vā saha, stambhane śleṣmaṇā nirmālyamālayā saha, mohane madyena dhuttūrakapuṣpe: saha, iti śāntikādyeṣu homadravyaniyamaḥ || 15 ||
yāmye nairṛtyakoṇe savaruṇapavane ācāryasyāsanaṃ ve bhavati narapate raṅgaṃ karmadvaye syādapi vibhakamale yakṣa rudrendravahnī śāntikarmādike ca
śveta kṛṣṇā kaṃpītaṃ śvetakṛṣṇākaṃpītaṃ bāhye buddhaprabhedaiḥ surayamavaruṇeṣūttare raṅgabhūmiḥ || 16 ||
iha śāntau yāmye āsanaṃ kartavyam, vakṣyamāṇam, puṣṭau nairṛtyakoṇe, māraṇe varuṇe, uccāṭane vidveṣe vāyavye, vaśye yakṣe, ākṛṣṭāvīśāne, mohane pūrve, stambhane agnikoṇe iti homakuṇḍāsananiyamaḥ śāntikarmādike
idānīṃ rajovidhirucyate - iha śāntikādī karmadvaye kuṇḍe vā maṇḍale vā madhye rajaḥ pāto bhavati
śāntipuṣṭyoḥ śvetaṃ rajaḥ, māraṇoccāṭanayoḥ kṛṣṇaṃ rajaḥ, vaśyākṛṣṭau raktam, mohanastambhanayoḥ pītam, sarvakarmaṇi haritaṃ śvetaḥ kṛṣṇo rakto vā pīto vā haritasahita iti
evaṃ kuṇḍe vā maṇḍale vā bāhye pūrve dakṣiṇe paścime uttare buddhabhedena bhūmyāṃ rajaḥpātastantroktavidhinā bhagavato vā vaktra' varṇabhedeneti raṅga pātaniyamaḥ || 16 ||
kuṇḍe vā raṅgabhūmirbhavati kulavaśād raṅgapātaśca bhūmi- nyāsādyaṃ prokṣaṇādyaṃ svahṛdayakuliśotsarjanaṃ devatānām
tathā vakṣyamāṇakrameṇa nyāsādyaṃ prokṣaṇādyaṃ svahṛdayakuliśenotsarjanaṃ devatā- nāmācāryeṇa kartavyamiti niyamaḥ
idānīmarghapātralakṣaṇamucyate- sphaṭikyādyaghaṃpātre vasudalakamalaṃ dvādaśāṅguṣṭhakaṃ ca daṇḍāgre'ñjatyapātraṃ bhavati ca culukaṃ cāhutī homakārye || 17||
iha śāntau sphaṭikapātramarghadānārthaṃ śarāvākṛtiḥ, 'kacolākṛtītyarthaḥ
sarvaṃ dvādaśāṅgulaṃ sakaṇikāṣṭadalātmakam
evaṃ sphaṭikādyarghapātre vasudalakamalaṃ dvādaśāṅguṣṭhakaṃ ceti
atra caturaṅgulā kaṇikā, caturaṅgulānyaṣṭadalāni evaṃ dvādaśāṅgulam
tathā puṣṭyādike raupyakapālāyasa suvarṇatā mradārumṛṇmayapātreṣu vidhiriti
tathā āhutī homārthaṃ pātrī dhruvakamucyate
iha daṇḍāgre hastamātradaṇḍasyāgre añjalyākārapātraṃ caturasraṃ cāṅgulaikocchritam, samatale padmaṃ padmapatrāgramayam oṣṭhābhyantarapāśvaṃ bāhye pañcaśūka- vajram, madhyaśuke cchidraṃ ghṛtadhārāyā nirgamārthaṃ mūtraśukradhārānirgamavat
āhutī kārye āhutī pātrīti hastamātradaṇḍāditi mūlatatre
hṛdayakamalādvajjraparyantamiti niyamaḥ
ta[thā daṇḍāgre homakāyeṃ culukaṃ bhavati, ūrṇāsthānād hṛdayaṃ yāvat, mukhato vā tiryaga vibhāga iti tatraiva ṣaḍaṅa gulaṃ padmaṃ karatala māneneti dravyahoma'śravāyāṃ daṇḍa-pṛṣṭhe vajracihnamiti homapātra niyamaḥ || 17 ||
idānīṃ śāntyādau devatāmūrtirucyate- śāntaḥ krūraḥ sarāgo bhavati kulavaśād devatā stabdhamūrtiḥ evaṃ karmadvaye syāt prakaṭitaniyato maṇḍale cādhidevaḥ
pañcākāro jinendrastrividhabhavagataḥ skandhadhātvādibhedaiḥ pañcākāraṃ hi tasmādapi bhavati rajomaṇḍale devatānām ||18||
iha kuṇḍe agnidevatāmaṇḍale nāyakaśca śāntipuṣṭau śāntaḥ śuklavarṇo bhavati, māraṇoccāṭanādye krūraḥ kṛṣṇavarṇaḥ, vaśyākṛṣṭau sarāgo raktavarṇaḥ, mohanastambhanādye stabdhaḥ pītavarṇa iti devatāvarṇaḥ śāntikā do
sarvakarmaṇi harita itīṣaddhasitarāga- mūrtiriti devatāniyamaḥ
idānīṃ rajoviśuddhirucyate - pañcākāra ityādinā
iha jinendro vajrasattvaḥ pañcākāro hi yasmāt pañcākārajñānaraśmispharaṇāt  tribhavagataḥ skandhadhātvāyatana- nirāvaraṇabhedena, tasmādapi pañcākāraṃ rajomaṇḍale devatānāṃ bhavatīti rajoniyamaḥ || 18 ||
vajrācāryādisarva karmaprasarasādhanālakṣaṇa mahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ sūtralakṣaṇamucyate— sūtraṃ hastāṣṭakaṃ syād bhavati karayavaikena vṛttaṃ trivṛttaṃ ācāryāṅguṣṭhakena trividhapathagataṃ sūtramekaṃ na cānyat
paryaṅkaḥ śāntikādī kramapariracitaṃ vajradetyotkaṭaṃ ca paryaṅkādhaṃ dvibhedaṃ gudagatacaraṇaṃ cāsanaṃ karmabhedaiḥ || 19 ||
sūtraṃ hastāṣṭakamityādinā
iha kanyākartitasūtre triguṇātmakairanekaiḥ sūtraṃ vartayitvā tatastrivṛttaṃ kārayed vajrācāryāṅguṣṭhayavapramāṇena
tadevāṣṭahastamiti maṇḍalasya dviguṇaṃ caturguṇaṃ vā ṣoḍaśahastaṃ yāvat, ācāryahastena sūtramekaṃ kartavyam
na cānyad lakṣaṇaṃ maṇḍale
maṇḍalaṃ sadā svātmavibhāgena ekahasta [mārabhya yāvat sahasrahastaṃ tāvadbhavati, tena sūtraniyamo maṇḍalaniyamaścācāryahastena yatra tatra dviguṇaṃ sūtraṃ maṇḍalāditi
tatrādibuddhe cittamaṇḍalaṃ dvādaśahastaṃ prakuryāditi niyamāccaturviṃśati- hastaṃ sūtram
evaṃ vāṅmaṇḍalaṃ ṣoḍaśahastam, kāyamaṇḍalaṃ viṃśatihastamiti niyama: sūtradviguṇatāyāḥ
na punarhastasahasramaṇḍale dvihastasahasrasūtreṇa sūtrapāto'bhidheyo'- tyadbhutatvāditi
nanu tantrāntare – “dvāviṃśatibhāgikaṃ sūtraṃ vṛttena dīrgheṇa maṇḍalād dviguṇaṃ katham ?
vṛttena yavamātraṃ dīrghatvenāṣṭahastakam iti kasyacidvacanaṃ bhaviṣyati ?
tasmā- ducyate - iha yadi sarvasmin maṇḍale 'dvāviṃśatibhāgena vṛttaṃ sūtram, tadā sahasrahasta- maṇḍale cakrābhāgikaṃ dvāraṃ pañcaviṃśatyadhikaśatahastaṃ bhaviṣyati
tasya viṃśatibhāgena ṣaḍaṅgulādhikaṣaḍhastaṃ vṛttena sūtraṃ bhavati
tena sūtreṇa dvisahasrahastena ko'sāvācāryaḥ sūtrapātaṃ kariṣyati maṇḍalabhūmyām
tasmādidaṃ vacanaṃ sāmānyamaṇḍale hastamātrādau,
na tadūrdhvaṃ sūtravṛtta 'niyamo bhagavata iti
sāmānyena kāyo narāṇāṃ caturhastaḥ
tena kāyād dviguṇamaṣṭahastam, trividhapathagataṃ kāyavākcittanāḍīgatamekalolībhūtam tena sahasrahastaparyantaṃ sūtrayenmaṇḍalaṃ garbhacakārebhyaḥ punaḥ punaḥ sūtrasthāne sūtrapāteneti sūtravidhiniyamaḥ
idānīṃ 'śāntyādikarmasādhanārthamā sanānyucyante - paryaṅka ityādinā
iha śāntikādo krameṇāsanāni bhavanti
tatra śāntī paryaṅka iti
vāmajānūpari dakṣiṇa- pādo gata uttānaka iti paryaṅkaḥ

daityamiti daityānaṃ māraṇe, aṅgakāra' karmapādavaditi daityāsanam
utkaṭaṃ ceti uccāṭane vidveṣe ca utkaṭaṃ bhavati
bhūmyāṃ dvau pādau samau gulphau phiccakamūlalagnau ūrdhvaṃ gataṃ jānudvayamūrudvayaṃ cetyutkaṭam

ākṛṣṭau dvitīyo bhedo dakṣiṇaḥ paryaṅkavad vāma utkaṭavat kiñcidvāme namra iti paryaṅgādhaṃ dvibhedam
gudagatacaraṇamiti

|| idānīṃ mantrajāpārthaṃ 'śāntyādīnyakṣasūtrāpyucyante - sphāṭikyaimauktikairvā narakharadaśane va'sthibhiḥ putrajīvaiḥ padmākhyeśākṣariṣṭa sugatakulavaśānmantrajāpe'kṣasūtram

|| sphāṭikyairityādinā
iha śāntī 'sphāṭikyamakṣasūtram, puṣṭau muktāphalam, māraṇe naradantakṛtaṃ, 'uccāṭane kharadantakṛtaṃ vā asthibhiḥ kṛtam, vaśye putrajīvākṣa- sūtram
ākṛṣṭau padmākṣasūtram, atha raktacandana bījeḥ kṛtam

sugatakulavaśāditi - vakṣyamāṇe vaktavyam

iha śāntī puṣṭau ca sugandhapuṣpaiḥ śvetairdevatādīnāmarcanam, māraṇe uccāṭane vidveṣe ca sakaṭukai kṛṣṇaiḥ sakaṇṭakairiti
vaśye ākṛṣṭau raktaiḥ, mohane stambhane potairiti puṣpārcananiyamaḥ
idānīṃ dhūpā ucyante - śīta ityādinā
śāntipuṣṭayoḥ śīto dhūpaḥ śītāguru- sikakarpūraidhūpo madhuśarkarāsahitaḥ śīto dhūpa ityucyate śītadravyaiḥ
tathā māraṇe uccāṭane vi māṃsa dhūpo madhurudhirayuta iti

evaṃ mohane stambhane ca kaṣāyadhūpaḥ
harītakīcūrṇaṃ guḍasahitaṃ kapāyadhūpaḥ
iti dhūpaniyamaḥ
evaṃ naivedyaṃ śāntī dugdhabhaktam, puṣṭau dadhibhaktam, māraṇe kālajam, uccāṭane māṃsam, vaśye ghṛtabhaktam, ākṛṣṭau tilatailabhaktam, mohane vyāmiśramudgaudanam
stambhane caṇakaṃ ca matsyodanamiti
evaṃ śāntipuṣṭayoścandanaṃ vilepanam, māraṇoccāṭane raktāṅgāreṇa ca vaśyākṛṣṭau kuṅkumagorocanena, raktacandanena vā
mohane stambhane haridrāharitāleneti gandhaniyamaḥ
tathā pradīpaḥ
śāntipuṣṭyoghṛtena pradīpaḥ māraṇoccāṭanayornaravasayā, vaśyā- kṛṣṭau tilatailena mohane stambhane jyo tiṣmatītailena bhallātakatailenetyādineti dīpa niyamaḥ
tathā śāntau puṣṭau akṣaitaṃ śālitaṇḍulāḥ, māraṇoccāṭane rājikāḥ, vaśyākṛṣṭau sarṣapāḥ, stambhane mohane masūrikāḥ, aparavrīhirvā iti
tathā vastrādīni śāntipuṣṭyoḥ sarvāṇi pūjādravyāṇi śvetāni, māraṇoccāṭanayoḥ kṛṣṇāni vaśyākṛṣṭau raktāni, stambhanamohanayoḥ pītāni, sarvakarmaṇi sarvāṇi haritānīti pūjāvidhiniyamaḥ || 20 ||
yantraṃ nyagrodhapatre citimṛtakapaṭe bahupatre'rkapatre śrīkhaṇḍe : śālipiṣṭaiścitibhuvanagatāṅgāra raktairviṣeṇa
kāśmīraiḥ śītapuṣpaistriphala rasakuśātālakerlekhanaṃ syād dūrvāśītā sthipiṣṭaiḥ kanakakhadirajā lekhanī vilvajārkā ||21||
'śālipiṣṭo- dakena dūrvāṅkareṇa śītalekhanyā puṣṭau, tathā māraṇoccāṭane ca śmaśānakapaṭe viṣa- ruṣi rāṅgāreṇa narāsthilekhanyā, kākapicchalekhanyoccāṭane, vaśyākṛṣṭau bhūrjapatre kuṅkumagorocanena suvarṇalekhanyā, khadiralekhanyākṛṣṭau raktacandanena piṣṭeneti, stambhane mohane arkapatre paripakve pītavarṇa triphalā rasasahitena tālakena haridrayā ca vilva- lekhanyā mohane ceti niyamaḥ
vimalaprabhāyāṃ idānīṃ yantrapratiṣṭhāpanavidhirucyate - iha śāntipuṣṭyo yantralikhitaṃ ' patrādikaṃ śvetasūtreṇa veṣṭayet yāvat patraṃ na dṛśyate
evaṃ karmavibhāgena kṛṣṇasūtreṇa raktasūtreṇa potasūtreṇa veṣṭayet || 21 ||
mṛnmande śrīkapāle trimadhuni rudhire sikthaveṣṭeṣṭamadhye sārdrasthāne pavitre citibhuvanatale cāgnitāpe dharaṇyām
yantrasyāropaṇaṃ syādaśubhaśubhavaśānmantriṇā veditavyaṃ candrabhe preta uṣṭre mṛgaturagapaśau kamaṃdehe krameṇa ||22||
tataḥ śāntipuṣṭyoḥ śarāvasaṃpuṭe sthāpayet trimadhuni madhughṛtadugdhasaṃmiśra, māraṇo- ccāṭane kapāle rudhirapūrṇe sthāpayet, agnitāpe kharparasaṃpuṭe sikthake' veṣṭayitvā vaśyākṛṣṭau stambhane mohane haritālodakapūrṇe iṣṭakamadhye sthāpayet
sārdrasthāne pavitre 'śāntipuṣṭayoḥ sthāpayed maṇḍalopari, māraṇoccāṭane mṛtadagdhakhānikātale nidhāpayet, vaśyākarṣaṇe 'cullītale, stambhane mohane śuṣka bhūmyāṃ nidhāpayediti mantriṇā'śubhaśubhakarmaṇi phalaṃ veditavyamiti niyamaḥ
idānīṃ likhitasya yantrasya cakrabāhye niyama ucyate - iha śāntau cakraṃ bāhye candramaṇḍalena veṣṭayet, uddhṛtya rekhayā puṣṭau 'hastinā veṣṭayet, hastidehe yathā cakraṃ bhavati, māraṇe pretadehe, uccāṭane uṣṭradehe, vaśye mṛgadehe, ākṛṣṭau turagadehe, mohane chāgaladehe meṣadehe vā, stambhane karmadehe yathā yantracakraṃ bhavati tathā bāhye rūpaṃ kartavyam
tataḥ pūrvoktasūtreṇa veṣṭayediti
evaṃ vidyāyāmapi niyamaḥ
śānti puṣṭyoḥ raupyanalikā vidyāyā madhye sthāpyā, māraṇe mānuṣāsthinalikā, uccāṭane kākāsthinalikā, vaśye suvarṇanalikā, ākṛṣṭau tāmranalikā, mohane lohanalikā, stambhane rītikānalikā iti niyamaḥ
iha yenaivoccāṭanaṃ tenaiva vidhinā vidveṣakāryaṃ kartavyam, yenaiva stambhanaṃ tenaiva kīlanaṃ kartavyam, kintu kīlane śatrūdvartanena pañcāmṛtasahitena pratikṛti kṛtvā madanakaṇṭakaiḥ

pṛthvītoyodbhūtāḥ kāyadhāturakṣakāḥ, agnivāyudhātudbhūtā vāgdhāturakṣakāḥ, śūnyajñānadhātūdbhūtāḥ sarvatra cittadhātu rakṣakāḥ

triprākārāṃstrivamahivalayagatān dūradṛṣṭyāvasāne prātaśchāyāvasāne kṣititalanilayādambare vajrakūṭam
madhye'bjaṃ sūrya hastaṃ bhavati yugakarā karṇikā sāsanā ca ātmānaṃ tasya mūrdhni vyapagatakaluṣaṃ yoginā bhāvanīyam ||24||
idānīmeṣāṃ krodhādīnāṃ pratyekaṃ balimantrapadāni bhavanti
tadyathā-oṃ āḥ hū~ ho : uṣṇīṣa sumbhani sumbhavighnāntaka nīladaṇḍa prajñāntakaṭakkirājapadmāntakaacalayamāntaka- mahābalebhyaḥ saparivārebhya idaṃ baliṃ gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ dīpam akṣataṃ dadāmahe, te cāgatya saparivārāḥ śīghramidaṃ baliṃ gṛhṇantu khādantu pibantu ja: hū~ vaṃ ho: saṃtṛptāḥ sarvasattvānāṃ śānti puṣṭi rakṣāvaraṇagupti kurvantu huṃ hū~ phaṭ vajradhara ājñāpayati svāhā iti sarvakrodhabalimantraḥ
tathā ākāśādikṛtsnasiddhānāṃ balimantrapadāni — oṃ āḥ hū~ ho : ākāśavāyuteja udakapṛthvī sādhitebhyaḥ saparivārebhyaḥ idaṃ baliṃ gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ dīpam akṣataṃ dadāmahe
teṃ cāgatya saparivārāḥ śīghramidaṃ baliṃ gṛhṇantu khādantu pibantu jaḥ hū~ vaṃ ho: saṃtṛptāḥ sarvasattvānāṃ śānti puṣṭi rakṣāvaraṇaguptiṃ kurvantu huṃ hūṃ phaṭ vajradhara ājñāpayati svāhā
tato dikpālānāṃ balimantrapadāni - oṃ ā : hū~ ho : brahmaviṣṇu- nairṛtyavāyuyamāgnisamudreśvarendrayakṣebhyaḥ saparivārebhya idaṃ baliṃ gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ dīpam akṣataṃ dadāmahe, te cāgatya saparivārāḥ śīghramidaṃ baliṃ gṛhṇantu khādantu pibantu ja: hū~ vaṃ hoḥ saṃtṛptāḥ sarvasattvānāṃ śānti puṣṭi rakṣāvaraṇa guptiṃ kurvantu huṃ hū~ phaṭ vajradhara ājñāpayati svāhā
tato graha balimantrapadāni – oṃ āḥ hū~ ho rāhukālāgnicandrasūrya budhamaṅgalaśukra bṛhaspatiketuśanibhyaḥ saparivārebhya idaṃ baliṃ gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ dīpam akṣataṃ dadāmahe, te cāgatya saparivārāḥ śīghramidaṃ baliṃ gṛhṇantu khādantu pibantu jaḥ hū~ va~ ho: saṃtṛptāḥ sarva- sattvānāṃ śānti puṣṭi rakṣāvaraṇa guptiṃ kurvantu hu~ hū~ phaṭ vajradhara ājñāpayati svāhā
tato nāgabalimantrapadāni - oṃ āḥ hū~ ho: jayavijayapadmakarkoṭakavāsukiśaṅkhapālakuli- kānantatakṣakamahāpadmebhyaḥ saparivārebhya idaṃ baliṃ gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ dīpam akṣataṃ dadāmahe, te cāgatya saparivārāḥ śīghramidaṃ baliṃ gṛhṇantu khādantu pibantu ja: hū~ va~ ho: saṃtṛptāḥ sarva- sattvānāṃ śānti puṣṭi rakṣāvaraṇaguptiṃ kurvantu hu~ hū~ phaṭ vajradhara ājñāpayati svāhā
tato bhūtabalimantrapadāni - oṃ āḥ haiṃ hoḥ vetāḍavikṛta mukhaśva kākaśūkaragṛdhravyāghrolūka- jambūkagaruḍamukhebhyaḥ saparivārebhya idaṃ baliṃ gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ dīpam akṣataṃ dadāmahe, te cāgatya saparivārāḥ śīghramidaṃ gṛhṇantu khādantu pibantu ja: hū~ va~ ho: saṃtṛptāḥ sarvasattvānāṃ śānti puṣṭi rakṣāvaraṇaguptiṃ kurvantu hu~ hū~ phaṭ vajradhara ājñāpayati svāhā
iti bhūtabalimantraḥ
evaṃ pratyekabali vakṣyamāṇakrameṇa śodhayitvā 'bodhayitvā pradīpayitvā' amṛtī- kṛtvā(tya) krodhādī nāṃ mantrī dadāti
evaṃ sarvatra maṇḍalavidhau catuḥ sandhyābali dattvā ācāryastato visarjanaṃ karoti
visarjanakāle guhyacakre uhā~kāre krodhacakraṃ praveśayet
uṣṇīṣe aṃkāre śūnyakṛtsnādicakram, lalāṭe 'ikāre dikpālacakram, kaṇṭhe ṛkāre grahacakram hṛdaye 'ukāre nāgacakram, nābhau ṛkāre bhūtacakraṃ praveśayediti visarjanavidhi nirvartya tato bhūmiparigrahaṃ karoti mantrī vakṣyamāṇakrameṇeti niyamaḥ ||24||
idānīṃ pṛthivyāvāhanaṃ vakṣyamāṇasamādhinā bhūmiśuddhinimittamucyate- bhūmau dikṣu trivaḥ prathamamapi bali kṣetrapālāya dattvā paścādvaścaturbhirdiśi vidiśi gataṃ nirdahenmāravṛndam
bhūmi cāvāhayitvā sasalilakusumairarghamasyai pradāya paścācchuddhiṃ yatheṣṭāṃ kuru bhuvinilaye prārthayitvā tu tāṃ vai ||25||
iha pṛthvīsamādhinā bhūmi cāvāhayitvā ja: hū~ va~ ho: ebhirmantrapade : pūrvoktārdhapātre sasalilakusumairardhagandhādisahitairarghamasyai pradāya paścācchuddhi yatheṣṭāṃ kuru bhuvi nilaye prārthayitvā tu tāṃ vai ityanuprārthanā || 25 ||
devi tvaṃ sākṣibhūtā surapatisahite mārabhaṅge jinasya tasmāttvaṃ pūjanīyā suravaranamite gṛhṇa gṛhṇārdhakaṃ me
bhagnaṃ mārasya sainyaṃ prabalamapi yathā bodhihetojinena śiṣyāṇāṃ sekahetorahamapi ca tathā māranāśaṃ karomi || 26 ||
15 20 20 25 5 vimalaprabhāyāṃ abhiṣeka- devi tvaṃ sākṣibhūtā surapatisahite mārabhaṅge jinasya tasmāttvaṃ pūjanīyā suravaranamite gṛhṇa gṛhṇārdhakaṃ me iti stutvā prārthayet tām
bhagnaṃ mārasya sainyaṃ prabalamapi yathā bodhihetojinena, śiṣyāṇāṃ sekahetorahamapi ca tathā māranāśaṃ karomi tvayā sākṣibhūtayā iti
tato buddhādīnāṃ vakṣyamāṇapūjāṃ kṛtvā arghādikaṃ dattvā prārthayet || 26 ||
atra prārthanā - ye buddhāḥ sarvadikṣu vyapagatakaluṣā bodhisattvāḥ sabhāryā - ste māṃ vai pālayantu paramakaruṇayā maṇḍale sekahetoḥ
tānevādhyeṣya sarvān dṛḍhakhadiramayaiḥ kīlakaiḥ kīlayet kṣmāṃ dikkrodhān digvibhāge praharaṇasahitān vinyasedrakṣaṇārtham ||27||
ye buddhāḥ sarvadikṣu vyapagatakaluṣā bodhisattvā sabhāryāsta māṃ vai pālayantu paramakaruṇayā maṇḍale sekahetoḥ
'tānevādhyeṣya sarvān dṛḍhakhadiramayaiḥ kīlakaiḥ kīlayet kṣmām, vakṣyamāṇakrameṇetyadhyeṣaṇāniyamaḥ
tathā uktakkodhān daśadigvibhāge vinyaset praharaṇasahitān bhūmikīlanarakṣaṇārthamiti niyamaḥ || 27 ||
idānī bhūmikhāni nimittamucyate—- śalyaṃ sāṅgāraśṛṅgaṃ maraṇabhayakaraṃ bhūmigarbhe niviṣṭaṃ tasmin sthāne vināśo bhavati guṇavaśācchodhanīyaṃ hi tasmāt
ratnaṃ śaṅkhaśca kāco viṣayasukhakaro maṇḍalārthaṃ hi bhūmī prāsādārthaṃ gṛhārthaṃ prakaṭitaniyataścātra yāgādihetoḥ ||28||
iha bhūmigarbhe śalyādikaṃ niviṣṭaṃ śubhakarmaṇi vighnakaraṃ bhavati
tasmācchodhanīyaṃ śalyaṃ manuṣyāsthi 'sāṅgāramaṅgāraṃ vā śṛṅgasādhāraṇaṃ maraṇabhayakaraṃ śubhakarmaṇi tasmin sthāne vināśo bhavati guṇavaśāt śodhanīyaṃ hi tasmāt atha ratnaṃ vā śaṅkhaśca kāco vā dṛśyate, tadā sukhakaro bhavati maṇḍalārthaṃ hi bhūmau
tathā prāsādārthaṃ gṛhārthaṃ prakaṭita- niyataścātra yāgādihetoriti sthānaśuddhiniyamaḥ || 28 ||

paṭale, 29-30 ilo. ] rakṣā cakrapūrvaṅgamabhūmyādisaṃgrahamahoddeśa : idānīṃ bhūmiśodhanārthaṃ dinamucyate- pūrṇāyāṃ bhūmiśuddhirgrahaṇamapi tathā saṃgrahaḥ putrakāṇāṃ dvādaśyāṃ sūtrapāto madanamanudine śrīrajaḥpāta eva
sekādyaṃ pūrṇimāyāṃ dadati varagururmārabhaṅge dine ca tasmin rātrī pratiṣṭhā bhavati jinakule nānyarātrau pratiṣṭhā || 29 ||
pūrṇāyāmityādi
iha pratimāse ṣaṭ pūrṇāḥ, tadyathā - pañcamyau dve, daśamyau dve, pañca- daśamyau dve
maṇḍalālekhanāya kālāvadhi jñātvā 'śubhāśubhakarma jñātvā iha śubhakarmaṇi śukla- pañcamyāṃ 'daśamyāṃ pañcadaśyāṃ viṣṭi varjayitvā bhūmi śodhayet
tataḥ pūrvoktamṛttikayā pūraye- diti
aśubhakarmaṇi kṛṣṇapañcamyāṃ daśamyāṃ amāvasyāyāṃ naṣṭacandre bhūmi śodhayet pūrvokta- mṛttikayā pūrayediti
tato grahaṇamapi pūrṇāyāmarcanādikam
putrakāṇāṃ śiṣyāṇāṃ saṃgrahaḥ pūrṇimāyām
tato maṇḍalālekhanakālaṃ jñātvā dvādaśyāṃ sūtrapātaḥ kartavyaḥ
madanamanudine trayodaśyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ śrīrajaḥpātaḥ
sekādyaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ pūrṇimāyāṃ dadatī- tyāgamapāṭhaḥ
varagururmārabhaṅge dine ca
cakārādaparāyāṃ pūrṇimāyāṃ dadātīti niyamaḥ
evaṃ pratimādonāṃ tasminnetra pūrṇimārātrau pratiṣṭhā bhavati, jinakule nānyarātrau pratiṣṭhā iti tithiniyamaḥ || 29 ||
anye naktaṃ pratiṣṭhā bhavati narapate śuddhalagne grahādye- rjīve śuke'stamete nahi bhavati tadā vai vivāhaḥ pratiṣṭhā
jñātvā''cāryaḥ samastaṃ kṣititalanilaye lokalokottaraṃ ca yadyatkāryaṃ karoti prabhavati hi śubhaṃ tattadevaṃ samastam ||30||
athāmye naktaṃ pratiṣṭhā bhavati narapate śuddhalagne grahādyaḥ
iha jīve bṛha- spatī śukre astaṅgate sati sūryamaṇḍale praviṣṭe
nahi bhavati tadā tasmin māse ve ekāntaṃ vivāhaḥ pratiṣṭhā
evaṃ puṣye caitre bṛhaspatikṣetre sūrye praviṣṭe pūrṇimāyāṃ punarbhavati rājñā 'puṣyābhiṣekata iti, caitre buddhābhiṣekata iti niyamaḥ
evaṃ jñātvā ācāryaḥ samastaṃ kṣititalanilaye lokalokottaraṃ ca yadyatkāryaṃ karoti śāntyādikaṃ prabhavati phaladaṃ tattadevaṃ samastamiti tithiniyamaḥ
evaṃ krūrakarmāśubhadinaniyamaḥ || 30 ||
yatkāryaṃ
idānīṃ śiṣyarakṣāvidhirucyate- abhiṣeka- kṛtvā śiṣyasya rakṣāṃ śirasi hṛdi tathoṣṇīṣanābhau ca kaṇṭhe śrīguhyānje jinādyairubhayakulagataiḥ kāyavākcittavaḥ
evaṃ lepā (khā ) dikānāṃ prakaṭitaniyataiḥ śrīsvaraiḥ pustakānāṃ sattvānāṃ mokṣahetoramukamapi vibho maṇḍalaṃ lekhayāmi || 31 ||
iha prathamaṃ śiṣyāde rakṣāṃ kṛtvā paścāt saṃgrahecchiṣyādikam
atra jinādyai- 'devatībhirubhayakulagatai rakṣāṃ śirasi ūkāram, hṛdaye īkāram, uṣṇīṣe ākāram, nābhau lakāram, kaṇṭhe ṛkāram, guhye ākāramiti
ubhayakulagataiḥ kāyavākcitta- bhinnairiti

aiṛullū iti
'pustakānāṃ rakṣāṃ kṛtvā tato buddhānadhyeṣayet satvānāṃ mokṣahetoramuka- mapi kālacakrabhagavato maṇḍalaṃ lekhayāmi
tasmād buddhabodhisattvādhiṣṭhānaṃ śiṣyādīnāṃ kurvantvityadhyeṣaṇāniyamo rakṣāniyamaḥ || 31 ||
idānīṃ 'saṃkṣepata ucyate- śuddhe sthāne supūrṇe susamaviracite kūrmapṛṣṭhonnate ca ekādo hastamāne va sunṛpayugasāhastrameva pramāṇe
sūtraṃ vajraṃ rajo vai surayamavaruṇe cottare vajraghaṇṭāṃ dattvā labdhe nimitte prathamaparadinaṃ maṇḍalaṃ sūtraṇīyam ||32||
yena vakṣyamāṇe vistareṇa vaktavyaṃ sūtrādyadhivāsanādikamiti
śuddhe sthāne supūrṇe susamaviracite caturasre kiñcitkūrmapṛṣṭhonnate ca, ekādau hastamāne yugavasunṛpa iti
caturhaste'haste ṣoḍaśahaste, evaṃ sahasrahastaṃ yāvat pramāṇe sthāne pūrṇe
tatra sūtraṃ sure, vajraṃ yame, rajo bhāṇḍāni varuṇe, uttare vajraghaṇṭām madhye vijayakalaśaṃ vattvā tato vakṣyamāṇakrameṇa supto yadācāryo nimittaṃ labhate śiṣyo vā, tadā labdhe nimitte prathamaṃ pūrvadiśam, aparaṃ paścimadiśaṃ maṇḍalaṃ sūtraṇīyaṃ guruśiṣyābhyāmiti niyamaḥ ||32||

rakṣācakrapūrvaṅgamabhūmyādisaṃgrahamahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ durnimittalakṣaṇamucyate- chinne sūtre gurośca kṣatirapi parighālaṅghane putrakāṇāṃ vātoddhataṃ rajaścet prakaṭayati bhayaṃ rājyabhaṅgaśca rāṣṭre
taddṛṣṭvā durnimittaṃ punarapi ca vibhormantrajāpaṃ prakuryād bhūyo labdhe nimitte samaviṣamapadaiḥ sūtrapāto vidheyaḥ || 33 ||
iha chinne sūtre sati guroḥ kṣatirbhavati
api ca, parighālaṅghane putrakāṇāṃ kṣatiḥ
vātoddhūtaṃ rajaścet
iha maṇḍalikāvātenoddhataṃ rajo yadā bhavati, tadā gurvādīnāṃ bhayaṃ prakaṭayati, rājabhaṅgaṃ ca rāṣṭre prakaṭayatīti niyamaḥ

yāvannimittaṃ punarbhavati

evaṃ vyatikrameṇa viṣamapadena sūtrapāto vidheya iti niyamaḥ || 33 ||
idānīṃ parigha ucyate- prajñopāyāṅgamadhye bhavati saparigho maṇḍale varjanīyo bhūmau saṃgrāmakāle śikhipavanagataḥ sainyamadhye phaṇīva
saṃgrāme sainyanāśo bhavati bhuvitale tvaṅgayuddhe prahāra : tasmādyuddhe ca seke tvatibalaparigho mantriṇā veditavyaḥ || 34 ||
iha prajñopāyāṅgamadhye prabhavati parigho maṇḍale varjanīya iti
iha prajñāṅga dakṣiṇapaścimam, upāyāṅgaṃ pūrvottaram
anayormadhye parigho maṇḍale varjanīyaḥ
evaṃ bhūmau saṃgrāmakāle śikhipavanagata iti
āgneyyāṃ vāyavyāṃ diśi yāvadgatayordvayoḥ sainyayomaṃdhye phaṇīva daṇḍākāraḥ
saṃgrāme sainyanāśo bhavati, bhuvitale parighalaṅghanāt
aṅgayuddhe prahāro bhavati maṇḍale kalahavigraho bhavati
tasmādyuddhe ca seke tyatibalaparigho mantriṇā veditavya iti niyamaḥ || 34 ||
idānīmiṣṭadevatālambanamucyate saṃkṣepataḥ- ādyeḥ kādyaiḥ savaḥ svahṛdayakamale candrasūryāgnimūrdhni dhyātvā śrīkālacakraṃ śaśadharamadanākrāntamālīḍhapādam
bhayakaraṃ bhaṅgaśca niymama dakṣiṇe aṅka vimala prabhāyāṃ prajñābharhṛdabje saraviśaśipuṭe svasvavajrāṅkuśena buddhānākṛṣya devī rajasi samarasā nyastasūtre ca bhāvyāḥ || 35 ||
ādyeriti ākārādyaiḥ svaraiḥ kādyairiti kakā rādyairvyañjanaiścandrasūryā- tmakaiḥ
savatrairiti hū~kārasahitaiḥ
svahṛdayakamale candrasūryāgnimūrdhni vakṣyamāṇakrameṇa dhyātvā śrīkālacakraṃ śaśadharamadanākrāntamālīḍhapādaṃ prajñābhatrahṛdabje saraviśaśipuṭe svasva vajrāṅkuśena buddhānākṛṣya devoriti
rajasi samarasā buddhā bhāvyā rajaākāreṇa, nyastasūtre ca devyo bhāvyāḥ sūtrākāreṇeti niyamaḥ
idānīṃ pūrvabhūmyāvāhanādyamārabhya idaṃ samādhiṃ yāvanmantravidhirucyate
devatā- samādhirmāranigrahaṃ mantraṃ kālacakrabhagavato'ṅganyāsaḥ kāyavākcittaśodhanaṃ cocyate
iha prathamaṃ sādhanāpaṭale vakṣyamāṇakrameṇa mukhaviśuddhi tathāgatānāṃ pūjāṃ pāpadeśanāṃ puṇyānumodanāṃ triśaraṇagamanamātmabhāvaniryātanaṃ bodhicittotpādanaṃ mārgāśrayaṇaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ śūnyatālambanaṃ kṛtvā devatāniṣpādanaṃ prati kāyavākcittajñānaviśodhakāni mantra- padāni bhavanti
oṃ āḥ hūṃ ho haṃ kṣaḥ prajñopāyātmakakāyavākcittajñānādhipate mama kāyavākcittajñānavatraṃ vajrāmṛtasvabhāvaṃ kuru kuru skandhadhātvāyatanaṃ niḥsvabhāva
svāheti
idaṃ mantramuccārya uṣṇīṣopari vaṃkārapariṇataṃ vajracandramaṇḍalaṃ ṣoḍaśakalāpūrṇa dhyāyānmantrī
tatastenoṣṇīṣamārabhya pādanakhāntaṃ yāvat svaśarīraṃ sarpakañcukavat tyajet trīn vārān

pūrve sparśaguṇa viśuddhayā raktasya dvau pādau, sparśarūpaguṇadvayaviśuddhayā dakṣiṇe śvetasya tripādāḥ, sparśarūparasaguṇaviśuddhayā uttare pītasya, sparśarūparasagandhacaturguṇaviśuddhayā catuḥpādā pronnatiḥ paścime |

anyā rekhā sāmānyā pītarajaso dviguṇā bhavati |

evaṃ kṣitijalahutabhugvāyuvalayeṣu vajrāvalīṣu ca ghuṇakadvaye prākāramānena kāyamaṇḍalasyeti rajaḥ pronnatiniyamo dhātuguṇabhedena, apara rajaḥpātaḥ sarvatra triguṇabhedena samānaḥ sarvabhūmiṣu prākārāṇāṃ triguṇastri'valayapramāṇa iti rajoviśuddhiḥ || 51 ||

tataḥ pratiṣṭhāpayitvā pūrvoktavidhinā'rghaṃ dattvā oṃ āḥ hū~ ho: traidhātukeśvara kālacakra aghaṃ pratīccha saparivārastvaṃ bhagavan me varado bhava śiṣyāṇāṃ ca ityadhyeṣyārdhaṃ maṇḍalarajo bāhye maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā dāpayet
tato'paramaṇḍalaṃ vijayakalaśāgrataḥ kṛtvā hastamekam, tatra pūrvoktagandhādikaṃ pādyaṃ pradāpayet
evaṃ sarvadikṣu vidikṣu pratyeka devatānāmapi naivedyādikaṃ deyam
sarvadikṣu rajomaṇḍalabāhye rajobhūmyāṃ puṣpādikaṃ na dātavyam
datte rajovilopo bhavati
pratiṣṭhāpitamaṇḍale rajovilopāt stūpabheda iti
tena maṇḍalabāhye pūrvadvāre vijayakalaśopari puṣpakṣepaḥ kartavyaḥ kulaparīkṣārtham
anyathā sahasra hastamaṇḍale kutaḥ puṣpaṃ patiṣyati śiṣyeṇa kṣiptam
pūrvavatrāciṣaṃ tyaktvā vajrajvālāpi tatra sapāda hastaśataṃ bhavati
kathaṃ tāṃ laṅghayi [ tvā devatā- dhiṣṭhānaṃ vinā kuladikṣu puṣpaṃ patiṣyati
tasmādvijayakalaśe pañcacihnāni kṛtvā tatra puṣpakṣepo vakṣyamāṇaḥ kartavyaḥ
tena gandhādikaṃ maṇḍalabāhye na rajobhūmyām
rajomaṇḍalaṃ bhagavataḥ kāyo veditavya iti niyamaḥ ||
||

idānīṃ 'puṣṭyādikarmabhedena mantravidhirucyate— puṣṭau svāhāntamantro bhavati narapate śāntike'sau namo'nta ākṛṣṭau voṣaḍanto bhavati ca vaṣaḍantaśca vaśye tathaiva

idānīṃ divyāveśānāmutpādalakṣaṇamucyate- āveśo mantriṇāṃ vai bhavati narapate bhāvanāyā balena sevābhedaiḥ kadācid bahuvidhasamaye mantrajāpādibhiśca

vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ abhiṣeka- tyaktetyādinā

prathamaṃ saṃvatyarthaṃ vratānyā bodhiparyantam

tatra vratānyābodhiparyantamāha bhagavān - hiṃsāsatyaṃ parastrīṃ tyaja svaparadhanaṃ madyapānaṃ tathaiva saṃsāre vajrapāśaḥ svakuśalanidhanaṃ pāpametāni pañca

kasmāt ?

idānīṃ maṇḍalapraveśa ucyate- śrīmantreṇābhimantraya karakamalapuṭe karakamalapuṭe puṣpamekaṃ pradeyaṃ ādo bhrāmya trivārān karakamalapuṭānmaṇḍale puṣpamokṣaḥ
yasmin sthāne supuṣpaṃ patati narapate tatkulaṃ tasya nūnaṃ paścāt saptābhiṣeka strividha iha yathānuttaraḥ saṃpradeyaḥ || 95 ||
śrīmantreṇetyādinā
iha prathamaṃ vratadānānantaram
pītavastreṇa raktavastreṇa vā sarvakarmaṇi mukhaṃ bandhayet, śāntyādiṣu varṇena devatāvarṇavarṇena sūkṣmavastreṇānena mantreṇa - oṃ dvādaśāṅganirodhakāriṇe 'hū~ phaṭ

anena sārddhavṛttadvayena

] mudrādṛṣṭimaṇḍala visarjanavīra bhojya vidhimahoddeśaḥ pāṇī pṛṣṭhe'ṅgulīnāṃ kramapariracitaṃ bandhanaṃ kaṇṭhikāyāṃ tryaṅgulyanyonyayogobhayakarakuṭilādyantayormekhalāyām || 182 ||
tathā hastābhyāṃ śaṅkhamudrā śliṣṭā mukuṭe bhavati ityūrdhva tarjanīdvandvayogaḥ
ubhayahastayo viṣama karataladeśaḥ pañcāṅgulīnāṃ pṛṣṭhataḥ kuṇḍaleṣviti kuṇḍalamudrā tathā pāṇau pṛṣṭhe'ṅgulīnāṃ kramapariracitaṃ parasparaṃ bandhanaṃ kaṇṭhikāyāmevaṃ kaṇṭhikā mudrā
tathā mekhalāyāṃ vyaṅgulyo'nāmāmadhyamātarjanyo vāmasavyayoraṅgalyagre melāpakaḥ kaniṣṭhā- ṅguṣṭhāyā muṣṭibandha iti mekhalāmudrā || 182 ||
aṅguṣṭho madhyame dve valayamiva kṛtī nūpure muṣṭibandhāt tadvat keyūrayugme bhavati ca kaṭake tarjanīdvandvayogaḥ
aṅguṣṭho ḍākinīnāṃ bhavati varakulaṃ tarjanī guhyakānāṃ gandharvāṇāṃ phaṇīnāṃ kramapariracitā madhyamānāmikā vā || 183||

tathā keyūre kaṭake vā'ṅguṣṭhayogastarjanoyogo valaya iva bhavatīti bhagavataścihnamudrā

mudrā ucyante
iha pañcāṅgulyardhavakrā siṃhanakhākāreṇa jātimudrā nakhīnāmekakaratale ubhaye vā
tathā tarjanyo dve'rdhavakre'rdhacandrākāreṇobhayakaramuṣṭayupari śirasi darśitā śṛṅgiṇāṃ muṣṭibandhā- iti śṛṅgiṇīmudrā || 184 ||
baddhe'nyonyaṃ kaniṣṭhe viṣamakaratale pakṣayogo'ṇḍajānāṃ pañcāṅgulyagravakā bhavati hi phaṇināṃ jātimudrā viśiṣṭā
tarjanyantāḥ prasārāḥ pratidivasabalau cāpare'dhaśca śliṣṭe jvālāyāṃ śliṣṭajyeṣṭhau varakaratalayostarjanī sāritānyā || 185 ||
tathā baddhe'nyonyaṃ kaniṣṭhe viṣamakaratale pṛṣṭhayogena tarjanīmadhya 'mādyā nāmikā - ṅguṣṭhayormukhayoga ityevaṃ garuḍamudrā
tathā pañcāṅgulyo'praparvavakrā darśitā phaṇināṃ jātimudrā bhavati
tathā jvālāmudrāyāṃ madhyamānāmākaniṣṭhā prasāritā ubhayakare tarjanyau dve'nyonyaṃ granthite śirasa uparyadho' mukhenāṅgaṣṭhau śliṣṭāviti varakaratalayo- stenaiva prakāreṇa, kintu tarjanī 'sāritā'nyā dvitīyā jvālāmudreti jātimudrā tiryaṅmukhīnāṃ darśanīyeti niyamaḥ || 185 ||
idānīṃ vīravīreśvarīṇāṃ parasparasaṃbhāṣaṇamudrā ucyante - tarjanyā darśanaṃ ve kathitamapi bhavet svāgataṃ yoginaśca dvābhyāṃ susvāgataṃ ca pravadati subhagā kṣemamaṅguṣṭhabandhāt
aṅgulyāichoṭikāyāḥ kathayati niyataṃ śreṣṭhamantrī tvamatra aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ sasamayasurayā tarpaṇaṃ te karomi || 186 ||
tarjanyetyādinā |
iha yatra kutracid 'darśanamātreṇa muṣṭiṃ baddhvā ūrdhvaṃ mukhāṃ tarjanīmaṅguṣṭhābhimukhāṃ śliṣṭāṃ darśayet |
tasyā darśane sati svāgataṃ kathitaṃ bhaved, yoginyā yogino yoginā yoginyā vā iti |
dvābhyāṃ tarjanīmadhyamābhyāṃ pṛṣṭhataḥ saṃyuktābhyāṃ susvāgataṃ kathitaṃ bhavati |
tathā pravadati subhagā kṣemaṃ vāmāṅguṣṭhamuṣṭi- bandhāditi |
tathā'ṅgulyāichoṭikāyā aṅguṣṭhatarjanyāḥ kathayati niyataṃ śreṣṭhamantrī tvamatreti |

oṣṭha ityādinā |

tatra oṣṭhe vakre bhrūvi vakre netre vakre tathā śirasi kaṇḍūyamāne vadatyati' mahāmūrkhastvaṃ yadā mudrā saṃketakaṃ na jānātī (sī) ti |
tathā yadi daṃṣṭrāmadhye kaniṣṭhāropitā tayā tadā sā kaniṣṭhā bhayaṃ prakaṭayati |
athavā tarjanī hṛdaye mukhe vā ropitā kathayati bhayamiti |
tathāṅguṣṭhe muṣṭibandhaṃ kṛtvā tasmā- nmuṣṭibandhāt tenaiva bhuvi sphālanāt karacaraṇābhyāṃ sphālane sati bhakṣayāmoti vadati, mudrāsaṃketānabhijñatvāditi nirbhartsanamudrāḥ |
tathā bhojanārthaṃ jihvāsparśe ca bhuktamanā- mikayeti |

paṭale, ślo. ] mudrādṛṣṭimaṇḍala visarjanavīra bhojya vidhimahoddeśaḥ keśetyādinā |
iha yadā sādhako mithyāhaṅkāreṇāvamānaṃ karoti, pratimudrāṃ vā darśayati sāmarthyaṃ vinā, tadā' sāmarthyayuktā svakeśān chedayati svadantairevaṃ keśacchede svadantaiḥ kṛte satīdaṃ vadati mudrayā, he narapaśo pātanīyastvamatra mayeti |
tathā'- nyonyaṃ dantairdantān ghṛṣyati tatra ghṛṣṭe satīdaṃ vadati tava piśitamidaṃ bhakṣaṇīyaṃ mayādya he narapaśo iti |
tathā jihvauṣṭhe lālite sati vadati tava tanau raktapānaṃ karomīti |
tathā oṣṭhe dantaiḥ saṃdaśyamāne'pi vadatyudaragatamidaṃ bhakṣayāmastavāntraṃ he narapaśo kva gacchasīti mudrāṃ darśayati kruddhā satī |
tena sāmarthyarahitena pratimudrā na darśanīyā |
tāsāmabhivādanaṃ kṛtvā hṛdaye vāmakaratalaṃ dattvā vāmāvartena paribhrāmya svakāyaṃ tato vāmahastenodhvaṃ praṇāmaṃ kṛtvā gantavyam |
tābhiḥ sārdhaṃ vādo na kartavya iti bālayogināṃ niyamaṃ yaddadāti tattena karaṇīyamanyathā maraṇaṃ nayati ruṣṭā dūtikā sāmarthyayukteti bhagavato niyamaḥ |
idānīṃ parasparamudrādarśane pratimudrālakṣaṇamucyate |
iha kālacakrabhagavato vāma- savyabhujābhyāṃ yāni cihnāni tatsvarūpā mudrāḥ |
tataśca parasparaṃ mudrāpratimudreti |
vajravajraghaṇṭayoḥ, khaṅgaphalakayoḥ, triśūlakhaṭvāṅgayoḥ, kartikākapālayoḥ, bāṇacāpayoḥ, aṅkuśapāśayoḥ, ḍamarukaratnayo:, mudgarapadmayoḥ, cakraśaṅkhayoḥ, kundadarpaṇayo:, daṇḍa- śṛṅkhalayoḥ, paśubrahmavaktrayoḥ, gajacarmatarjanyoḥ, mukuṭakuṇḍalayoḥ, kaṇṭhikārucakayoḥ, mekhalānūpurayoḥ, śṛṅgīnakhyoḥ, nāgagaruḍayoḥ, hastapādayoḥ, mukhagudayoḥ bhagaliṅgayāḥ, stanauṣṭhayoḥ, netrabhruvoḥ, tilakakajjalayoḥ, prakopaśikhāmokṣaṇayo:, jānūrvoḥ kaṇṭha- lalāṭayoḥ, nābhihṛdayayoḥ, sīmantasindūrarekhayoḥ, daṃṣṭrākanīyasyoḥ aṅguṣṭhānāmikayoḥ, tarjanīmadhyamayoḥ |

tadyathā - lāsyāyogena lāsyā bhavati narapate hāsyayogena hāsyā, nṛtyāyogena nṛtyā bhavati bahuvidhā vādyayogena vādyā, gītāyogena gotā varavividhaguṇā gandhayogena gandhā, mālāyogena mālā bhavati guṇavaśāda dhūpayogena dhūpā || 192 ||
vimalaprabhāyāṃ dīpākāreṇa dīpā khalu nihatatamā pātramudrā'mṛtā syād ityevaṃ sarvamudrāḥ punarapi ca tataḥ pañcabhedaivibhinnāḥ |
anyā mudrāstvanantāḥ sakalatanugatā yoginā veditavyā yadyad vastusvabhāvo bhavati bhuvitale tatsvabhāvāśca mudrāḥ || 193||
dopākāreṇa dīpā khalu nihatatamā pātramudrā'mṛtā syāt ityevaṃ sarvamudrāḥ punarapi ca tataḥ pañcabhedaivibhinnāḥ, anyā mudrāstvanantāḥ sakalatanugatā yoginā veditavyāḥ, yadyad vastusvabhāvo bhavati bhuvitale tatsvabhāvāstu mudrā ityataḥ - yāvanto dṛṣṭivikṣepāstāvanmudrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
mudrāyāḥ pratimudrāyāṃ kaḥ samartho'vadhāritum ||

idānīṃ dṛṣṭi 'saṃketa ucyate- tiryagdṛṣṭyā ca dūtī kathayati subhagasyāgatastvaṃ kutaśca pratyuktaṃ yoginaḥ syāt śirasi gatakarasyekṣaṇe taddiśo vai |
kṣema ste'pyūrdhvadṛṣṭyā cititalagatayā tiṣṭha viśrāmaya tvaṃ gaccha tvaṃ vakradṛṣṭyā kathayati surataṃ rāgadṛṣṭyā ca dūtī || 194 ||
tiryagityādinā |
iha yadā yogino darśane dūtoti yoginī tiyaṃgdṛṣṭyā darśayet tayā dṛṣṭyā kathayati subhagasyeti yogin āgato'si kutaḥ sthānāt tvamiti pṛcchati |
tataḥ pratyuktaṃ yoginaḥ syāt śirasi vāmakaragatasyekṣaṇāt taddiśo vai sthānādāgamanakathanam |

gaccha tvaṃ vakradṛṣṭaceti kathayati |
rāgadṛṣṭyā surataṃ kurviti kathayati dūtī || 194 ||
mitraṃ me saumyadṛṣṭyā prakaṭayati bhayaṃ krodhadṛṣṭyā bhṛkuṭayā krūrā'haṃ keśadṛṣṭyā kathayati subhagasyeṅgiteḥ svasvabhāvam |
ūrṇādṛṣṭayottamāhaṃ prakaṭayati guṇaṃ yoginī ghrāṇadṛṣṭyā saubhāgyaṃ coṣṭhadṛṣṭyā vadati kucayugālokane'haṃ sumudrā || 195 ||
paṭale, 193-197 balo. ]
mudrādṛṣṭimaṇḍala visarjanavī ra bhojya vidhimahoddeśaḥ - mitraṃ me tvaṃ saumyadṛṣṭyā kathayatīti saṃbhāṣaṇadṛṣṭiniyamaḥ |
idānīṃ bhayadṛṣṭaya ucyante – iha yadā bhṛkuṭīṃ kṛtvā krodhadṛṣṭi darśayati tadā tayā krodhadṛṣṭyā bhṛkuṭadhā yogino bhayaṃ prakaṭayati, ajñatvāditi |
tathā keśadṛṣṭyā krūrā'hamiti kathayati subhagasya yoginaḥ svasvabhāvamebhiriṅgitairiti |
tathā ūrṇādṛṣṭayā uttamāhamiti prakaṭayati |
tathā guṇaṃ prakaṭayati ghrāṇadṛṣṭayā |
oṣṭhadṛṣṭyā saubhāgyaṃ svakīyaṃ prakaṭayati |
tathā svakucayugālokane'haṃ sumudreti vadati || 195 ||
hṛddṛṣṭyā bhāvitātmā vadati bhujayugālokane'haṃ pracaṇḍā śaktāhaṃ skandhadṛṣṭyā sanakhakaratalālokane rākṣasī ca |
pṛṣṭhālo ke bhujaṅgī tvahamiti samayī nābhidṛṣṭayā narendra śuddhāhaṃ guhyadṛṣṭyā'pyahamapi surate durjayā corudṛṣṭyā ||196||
hṛddṛṣṭayā bhāvitātmeti vadati |
tathā bhujayugālokane'haṃ pracaṇḍeti vadati |
tathā skandhadṛṣṭyā śaktāhamiti vadati |
tathā sanakhakaratalālokane rākṣasī cāmiti vadati |
tathā pṛṣṭhāloke nāginyahamiti vadati |
tathā nābhidṛṣṭacā'haṃ samayinīti vadati |
narendretyāmantraṇam |
tathā guhyadṛṣṭayā śuddhāhamiti vadati |
tathā ūrudṛṣṭaghā surate durjayāhamiti kathayati || 196 ||
siddhāhaṃ jānudṛṣṭyā kathayati niyataṃ caddhidā pādadṛṣṭyā pādāṅguṣṭhāvaloke tvahamapi bhuvane vajrakāyaikavīrā, sarvāṅgulyagradṛṣṭyā tribhuvananilaye sarvagā viśvamātā dūtīnāmeva dṛṣṭi: kṣititalanilaye yoginā veditavyā || 197||
tathā jānudṛṣṭacā siddhā'hamiti kathayati |
tathā pādadṛṣṭacā'hamṛddhideti kathayati |
tathā pādāṅguṣṭhāvaloke kṛte sati tayā dṛṣṭyāpyahaṃ bhuvane vajrakāyaikavīreti kathayati |
tathā sarvāṅgulyapradṛṣṭyā pādayostribhuvananilaye 'sarvagā viśvamātā'hamiti kathayati |
evaṃ yogyapi sāmarthyayuktamātmaguṇān dūtīnāṃ prakaṭayati |
evamuktakrameṇa tīnāmeva dṛṣṭiḥ punarapi bahuvidhā veditavyā svabhāvairiti dṛṣṭisaṃketa niyamaḥ |
tathā chomakāḥ |
yasya bhāvasya yannāma tasyādyākṣareṇa tadgrahaṇaṃ veditavyam, prastāva - vaśāditi |
yathā saindhavamānayetyukte snāne vastram, bhojane lavaṇam, gamane'śvaḥ, yuddhe vimalaprabhāyāṃ khaḍgamiti nyāyena 'sarvaḥ prathamākṣara saṃketavastudharmo veditavyaḥ |
gaṇacakrādike'samaya- sattvamadhye sandhyābhāṣāntareṇa chomakena vā vaktavyaṃ yoginā yoginyā vā iti sarvatra niyamaḥ || 197 ||
idānīṃ śiṣyāṇāṃ dānārtha svaśarīrādivibhāganiyama ucyate-- ṣaḍbhāgaṃ dehamadhye karacaraṇatanordānamapyuttamāṅga vācā karmendriyāṇāṃ saguṇamapi manastvindriyāṇāṃ ca madhye |
dhātvaṃśaṃ dhātumadhye dvipadapaśugaṇān tattvabhāgena cānyad ācāryāya pradāya vrajati sukhapadaṃ divyamudrānuviddhaḥ || 198 ||
ṣaḍbhāgamityādinā |
iha yadā vajrācāryeṇābhiṣikto gṛhastha 'ścellako bhikṣuko vā, teneyaṃ pratijñā kartavyā mayā sarvakālaṃ 'ṣaḍaṃśaṃ sarvavaratūnāṃ dānaṃ dātavyamiti |
tatra prathamaṃ tāvat ṣaḍbhāgaṃ dehamadhye karacaraṇatanoriti hastadvayasya caraṇadvayasya tanoreṣu pañcasu madhye ṣaṣṭhamuttamāṅgadānaṃ namaskārārtham ācāryāya pradeyaṃ buddhabodhisattvāya gurave |
taṃ dattvā vrajati sukhapadaṃ divyamudrānuviddho dānadāteti | tathā vācā karmendriyāṇāṃ madhye deyā pāṇipādapāyubhagādīndriyāṇāṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ vāgindriyaṃ satyavacanārthaṃ vācā deyeti bhagavato niyamaḥ |
tathā cakṣuḥ śrotraghrāṇajihvākāyendriyāṇāṃ madhye ṣaṣṭhaṃ manaḥ saguṇaṃ māyāprapañcarahitaṃ sattvārthaṃ deyamiti dānaniyamaḥ |
tathā dhātvaṃśamiti |
cirāgantukadhātūnāṃ ṣaḍaṃśaṃ deyaṃ catuṣpadāni ṣaḍaṃśena deyāni dhātavaḥ svarṇaratnadhānyādyacetanāni dravyāṇi teṣāṃ yoginyādipūjārthamiti |
tathā sacetanāni dvipada- pañcāṃśānyātma kuṭumba bhogāya sthāpayitavyāni |
tathā tatsvabhāgena cānyad rūpabhāryādikaṃ māsamadhye pañcavārān kāmadānena deyamiti tathāgata- niyamaḥ |
anyathā mantranaye kāma dānena vinā'nantakalpairmahāmudrāsiddhirna bhavati |
karmamudrājñānamudrāsiddhirapi na bhavati, rāgābhibhūtasya kula grahāditi |
evaṃ ṣaḍ- vibhāgadānaniyamaḥ || 198 ||
idānīṃ karuṇābhiṣeka ucyate- ye satvā lokadhātau trividhabhavagatā jñānavajrāṅkuśena ākṛṣṭvā tān samantāt paramakaruṇayā maṇḍale cābhiṣicya |
nāsti |
ṣaḍaṅgaṃ |
ṣaḍaṅgaṃ |
ityadhikam |
budderva jāmṛtenāmalaśaśivapuṣā vajriṇo labdhamārgāḥ svasthāne preṣaṇīyā vyapagatakaluṣā bodhicaryānurūḍhāḥ || 199 ||
ye sattvā ityādinā |
iha lokaghātau ṣaḍgatiṣu ye sattvā' anabhiṣiktā- strividha' bhavagatāstān sarvajñajñānavajñāṅkuśenākṛṣya bhāvanāmātrāt paramakaruṇayā maṇḍale cābhiṣicya buddhaviṇmūtraraktamajjābhiḥ tathā vajrāmṛtena śukreṇāmalaśaśivapuṣā tato vajriṇo labdhamārgāḥ santa iti vibhāvya tataste svasthāne preṣaṇīyā vajrācāryeṇa sarve vyapagatakaluṣā bodhicaryānurūḍhā iti karuṇābhiṣekaniyamaḥ || 199 ||
idānīmavadhūtasya śiṣyasyābhiṣeka niyama ucyate- dravyābhāve'bhiṣeko jinapativacanenāvadhūtasya deya evaṃ dhūmādimārgaḥ sakalaguṇa nidhirnāḍikā yogayuktaḥ |
sevārthaṃ hastamudrā svahṛdayavaśagā sarvadoṣairvimuktā anyeṣāṃ neva deyaṃ jinavarahṛdayaṃ mātṛpujāvihīnam || 200||
dravyābhāva ityādinā |
iha yadāvadhūtasya śiṣyasya dravyābhāvastadā guruṇā dravyābhāve'bhiṣeko jinapativacanenāvadhūtasya deyo yathānukrameṇa saptābhiṣekastato deyaḥ |
kalaśādikastrividhaḥ evaṃ caturtho dhūmādimārge deya iti kathanīyo vāceti |
tathā sakalaguṇa nidhirnāḍikā yogayukto mahākṣarasukhakṣaṇo vācā kathanīyaḥ |
tasya sevārthaṃ karmamudrā'bhāve sati hastamudrāniyamo deyaḥ |
svahṛdayavaśagā sā hastamudrā sarvadoṣa- vimuktā bodhicittasthirīkaraṇāyeti niyamaḥ |
anyeṣāṃ punargṛhasthānāṃ naiva deyaṃ jinavara- hṛdayaṃ vajrapadaṃ mātṛpūjāvihīnamiti tathāgatase kaniyamaḥ || 200 ||
dattaṃ idānīṃ maṇḍalavisarjana mucyate- sekānte śrīghaṭānāṃ mṛdutanusukhadaṃ kañcukaṃ vastrayugmaṃ deyaṃ śrīyoginībhyastvaparamapi tathā kañcukaṃ vastrayugmam |
dvārasthebhyaḥ pradeyaṃ sakalagaṇakulāyātmaśaktyā tathānyad ante homaṃ prakṛtya svahṛdayakamale jñānasattvaṃ praveśya || 201 ||
sekānta ityādinā |
iha sekāvasāne yadvastrayugmaṃ sakañcukaṃ ghaṭopari tadyoginībhyo ghaṭarakṣapālikābhyo deyam |
aparamapi tathā kanakaṃ • • vimalaprabhāyāṃ vastrayugmam, pratyekaṃ dvārasthebhyaḥ pradeyaṃ rakṣapālebhyaḥ prāg yathāvibhavato mukuṭādikaṃ deyamiti niyamaḥ |
tathā sakalagaṇakulāyātmaśaktyā tathānyad gaṇacakre voravīreśvarībhya iti |
tato gaṇacakraṃ visarjya ante homaṃ prakṛtyācāryaḥ pūrvavat pūjāṃ kṛtvā pūrvadvāre bhagavato'bhimukho vajravajraghaṇṭāṃ gṛhītvā vajrabandhena svahṛdayakamale jñānasattvaṃ praveśya || 201 ||
svasthāne laukikān ve sakalamapi rajo vāhayecchuddhanadyāṃ tāmbūlaṃ gandhadhūpaṃ kusumaphalasamaṃ śāṭikāṃ kanyakānām |
dattvā''cāryaḥ saśiṣyaḥ sakalagaṇakulaṃ tarpayitvā yatheṣṭaṃ śiṣyasyājñāṃ pradāya pravarakaruṇayā preṣayet svasvadhāni || 202||
svasthāne laukikān vai indrādīn tato'śvatthapatreṇa svakareṇa vā vajreṇa vā brahmasūtramārgeṇa mahāsukhacakravajraṃ yāvallopayet tataḥ svaśirasi rajastruṭimātraṃ dattvā padmādikaṃ lopayet iti maṇḍalavisarjana niyamaḥ |
tataḥ sakalaṃ rajo gajopari chatra- cāmaradhvajapūjāsahitaṃ nītvā śuddhanadyāṃ samudragāminyāṃ vāhayet |
yatra kalaśe nītaṃ taṃ kalaśamudakapūrṇa kṛtvā punargajaskandhe sthāpya maṇḍalagṛhamānayet |
gajābhāve sukhāsane ṛmpāṇe kṛtvā neyamiti rajovisarjanam |
tato maṇḍalagṛhamāgatya gomayenopalipte maṇḍalagṛhe daśakumārikāṃ pañcavarṣādārabhya daśavārṣikāṃ yāvad dugdhena ghṛtena pāyasena khaṇḍalaḍḍukādyairmadhurāhāraiḥ pūrvāhṇe saṃtarpya tatastāmbūlaṃ gandhadhūpaṃ kusumaṃ ca phalasamaṃ śāṭikāṃ kalaśagrīvā- veṣṭitāṃ kanyakānāmiti kumārikāṇāṃ dattvā''cāryaḥ saśiṣyaḥ sakalagaṇakūlaṃ vīra bhoje - na tarpayitvā yatheṣṭamiti |
tatra vīrabhojye vidhirayam - ihācārya parīkṣāyāṃ tridhā vajrācāryaḥ, uttamo madhyamo'dhama iti |
tadyathā- daśatattvaparijñānāt trayāṇāṃ bhikṣuruttamaḥ |
madhyamaḥ śrāvaṇe rākhyo gṛhasthastvadhamastayoḥ || iti niyamāt tantre teṣāṃ bhikṣucellakagṛhasthānāmekasaṃkaraṃ sāmānyena jyeṣṭhakaniṣṭhatvaṃ vā'bhiṣekataḥ |
tasmādbhikṣuvajradharapaṅktiḥ pūrvābhimukhī bhavati kartavyā vā, cellaka- paṅktiruttarābhimukhī, gṛhasthācāryapaṅkti dakṣiṇābhimukhī |
evaṃ bhikṣuṇīpaṅktiḥ, taṃ taṃ kalaśa |
kusumaphala |
vā nāsti |
pūrvāmukhī |
dakṣiṇāmukhī |
paṭale, ślo. ]
mudrādṛṣṭimaṇḍala visarjana vīrabhojya vidhimahoddeśaḥ mahallikāpaṅktiḥ upāsikāpaṅktiḥ pṛthak |
teṣāṃ jyeṣṭhakaniṣṭhādinā āsanāni deyāni |
tatra bhikṣūṇāṃ yo jyeṣṭhaḥ sekena kintu mūrkhaḥ, laghuko mahācāryastantradeśakaḥ, tayo- ryastantradeśakaḥ sa vīrabhojye gaṇanāyakaḥ |
jyeṣṭho'nyagṛhe pṛthak santarpaṇīyaḥ |
evamanye'pi jyeṣṭhā dharma  deśakā  upadeśakā iti sattvārthaka raṇe'śaktatvāditi |
anye punaścellaka- gṛhasthāḥ prāgabhiṣiktā bhikṣorvajradharasya jyeṣṭhā na bhavanti, yāvadabhijñā notpadyate |
atha vivādaṃ karoti kaścit, tadā sāmarthyaṃ pṛcchyate |
yadi darśayatyabhijñādikam, tadā sa gaṇacakranāyaka iti |
atha mithyābhiṣekābhimānaḥ kalahaṃ karoti saṃvṛti tyaktvā tadā svagṛhānni rghāṭayet |
atha nirdhāṭito daṇḍamaṅgīkaroti, tadā khānapānādiko daṇḍo deyo daṇḍā- dhipatinā |
evaṃ bhikṣucellakagṛhasthānāṃ yathānukrameṇa khānapānādikaṃ deyam |
tadeva sarvaṃ prāk sthāpanīyam |
teṣāṃ madhye maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā kālacakrabhagavataḥ prathamapaṭṭikāṃ khānapānādikaṃ dattvā tato bhikṣvādīnāmācāryāṇāmanyeṣāmabhiṣiktānāṃ teṣu mūleṣu sthitānāṃ deyam |
evaṃ sakalagaṇakulaṃ tarpayitvā yatheṣṭam, tata ācāryaḥ śiṣyasyājñāṃ pradā saṃghadānārthaṃ tadā''tmaśaktyā saṃghāya dakṣiṇāṃ dattvā pravarakaruṇayā ānanditaṃ preṣayet svasvadhāmni iti vīrabhojyaniyamaḥ || 202 ||
idānīṃ sarvabhayopadravaśamanamucyate- śatruḥ siṃho gajendro haviruragapatistaskarā pāśabandha: kṣubdhāmbhodhiḥ piśācā maraṇabhayakarā vyādhirindropasargaḥ |
dāridryaṃ strīviyoga: kṣubhitanṛpabhayaṃ vajrapāto'rthanāśo nāśaṃ tasya prayānti pratidina caraṇaṃ yaḥ smaredyoginīnām || 203||
śatrurityādinā |
iha kaścidyaḥ kulaputroṃ maṇḍalaṃ vartayitvā'bhiṣekaṃ gṛhītvā pratidinaṃ caraṇaṃ yoginīnāṃ pūrvoktānām, adhyātmanyavadhūtyādīnāṃ caraṇaṃ smarati, tasya sarvāṇi bhayāni nāśaṃ prayānti |
śatrubhayaṃ siṃhabhayaṃ gajabhayaṃ vahnibhayam uragabhayaṃ taskara- bhayaṃ pāśabandhabhayaṃ kṣubdhasamudrabhayaṃ piśācabhayaṃ vyādhibhayam indropadravabhayaṃ dāridryadu:khabhayaṃ strīviyogaduḥkhabhayaṃ kṣubhita nṛpabhayaṃ vajrapātabhayam arthanāśabhayam |
evaṃ ṣoḍaśabhayānya- nyānyapi nāśaṃ prayānti |
eṣāṃ vistāraṃ prathamapaṭale stutidvāreṇa kathitam, tenātra na vimalaprabhāyāṃ prakāśitam |
evaṃ kālacakramaṇḍale'bhiṣiktaḥ sarvayoginī yoga tantreṣvabhiṣikto bhavati |
sarvatantrāṇāṃ deśakaḥ, sarva mantrāṇāmanujñāpakaḥ, sarvasiddhīnāṃ sādhakaḥ |
yathā mañju - śrībhagavān, yathā kālacakra ādibuddhastathā vajrācāryo draṣṭavyo mokṣārthibhiḥ kālacakra - tantra deśaka iti paramādibuddhānusāreṇābhiṣekapaṭala ṭīkā likhitā || 203 ||
iti śrī mūlatantrānusāriṇyāṃ laghukālacakratantrarājaṭīkāyāṃ dvādaśasāhasrikāyāṃ vimalaprabhāyāmabhiṣekapaṭale mudrādṛṣṭimaṇḍala visarjanavīrabhojya- vidhi mahoddeśaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ ||

gurupratyayataḥ śuddhaṃ rajomaṇḍalamādiśet

evaṃ pratyayiteḥ kathaṃ punarayaṃ satyena no gṛhyate saṃvṛtyā paramārthato'pi gaditaḥ sekārthatattvakramaḥ
yatsatyaṃ tadihābhiṣekapaṭale tāthāgatābhyāgata- śreyaḥ śrībhiralaṅkṛtaṃ ratiphalaṃ mokṣasya saukhyasya ca
sukhādvījādasmāt prabhavati manaḥ kalpaviṭapo mahārāgāsekāt tribhuvanabhuvaḥ sarpati tataḥ

namaḥ śrīkālacakrāya
puṇyajñāna vinirmitaṃ bhagavato durdāntasattvāḥ sadā rūpaṃ bhairavabhīṣaṇaṃ gatamadaṃ paśyanti santo janāḥ
bhāṣā sarvarutā paraśrutigatā sanmārgasaṃdeśikī sattvānāmadhimukticittavaśato yasyaiva tasme namaḥ
sarvākāravaropetaḥ kāyo nānādhimuktitaḥ
svasvabhāvena sattvairnirmāṇalakṣaṇaḥ
yaḥ prakāśate
sattvāśayavaśenaiṣa kāyaḥ saṃbhogalakṣaṇaḥ
nānityo nāpi nityo yo naiko nānekalakṣaṇaḥ
na bhāvo nāpyabhāvo'sau dharmakāyo nirāśrayaḥ
dṛśyate sarvasatvarutairṛddhimātmano śūnyatā karuṇā'bhinno rāgārāgavivarjitaḥ
na prajñā nāpyupāyo'sau kāyaḥ svābhāviko'paraḥ
kālacakramiti khyātaṃ catuṣkāyātmakaṃ śivam
praṇipatya sarvabhāvena mañjuśrīcoditena ca
puṇḍarīkeṇa likhyate
lokeśenābjadhāriṇā
sādhanāpaṭale ṭīkā mayā nirmitakāyena iha śrīmati kalāpagrāmadakṣiṇamalayodyāne śrīkālacakra maṇḍala gṛhapūrvadvārāvasāne ratnamaṇḍape 'ratnasiṃhāsanastho mañjuśrībhagavān nirmitakāyo yaśonarendraḥ sūryarathādhyeṣitaḥ san paramādibuddhāt sādhanāpaṭale sucandrādhyeṣaṇaṃ buddhabhagavataḥ prativacanaṃ prathamavṛttena mahāparṣada: prakāśayati sma - labdhaḥ saptābhiṣeko jinajanaka mayā kumbhaguhyābhiṣeka: prajñājñānābhiṣeko bhavabhayamathano yogagamyaścaturthaḥ
bhūyaḥ pṛcchāmi samyag jinavarasahitaṃ sādhanaṃ viśvabhartuḥ saucandravākyaṃ gadati jinapatiḥ sādhanaṃ vajriṇaśca

śrutvā śrīkālacakrabhagavataḥ
vimalaprabhāyāṃ cakārāccākṣobhyāditathāgatānāṃ iha vṛtte padatrayeṇa sucandrādhyeṣaṇaṃ 'sādhanapaṭaladeśanāya
tataścaturthapa damārabhya yāvat paṭalaparisamāptistāvadbhagavataḥ prativacanamiti
idānīṃ śrutvā saucandravākyaṃ gadati jinapatiḥ śākyamunirbhagavān kālacakrasamādhisamāpannaḥ sādhanaṃ vajriṇaḥ vajradhātvīśvaryādidevīnāṃ śabdavajrādiviṣayadevīnāmuṣṇīṣādi 'mahā' krodha- rājānām atinīlādikrodhadevīnāṃ cacikādimātṝṇāṃ viṣṇvādidevānāṃ jayādināga- rājānāṃ śvānāsyādipracaṇḍānāṃ pratyekaṃ sādhanamanyeṣāmapi gadati jinapatilaukika siddhi- sādhanāyākaniṣṭhabhuvanaparyaṃntaṃ rūpa bhāvanayeti bhagavato niyamaḥ

vajrapāṇyādibodhisa ttvānāṃ idānīṃ kālaviśuddhayā bhagavato rūpakalpanoddeśa ucyate— candrāṅgaṃ yugmapādaṃ śikhigalamudadhi śrīmukhaṃ viśvavarṇa ṣaṭskandhaṃ sūryabāhuṃ jinakarakamalaṃ śūnyaṣaḍvahniparvam

evaṃ sūryacandra divāniśābhedenāhorātraṃ kāla ityucyate, tasya cakraṃ ṣaṭśatādhikaika- viṃśatisahasraśvāsātmaka dvādaśāṅgaṃ pratītyalakṣaṇaṃ rāśicakraṃ laukika saṃvṛtyotpāda- kṣayahetubhūtaṃ sarvasattvānām
tathā cāha- kāla: sṛjati bhūtāni kālaḥ saṃharate sadā
kālo hi bhagavān vajrī ahorātrasvarūpavān
iti
sthānarakṣāpāpadeśanādimahoddeśaḥ evamasya kālacakrasya sādhanamutpādakṣayavināśārthaṃ yogibhiḥ kartavyaṃ vakṣyamāṇa- krameṇeti rūpa kalpanāniyamaḥ

tata- ścaturlagnātmakaṃ vāmadakṣiṇamadhyakaṇṭhaṃ śikhigalamiti trikaṇṭham
evaṃ tritrilagnātmakaṃ pūrvadakṣiṇapaścimottara' vaktracatuṣkamudadhiriti
caturmukhaṃ viśvavaṇaṃ vakṣyamāṇamiti
evaṃ dvidvilagnātmakaṃ vāme dakṣiṇe ca pūrvāparaṃ madhyaskandha'ṣaṭskandhamiti
tathā pratyekamāsātmaka dvādaśabhujāstairbhujaiḥ sūryabāhumi ti
evaṃ pratyekārdha- lagnam
pakṣabhedena caturviṃśatikaraṃ jinakarakamalamiti
evaṃ ṣaṣṭiṣaṣṭipratyeka- śvāsātmakena dinabhedena ṣaṭyuttaratriśatāṅgulīparvaṃ pratyeka kare pañcāṅgulītriparvabhedena pañcadaśaparvāṇi caturviṃśatikareṣu ṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatrayaṃ bhavati
evaṃ śūnya ṣaḍvahniparvam
pādābhyāṃ mārarudramiti
skandhakleśamṛtyu devaputramāram, rāgadveṣamohamānātmakaṃ rudram,
śaśiravihutabhuṅmaṇḍale śrāsyamānam, lolayākrāntaṃ yena kālacakreṇa tameka- mabhavabhava samaṃ nirvāṇabhavaikalolībhūtaṃ nirāvaraṇataḥ
evaṃ sādhayet kālacakramiti bhagavato niyamaḥ
|| 2 ||
idānīmasya sādhanāya sthānānyucyante- udyāne parvate vā jinavarabhavane śūnyadevālaye ca siddhasthāne śmaśāne sarasi sunilaye guptabhūmyāṃ tathaiva
yasmiṃścittapratoṣo bhavati narapate sādhanaṃ tatra kuryāt kṛtvā pūrvoktarakṣāṃ khalu mṛduśayane cāsane copaviśya

udyāna ityādinā
iha laukika karmasādhanānurūpeṇa sthānaṃ bhavati

idānīṃ vaktra' śuddhayādi ' rucyate- - ādo hṛccandramadhye daśadiśi vividhān bhāvayettattva raśmīn kṛtvā vaktrādiśuddhi punarapi gagane sphāritānāṃ jinānām
kṛtvā pūjāṃ vicitrāṃ bahuvidhakaluṣaṃ sañcitaṃ deśayitvā kartavyaṃ sādhakena triśaraṇagamanaṃ kāyavākcittaśuddhayā
||4||
ādāvityādinā
iha yoginā pūrvoktarakṣā kartavyā 'māranirghā 'ṭanaṃ ca
tataḥ sādhanāpaṭaloktavidhinā devatārūpamātmānaṃ 'jhaṭityākāreṇa kṛtvā svahṛdaye paṃkārapariṇata- maṣṭadala raktapadmam, tadupari kaṇikāyām a~kārapariṇataṃ candramaṇḍalam tasya madhye tavamiti saṃvṛtyā hūṃkārajaṃ vajraṃ pañcaśūkam, tasya raśmīn vividhān pañcavarṇān bhāvaye- dyogo
pūrvaṃ kṛtvā vaktrāviśuddhi pañcāmṛta gulikayā mukhe prakṣiptayā vaktraśuddhirbhavati
tathā pūrvoktayā divyamudrayā śirasā rabhya yāvat pādāntaṃ tāvadātmānaṃ saṃsparśayet
evaṃ kāyaśuddhiḥ
evaṃ kṛtvā vaktrādiśuddhi tataścandramaṇḍale vajraraśmibhirgaganatale tathāgatān pratibodhya teṣāṃ sphāritānāṃ jinānāṃ pūjārthaṃ tān raśmīn punarākṛṣya svahṛdaye candravajrapraviṣṭān vibhāvya tataścandramaṇḍale dvādaśapūjādevīnāṃ bījākṣarāṇi dhyāyāt
kkhgghṅa kkhgghṅā cchjñña cchjhajñā ṭṭhūrṇa ṭṭhūrḍhṇā paphbbhma pphbbhmā tthdhna tthdhnā spṣś ka spaṣkā ityebhirbījākṣarainiṣpannā yathāsaṃkhyaṃ nṛtyā vādyā gandhā mālā dhūpā dīpā naivedyā akṣatā lāsyā hāsyā gītā kāmā ityādibhistathāgatānāṃ pūjāṃ kṛtvā'bhiṣekapaṭaloktavidhinā tato vakṣyamāṇakrameṇa bahuvidhakaluṣaṃ sañcitaṃ deśayitvā ādau tataḥ kartavyaṃ sādhakena triśaraṇagamanaṃ kāya- vākcitta śuddhayeti niyamaḥ
|| 4 ||
idānīṃ pāpadeśanāvasāne puṇyamanumodayet - saṃbuddherbodhisattve bahuvidhakuśalaṃ yatkṛtaṃ ranumode tatsamastaṃ vyapagatakaluṣo bodhicaryānurūḍhaḥ
buddhaṃ dharmaṃ ca saṃghaṃ bhavabhayaharaṇaṃ bodhisīmnaḥ prayāmi saṃbuddho'haṃ bhavāmi praṇidhimiti karomyatra sattvārthahetoḥ
||5||
saṃbuddha bodhisattvaṃ bahuvidhakuśalaṃ yatkṛtaṃ cāryasaṃghairanumode tat samastaṃ vyapagata- kaluṣo bodhicaryānurūḍho mantrī
tatastriśaraṇaṃ gacchati - buddhaṃ dharmaṃ ca saṃghaṃ bhavabhayaharaṇaṃ bodhisomnaḥ prayāmi

evaṃ vandanā pūjanā pāpadeśanā puṇyānumodanā tathāgatānā- madhyeṣaṇā yācanā puṇyapariṇāmaneti
evaṃ saptavidhāṃ pūjāṃ kṛtvā tatra trīṇi mūlāni smaret, bodhicittotpādaḥ, āśayaviśuddhiḥ, āśayaviśuddhiḥ, ahaṃkāramamakāraparityāgaḥ kartavyaḥ
tato daśa pāramitā' ścintayet
puṇyajñānaśīlasaṃbhārārthaṃ dānapāramitā
evaṃ 'śīla kṣāntivīryadhyānaprajñā- upāyapraṇidhibalajñānapāramitā vicintya tato brahma- vihārān smaret maitrīkaruṇāmuditopekṣāmiti

idānīṃ punarbhavagrahaṇāya śūnyatālakṣaṇamucyate- śūnyaṃ bhāvād vihīnaṃ sakalajagadidaṃ vasturūpasvabhāvaṃ tasmād buddho na bodhiḥ parahitakaruṇā cānimittapratijñā
evaṃ jñātvā samastaṃ tadapi narapate kāyavākcittavajraṃ dhyātavyaṃ bodhisattvaraparimitaguṇaṃ maṇḍale maṇḍaleśam
|| 6 ||
śūnyamityādinā
śūnyaṃ bhāvāda vihīnaṃ sakalajagadidaṃ vasturūpasvabhāvaṃ 'yattasmā- nmahāśūnyācca buddho na bodhiḥ parahitakaruṇā na
evaṃ cānimittapratijñā buddho bhaveyaṃ jagato hitāyeti
evaṃ jñātvā samastaṃ buddhatvāya
tadapi narapate kāyavākcittavajra dhyātavyaṃ bodhisattvairaparimitaguṇaṃ maṇḍale maṇḍaleśamiti

|| 6 ||

paṭale, ślo. ] utpattikrameṇa kāryaniṣpattimahoddeśaḥ
( ) utpattikrameṇa kāryaniṣpattimahoddeśaḥ
idānīṃ 'pūrvapraṇidhāna paripūraṇāya dharmacakrapravartanārthaṃ garbhāvakramaṇanyāyena bhagavata utpattikrameṇa sādhanamucyate-
śūnyaṃ vāyvagnitoyānyavanisuranagābjendusūryāgnayaśca kūṭāgāraṃ samantāt sphuradamalakaraṃ vajrajaṃ pañjaraṃ vā |
tanmadhye vajrabhūmau maṇikaranikarairmaṇḍalaṃ visphurantaṃ oṃkāraṃ jñānajātaṃ jinavarakamalaṃ candrasūryāsanānām || ||
śūnyamityādinā
iha bāhye adhyātmani utpattinimittamanantākāśadhātuḥ prajñā- dharmodayaṃ trikoṇam
bāhye vartulaṃ daśāravajramayaṃ martye mātṛśarīramadhyātmani tatra bāhye vāyumaṇḍalaṃ madhye dhanvākāraṃ tiryaṅmānena caturlakṣayojanaṃ ya~kārabījapariṇataṃ kṛṣṇamadha ūrdhvaṃ hū~kārapariṇataviśvavajradvayasahitaṃ dhvajāṅkitam
tadupari trilakṣayojanāyāmaṃ ra~kārapariṇataṃ trikoṇaṃ vahnimaṇḍalamadha ūrdhvaṃ hū~kārapariṇataṃ viśvavajradvayasahitaṃ raktaṃ svastikāṅkitam
tadupari toyamaṇḍalaṃ dvilakṣayojanaṃ ba~kārapariṇataṃ śuklamadha ūrdhvaṃ hū~kārapariṇataṃ viśvavajradvayasahitaṃ padmalāñchitaṃ vṛttam
tadupari la~kārapariṇataṃ pṛthvī- maṇḍalaṃ caturasraṃ pītavarṇamadha ūrdhvaṃ hūṃkārapariṇataviśvajradvayasahitaṃ vajralāñchitaṃ lakṣayojanam
tadupari ma~kārapariṇataṃ vajramayaṃ mahāmerumadho vistāreṇa ṣoḍaśasahasram ūrdhvaṃ pañcāśatsahasraṃ tanmadhye viśvābjaṃ merupramāṇārdhena śa~kārapariṇatam
tasya tribhāgikā kaṇikā tulyaṃ sārdhadvādaśasahasra yojanā yāmāt kṣaikārapariṇatā
tadupa- ri ha~kārapariṇataṃ candramaṇḍalaṃ kaṇikātulyam
tadupari visargapariṇataṃ sūryaṃ - maṇḍalam
tadupari agniriti rāhumaṇḍalaṃ nīlavarṇaṃ bindupariṇatam
evaṃ samastameka- lolībhūtaṃ ha kṣam lav r ya~ iti bījākṣaraṃ vibhāvya tato lokadhātuṃ niṣpannaṃ cintayediti bāhye

nābherguhyakamalaparyantaṃ mahāmeruḥ
guhyakamalaṃ bhagavataḥ kamalamiva

utpattikrameṇa kāryaniṣpattimahoddeśaḥ karālamiti kāryaniṣpattī kāya vijñānādhipaterlakṣaṇam
madhye maṇḍalakamala karṇikāyāṃ 'svaśarīrāntarbhūto devatāvinyāso maṇḍalākāra ucyate || 10 ||
śrīmatyoṅkārajāte jinapatikamale candrasūryāgnimūni rudrānaṅgadvayorhatsulalitacaraṇālīḍhapādaṃ jinendram
māro rakte ca savye varacaraṇatale śuklavāme ca rudro madhyaṃ savyāvasavyaṃ bhramararavinibhaṃ candravarṇaṃ ca kaṇṭham || 11 ||
śrīmati oṃkārajāte jinapatikamale hṛtkamale candrasūryāgnimūni lalanā- rasanā'vadhūtī mūrdhni rudrānayordvayormārakleśa pakṣayorvāmadakṣiṇapravā hayoḥ, hṛdi sulalitacaraṇālīḍhapādaṃ jinendramiti

tathā rudrastrinetra ekānanaścaturbhuja striśūlaḍamarukapālakhaṭvāṅgadharo vāmapādatale śukle śukla iti
evaṃ nīlāṅgaṃ tathā madhyakaṇṭhaṃ nīlaṃ dakṣiṇe ravinibhaṃ raktam
avasavyaṃ vāmaṃ candravarṇaṃ śuklamevaṃ trikaṇṭhaṃ pūrvoktavidhānāt
pūrvamukhaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ daṃṣṭrākarālogram
dakṣiṇaṃ sarāgaṃ raktaṃ vāmaṃ praśāntaṃ śuklam
paścimaṃ samādhisthaṃ pītam
jaṭāmukuṭe viśvavajram ardhacandraṃ vajrasattvamukuṭaṃ, vajramaṇivaca kuṇḍalavajrakaṇṭhikāvajrarucakavajramekhalā- vajranūpuravajrapaṭṭavajra mālāvyāghracarmāmbaradharam || 11 ||
skandhaṃ nīlaṃ ca raktaṃ śaśadharadhavalaṃ dakṣiṇe cottare ca dvau dvo savyāvasavye'sita ravivapuṣo bāhavaścandravarṇāḥ
tadvad vai tryaṣṭakena praharaṇasahitāḥ pāṇayaśca krameṇa pañcāṅa gulyastriparvāḥ śaśikarakamale pañcavarṇāḥ sphurantyaḥ ||12||
