yakṣa
parvāḥ ' vimalaprabhāyāṃ idānīmastravṛndamucyate— kṛṣṇe rakte ca śukle pravarakaratale saṃsthitaṃ cāstravṛndaṃ vajraṃ khaḍgastriśūlaṃ bhuvanabhayakarā kartikā vahnibāṇaḥ
tasmād vajrāṅakuśo vai saravaḍamaruko mudgaraścakrameva kunto daṇḍaḥ kuṭhāro ravikarakamale dakṣiṇe vajriṇaśca || 13 ||
kṛṣṇa ityādinā
kṛṣṇe karatalacatuṣke prathame vajram, dvitīye khaṅgaḥ, tṛtīye triśūlam, caturthe kartiketi
tathā rakte karatalacatuṣke prathame'gnibāṇaḥ, dvitīye vajrāṅkuśaḥ, tṛtīye 'raṇaḍḍamarukaḥ, caturthe mudgara iti
tṛtīye śukle karatalacatuṣke prathame karatale cakram, dvitīye kuntaḥ tṛtīye daṇḍaḥ
caturthe parśuriti dakṣiṇe'stra- vṛndam, ravikarakamale dakṣiṇe vajriṇaśceti || 13 ||
idānīṃ vāmakṛṣṇakara talacatuṣke cihnamucyate- ghaṇṭā kheṭaṃ ca gkhaṭvāṅgavikasitamukhaṃ raktapūrṇa kapālaṃ kodaṇḍaṃ pāśaratne kamalajalacarau darpaṇa: śṛṅkhalā ca
vedāsyaṃ brahmaṇo yacchira kamalamalaṃ vāmahaste jinasya kurvantyo dīnavaktraṃ dhṛtacaraṇatale mārarudrasvadevyau || 14 ||
prathame vajraghaṇṭā, dvitīye kheṭam, tṛtīye khaṭvāṅgaṃ vikasitamukham, caturthe raktapūrṇa kapālamiti
tathā rakte karatalacatuṣke prathame kodaṇḍam, dvitīye pāśaḥ,
tṛtīye maṇiratnam, caturthe śvetakamalamiti
tathā śukle karatalacatuṣke, prathamakaratale jalacara iti śaṅkhaḥ, dvitīye darpaṇaḥ, tṛtīye vajrabhṛṅkhalā, caturthe brahmaśira iti
evaṃ vedāsyaṃ brahmaṇo yacchirakamalamalaṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ vāmahaste jinasyeti
tatra mārarudra- saṃnidhāne kurvantyau dīnavaktraṃ dhūtacaraṇatale mārarudrayoḥ svavevyau ratirmārasya, umā rudrasyetyakṣobhyapraveśaḥ || 14 ||
idānīṃ viśvamātālakṣaṇamucyate- hemābhā vedavaktrā vasukarakamalāliṅgitā viśvamātā savye kartyaṅa kuśo vai sarabaḍamarukaścākṣasūtraṃ krameṇa
vāme śuktiśca pāśaḥ śatadalakamalaṃ divyaratnaṃ tathaiva pratyālīḍhākanetrā jinapatimukuṭā mudritā mudrikābhiḥ || 15 ||
ga. 'raṇat' nāsti
ga. ca. paraśu
ca. vāme
kha. cha. tale
ca. 'bhūṣaṇaṃ' nāsti
utpattikrameṇa kāryaniṣpattimahoddeśaḥ hemābhetyādinā
tatraikasamarasāccandraśukrasvabhāvena bhagavata utpādaḥ, sūryaraja:- svabhāvena devyā utpādaḥ
tatra śvetakṛṣṇa dharmā candraḥ, raktapīta dharmā sūryaḥ
tena hemābhā vedavaktrā caturmukhā vasukarakamalā aṣṭabhujāgrata āliṅgitā viśvamātā
tasyāḥ prathamakare dakṣiṇe, katikā, dvitīye'ṅkuśaḥ, tṛtīye saravaḍamarukaḥ, caturthe akṣasūtramiti
vāme prathamakare śuktiḥ, dvitīye pāśaḥ, tṛtīye śatadalakamalaṃ dhavalam, caturthe divyaratnaṃ tathaiva ca
evamālīḍho bhagavān pratyālīḍhā viśvamātā
arkanetrā dvādaśanetrā jinapati- mukuṭā vajrasattvamukuṭā
mudritā pañcamudrābhiḥ samāpattisthā || 15 ||
aṣṭau devyo'ṣṭapatre vasukarakamalā vedavaktrārkanetrāḥ koṇe tāsāṃ catasrastvahicamaradharā vaktrabhedejinasya
īśe nairṛtyakoṇe śikhini ca pavane dharmaśaṅkhaśca gaṇḍī evaṃ cintāmaṇiḥ syād bhavati khalu tathā kalpavṛkṣakrameṇa || 16 ||
tasyā viśvamāturjñānapāramitāyā antarbhāvitā aparāḥ pāramitā' dānādayaḥ pāramitā aṣṭapatre 'vaṣṭo devyaḥ
tā vasukarakamalā aṣṭabhujāḥ vedavaktrāścaturmukhāḥ, arkanetrā dvādaśalocanāḥ
tāsāṃ madhye catasro'hicamaradharā aṣṭabhujairaṣṭacamaradharāḥ
catuḥ koṇa vaktrabhedaijinasyeti
agnau kṛṣṇā, nairṛtye raktā, vāyavye pītā, īśāne śuklā evaṃ tāsāṃ pṛṣṭhata īśe dharmaśaṅkhaḥ śukla, nairṛtye dharmagaṇḍī raktā, śikhini cintāmaṇiḥ kṛṣṇaḥ, vāyavye kalpavṛkṣaḥ pīta iti
evaṃ pūrvapatre kṛṣṇā, dakṣiṇe raktā, uttare śvetā, paścime pītadīpteti
yathā bhagavato mukhabhedaścaturdikṣu bhedena tathā devīnāṃ viśvamātuḥ prathamaṃ mukhaṃ hemābham dakṣiṇaṃ śuklam, vāmaṃ raktam, paścimaṃ nīlam
evaṃ pītānāṃ devīnām
śuklānāṃ śuklaṃ pūrvam, dakṣiṇaṃ kṛṣṇam, paścimaṃ raktam, vāmaṃ pītam
raktānāṃ prathamaṃ raktam, dakṣiṇaṃ pītam, vāmaṃ nīlam, paścimaṃ śuklam
kṛṣṇānāṃ pūrvaṃ kṛṣṇam, vāmaṃ śuklam, paścimaṃ pītamiti
garbhapadmadevīnāṃ yathā bhagavatyā alaṅkārāḥ pañcamudrāstathā jñātavyā iti niyamaḥ || 16 ||
idānīṃ kṛṣṇadīptādīnāṃ savyavāma hasteṣu cihnānyucyante- kṛṣṇāyā dhūpapātraṃ prathamakaratale śītapātraṃ dvitīye piṣṭaṃ raktaṃ tṛtīye samadaśaśadharaṃ savyahaste caturthe
vimalaprabhāyāṃ vāme ghaṇṭā ca padmaṃ suratarukusumaṃ puṣpamālā krameṇa raktāyā dīpahārī samukuṭakaṭakaṃ dakṣiṇe vāmahaste || 17 ||
kṛṣṇetyādi
kṛṣṇāyā dhūpapātraṃ prathamakaratale, śītapātraṃ candanapātraṃ dvitīye, piṣṭaṃ raktaṃ kuṅkamapātraṃ tṛtīye, samadaśaśadharaṃ kastūrikāsahitaṃ karpūrapātraṃ caturthe
iti dakṣiṇakareṣu
vāme prathamahaste ghaṇṭā, dvitīye padmam, tṛtīye suratarukusumam, caturthe nānāpuṣpamālā krameṇeti pūrvapatre
raktadīptāyā: prathamahaste savye pradīpaḥ, dvitīye hāra:, tṛtīye 'mukuṭaḥ, caturthe kaṭakamiti savye || 17 ||
vastraṃ vai mekhalā ca sphuradamalakaraṃ kuṇḍalaṃ nūpuraṃ ca pītāyāḥ śaṅkhaveṇū samaṇiḍamarukaḥ savyahaste krameṇa
vāme vīṇā ca ḍhakkā praguṇaraṇaraṇatkaṃsikā kāhalā ca dugdhāmbvoṣadhyapānaṃ tvamṛtarasaphalaṃ bhaktapātraṃ sitāyāḥ || 18 ||
tathā vāmahaste prathame vastram, dvitīye mekhalā, tṛtīye ratnakaṇḍalam, caturthe nūpuramiti
tathā pītadīptāyāḥ savye prathamakare śaṅkhaḥ, dvitīye veṇuḥ, tṛtīye maṇiḥ, ḍamarukaścaturtha iti
vāme prathame vīṇā, dvitīye ḍhakkā, tṛtīye raṇatkaṃsikā', caturthe kāhalā ca krameṇeti
tathā śvetadīptāyāḥ, prathame haste dugdhapātram, dvitīye- 'mbupātram, tṛtīye divyauṣadhī, caturthe madyapātram
vāme prathamahaste amṛtapātram, dvitīye siddharasapātram, tṛtīye'mṛtaphalam, caturthe bhaktapātraṃ sitāyāḥ
iti vajra - satvaniṣpattiḥ svamudrāsahitā || 18 ||
dikpadmeṣvabdhibuddhāḥ khalu navanayanā vahnivaktrartu hastā: koṇe tārādidevyaḥ punarapi ca tayoraṣṭakakṣe'ṣṭakumbhāḥ
kṛṣṇā raktā ca pītā śaśadharadhavalā devatā devatī ca kṛṣṇā śvetendumūrdhni tvatha vidiśi gate raktapīte'rkamūrdhni || 19 ||
idānīṃ nāmarūpādyutpādāya mahārāgavaineyasaptaloka mādibuddhadeśanāyāṃ bhājana- mabhisaṃvīkṣya suratadhvaninā svakāye'kṣobhyādijinasamūhaṃ praveśyākāśādidhātusamūhaṃ utpattikrameṇa kāryaniṣpattimahoddeśaḥ 'taṃ puruṣa vidyā cakṣurādirūpādiviṣayasvabhāvena devatāsvarūpāvirbhūtaṃ punarapi svakāyānniścārya viśvamātari yathāvadantarbhāvayet
tatastathaiva niścārya punarapi maṇḍalacakrākārān tathāgatān sadhātūn svakāye praveśya candradravāpannānu svakuliśenotsṛjya svavidyākamalodare tatparāvṛttaṃ devatādevatīgaṇamaṇḍalamādhārādheya - lakṣaṇamakṣobhyādhipati dhyātvā pūrvaṃ bhagavataḥ kāye praveśayet
tato dikpadyeṣva- bdhibuddhāścatvāraḥ khalu navanayanā vahnivaktrāstrimukhā ṛtuhastāḥ ṣaḍbhujāḥ sphāraṇīyāḥ
tatra pūrvapadma sūryamūni amoghasiddhiḥ kṛṣṇaḥ dakṣiṇāvartena kṛṣṇarakta- sitavadanaḥ dakṣiṇe ratnasaṃbhavo raktavarṇo dakṣiṇāvartena raktasitakṛṣṇānanaḥ, uttare amitābha : śuklo dakṣiṇāva rtena sitakṛṣṇaraktānanaḥ, paścime vairocanaḥ pīto dakṣiṇāvartena pītasitakṛṣṇānana iti catvāraḥ sūryamaṇḍale
“dinastu bhagavān vajrī iti jñāpakāt
āgneyyāṃ tārā amoghasiddhivat, nairṛtye pāṇḍarā ratnasaṃbhavavat, īśāne māmakī amitābhavat, vāyavye locanā vairocanavat, iti catasraścandramaṇḍale
“naktaṃ prajñā prakīrtitā iti jñāpakāt
punarapi ca tayordevatā devyormadhye'ṣṭakakṣāsvaṣṭā mṛtakalaśāḥ
evaṃ kṛṣṇā raktā ca pītā śaśadharadhavalā devatā devatī ceti
pūrvadvāre'tibalaḥ krodho varṇamukhabhujato'moghasiddhivat kṛṣṇaḥ, dakṣiṇadvāre jambhako ratnasaṃbhavavad varṇamukhabhujato raktaḥ, paścimadvāre stambhako vairocanavad 'varṇamukhabhujataḥ pītaḥ, uttare mānako'mitābhavad varṇamukhabhujataḥ śuklaḥ, ūrdhvaṃ uṣṇīṣo'kṣobhyavad varṇamukhabhujataḥ śyāmaḥ
kintvete krodhā ālīḍhāḥ, krodhadevyaḥ pratyālīḍhā iti
caturthe māse nāmarūpotpādakāle rūpaṃ caturmahābhūtātmakaṃ vāyva- gnyudakapṛthvīdhātvātmakam
tena skandhadhātukrodhānāmutsargaḥ || 19 ||
tataḥ pañcame māse ṣaḍāyatanotpādakāle khagarbhādīnutsṛjet - vaigarbhādyāśca bhittau diśividiśigatāḥ sparśavatrādayaśca pūrve savye'vasavye paradiśikamale viśvabhadrastathaiva
śrīmān vai vajrapāṇiḥ khalu ravakuliśā dharmadhātuḥ krameṇa jambhaḥ stambhaśca mānastvatibala iti yo dvārapālaḥ sa pūrve || 20 ||
vaigarbhādyāśca bhittāviti
iha vāyujanyo ghrāṇo vegarbho'moghasiddhivat saṃsthānataḥ pūrvadvārasya savye prākārabhittau diśoti
vidiśigatāgneyakoṇe vāyujanyā sparśavatrā tārāvat saṃsthānataḥ
evaṃ dakṣiṇadvārasavye tejojanyaṃ cakṣuḥ kṣitigarbho ratnasaṃbhavavat saṃsthānataḥ
evaṃ tejodhātujanyā rasavatrā pāṇḍarāvannairṛtyakoṇe paścimadvārasavye
pṛthivījanyaṃ kāyendriyaṃ sarvanīvaraṇaviṣkambhī vairocanavat

adho 'jñānadhātujanyaṃ manaḥ samantabhadro nolavarṇastrimukhaḥ ṣaḍbhujo varṇataḥ kālacakravat pūrvadvārasya vāme prākārabhittau
evaṃ jñānadhātujanyā śabdavatrā uttaradvāravāme samantabhadravat saṃsthānataḥ
ūrdhve ākāśa- dhātujanyaṃ śrotraṃ vajrapāṇirakṣobhyavat śyāmo dakṣiṇadvāravāme
evamākāśadhātujanyā dharmadhātuvajrā paścimadvārasya vāme vajrapāṇivat saṃsthānataḥ
evaṃ ṣaḍāyatanaṃ pañcame māse “ṣaṣṭhe sparśaḥ iti jñāpakāt sparśādayo viṣayāḥ
evaṃ dvādaśāyatanotsarge dvitīya- mātrā kāyavajrasya
atra kṛṣṇā [:] śvetā devatādevatyaḥ pūrvottarā ūrdhvasthāścandra- maṇḍale deyāḥ, śukradharmitvāt khavāyūdakadhātūnām
atha vidiśigatāḥ koṇadevyaścandre naktaṃ prajñā prakīrtitā ( vi. pra. pū. 150 ) iti vacanāt
evaṃ raktā pītā dakṣiṇa- paścimā' devatādevyo'dhastācca, sūryamaṇḍale jñānatejaḥ pṛthvīdhātūnāṃ rajodharmatvāt
atha diśigatā devatā: sūrya: dinastu bhagavān vajrī ( vi. pra. pū. 150 ) iti vacanāt
evaṃ yo bhagavato'bhimukhaḥ sa nāyako dikṣu, vidikṣu devo nāyikā bhagavato'bhimukhīti
parāṅmukho'nunāyaka iti || 20 ||
prajñotsaṅge hyapāyaḥ śaśadharakamale devatānāṃ ca devī anyonyāliṅgitau dvau svakarasalilajaiḥ svasvacihnāṅkitaiśca
yaccihnaṃ yasya savye prathamakaratale sāsya mudrābjahīnā prajñopāyena cakraṃ paramasukhagataṃ padmavajrāsanāḍhyam ||21||
ataḥ prajñotsaṅge pāyo'nunāyakaḥ śaśadharakamale koṇabhāge vidikṣu
evaṃ devatānāmupāyānāmutsaṅge devī anunāyikā sūryamaṇḍale dikṣu
anyonyāliṅgitau dvau svakarasalilajaiḥ svasvacihnāṅkitairvakṣyamāṇairyaccihnaṃ yasya savye prathamakaratale sāsya mudrā'bjahonā
abjacihnaṃ vāme'pi dakṣiṇe'pi sādhāraṇaṃ ratnaṃ khaṅgaśceti
[ 260a] evaṃ pūrvāparaṃ vāmadakṣiṇaṃ adha ūrdhvaṃ prajñopāyena cakraṃ paramasukhagataṃ vajrāsanāḍhyam upāyanāyakam, padmāsanāḍhyaṃ devīgaṇa nāyakamiti niyamaḥ ||21||
idānīṃ cihnānyucyante- kṛṣṇānāṃ khaṅgakartyāṃ bhuvanabhayakaraṃ savyahaste triśūlaṃ vāme kheṭaṃ kapālaṃ bhavati karatale śvetakhaṭvāṅgameva
bāṇo vajrāṅkuśo vai saravaḍamarukaḥ savyahaste krameṇa vāme kodaṇḍapāśī sphuradamalamaṇilahitānāṃ tathaiva || 22 ||

tatra kṛṣṇānāṃ saṃskārakulajānāṃ prathame dakṣiṇa kare khaṅgaḥ, dvitīye katikā, tṛtīye triśūlam
vāme prathame kheṭam, dvitīye kapālam, tṛtīye karatale śvetakhaṭvāṅgam amoghasiddhitārā- atibalakhagarbhasparśavatrāṇāmiti
tathā lohitānāṃ savye prathama ' haste'gnibāṇaḥ, dvitīye vajrāṅkuśaḥ, tṛtīye saravaḍamarukaḥ
krameṇeti vāme prathame cāpam, dvitīye vajrapāśaḥ, tṛtīye sphuradamalamaṇī ratnam, navaśūkaṃ vedanākulajānāṃ ratnasaṃbhavapāṇḍarājambhakakṣiti- garbharasavajrāṇāmiti ||22||
pītānāṃ cakadaṇḍaṃ bhayakarakuliśaṃ savyahaste krameṇa śrīśaṅkhaḥ śṛṅkhalā vai bhavati ca saravā vajraghaṇṭā ca vāme
savye śrīmudgaro ve śaśadharadhavalānāṃ ca kuntastriśūlaṃ vāme śvetaṃ ca padmaṃ śatadalasahitaṃ darpaṇaṃ cākṣasūtram ||23||
evaṃ rūpakulajānāṃ savye prathamahaste cakram, dvitīye daṇḍaḥ, tṛtīye bhayakara- kuliśam
vāme prathame śaṅkhaḥ, dvitīye vajraśṛṅkhalā, tṛtīye saravā vajraghaṇṭā
evaṃ pītā 260bnāṃ vairocanalocanāstambhakasarvanīvaraṇaviṣkambhigandhavatrāṇāmiti
tathā saṃjñākulajānāṃ śvetānāṃ savye prathamahaste mudgaraḥ, dvitīye kuntaḥ tṛtīye triśūlam
vāme prathame śvetaṃ śatadalapadmam, dvitīye darpaṇam, tṛtīye makṣasūtram ami- tābhamāmakīpadmāntakalokeśvararūpavajrāṇāmiti || 23 ||
vajraṃ kartī kuṭhāraḥ prabhavati haritānāṃ ca savye krameṇa vāme ghaṇṭā kapālaṃ sakalaguṇanidhirbrahmavaktraṃ tadeva
nīlānāṃ veditavyaṃ prakṛtiguṇavaśād devatīnāṃ ca tadvat kṛṣṇā raktā ca śuklā drutakanakanibhāḥ pūrvabhūmyādidevyaḥ ||24||
tathā vijñānakulajānāṃ haritānāṃ prathame savye vastram, dvitīye kartī, tṛtīye paśuḥ
vāme prathame ghaṇṭā, dvitīye kapālam, tṛtīye brahmaśiraḥ, akṣobhyavajradhātvī- śvarī - uṣṇīvajrapāṇidharmadhātuvajrāṇāmiti
evaṃ nīlānāmapi jñānakulajānāṃ vajrasattva- viśvamātā sumbharāja samantabhadraśabdavatrāṇāmiti cihna niyamaḥ
athavā tārāyāḥ samantabhadrasyotpalaṃ vā khaṭvāṅgasthāne brahmaśiraḥsthāne ceti ||24||

vaiṣṇavyāḥ prathamapatrādau śrīḥ, māyā, kīrtiḥ, lakṣmī, vijayā, śrījayā, śrījayantī, śrīcakrī cāṣṭamā ve kamalavasudale vaiṣṇavī digpradeśe'gnau
tato vārāhyāḥ prathamapatrādau kaṅkālī, kālarātrī, prakupitavadanā, kālajihvā, karālī, kālī, ghorā, virūpā iti kamalavasudale śūkarī patradevī' dakṣiṇe ||30||

vāmahastadvaye sarveṣāṃ yathānukrameṇa cihnāni
kamalakaṇikāsthānāṃ teṣāṃ nairṛtyādīnāṃ kamaladaleṣvaṣṭāviśaleṣu dakṣiṇāvarteṣu triparimaṇḍaladaleṣu prathama- parimaṇḍale catvāri dalāni dvitīye'ṣṭau tṛtīye ṣoḍaśa
evamaṣṭāviṃśatidaleṣu caitrādimāsatithayaḥ

evaṃ caitratithayo nairṛtyasya kamaladale, vaiśākhatithayo vāyoḥ, jyeṣṭhatithayaḥ pāvakasya, āṣāḍhatithayaḥ ṣaṇmukhasya śrāvaṇatithayaḥ samudrasya, bhādratithayo vināyakasya, āśvinatithaya indrasya, kārtikatithayo brahmaṇaḥ, mārgatithayo harasya, pauṣatithayo yakṣasya, māghatithayo viṣṇoḥ, phālgunatithayo yamasya
tadvadvarṇāyudha saṃsthānena devyaḥ

paramaśaśikalā veditavyāstā abdayogāditi
āsāṃ vakṣyamāṇa- bījenādibhūtena vajrāntaṃ nāma bhavatīti niyamaḥ
idānīṃ hastapādataloṣṇīṣagudanāḍī- spharaṇaśuddhayā dvāre devyo rathasthā mārīcyādyā ekavaktrāścaturbhujāḥ

vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ sādhanā- evaṃ nityaṃ mahāśūnyaṃ dṛṣṭvā vicārya vā'nityaṃ paramāṇusamāgamaṃ ca viyogaṃ ca 'teṣāṃ dṛṣṭvobhayoḥ sādhanaṃ na syād buddhatvāya |

idānīmādhārādheya ucyate- śrīvatrī cittadatraṃ bhavati narapate maṇḍalaṃ kāyavajraṃ vāgvajraṃ devatānāmalikalikulajaṃ candramindvarkamūrdhni |
kandaṃ nālaṃ tvakāro dalamapi ca tathā keśarāṇyapyukāro madhye śrīkaṇikā ca dvividhapathagatau candrasūryau makāraḥ || 49 ||
śrītyādinā |
kāyavākcittamaṇḍale śrīvatrī nāyakaścittavatraṃ bhavati, narapate !

evaṃ pañcajñānātmakaṃ kālacakraṃ bhagavantaṃ vajrasattvamukuṭinaṃ dhyātvā suviśuddhadharmadhātvātmakam, tataḥ ṣaḍgatisthān sarvasattvānākṛṣya tasminneva maṇḍale praviṣṭān vibhāvya tato vairocanādīṃstathāgatān svahṛdaye praveśya sadhātūn bodhicitta - dravāpannān svaguhyakuliśenotsṛjya tena bodhicittena tān sarvasattvānabhiṣiktān
dhyāyāt
tatastān bodhicittaraśmibhiḥ spṛṣṭān sarvasattvān trimukhān nānāmukhān nānāvarṇān devatāsvarūpān prajñopāyātmakān paramānanda sukhapūrṇān bhāvayet

cakārāt prajñayā sādhaṃ vīkṣya, adhyātmani pañcamaṇḍalavāhārthaṃ sarvabāhyaviṣayopabhogārthaṃ bāhye devatāniṣpattī sakalajagadarthakaraṇāya maitrīkaruṇāmuditopekṣāsvabhāvinyastārāmāmakīpāṇḍarālocanāgītaṃ kurvanti devyaḥ
tvamapi bhagavan sarvasattvopakārī asmān rakṣāhi vajrin tridaśanaraguro kāmakāmāthi - nīśceti tārā maitrīrūpeṇa cittavatraṃ codayati, māmakī karuṇārūpeṇa kāyavajram, pāṇḍarā muditāmūrtyā vāgvajram, locanā upekṣāmūrtyā jñānavatraṃ codayati

atra maitrīkaruṇā muditopekṣāsvabhāvinya- stārāmāmakīpāṇḍarālocanā devyaścodayanti pañcamaṇḍalavāhārthaṃ bālānāṃ bhagavato jagadarthāyeti devī vajragītikācodanāniyamaḥ

evaṃ samasukhaphala de nābhiguhyādikamale mūrcchāgataṃ vijñānaṃ prabodhayanti bālānā- miva devatā kāyasthamiti nītārthaḥ || 50 ||
idānīṃ devatotthānamucyate- gītaṃ śrutvā sa vajrī tribhuvanasakalaṃ tvindrajālopamaṃ vai dṛṣṭvotpattiṃ karoti sphuradamalakaraṃ sphārayitvā svacihnam
vajrālaṅkārayukto jinapatimukuṭaḥ prajñayāliṅgitaśca prajñopāyena rājan punarapi sakalaṃ maṇḍalotsarjanaṃ ca ||51||
gītaṃ śrutvetyādinā |
atra śūnyatāyāṃ praviṣṭo bhagavān gītikābhiḥ prabodhitaḥ māyopamaṃ sakalaṃ jagad dṛṣṭvā sattvārthāya bhūya utpatti karoti sphuradamalakaraṃ sphāra- pitvā svacihnaṃ pañcaśukavatraṃ hū~kārapariṇatam |
tena vajrālaṅkārayukto jinapatimukuṭo'- kṣobhyamukuṭaḥ prajñāliṅgito ve śūnyamaṇḍalavāhinyā vajradhātvośvaryā viśvamātrā |
tadūhṛdaye bālānāmiva prajñopāyena |
rājaniti sambodhanam |
prajñopāya samāpatyā gaganasthān skandhadhātvāyatanādisvabhāvena samayamaṇḍalārthaṃ buddhān pañcamaṇḍala svabhāvān svakāye praveśya prajñāpadme pratyekākṣara' mantrasvabhāvān tataḥ padmādutsṛjet pūrvavat vaktrabhuja- cihnasaṃsthānalakṣaṇān jñānacittavākkāya maṇḍaleṣu |
evaṃ maṇḍalotsarjanaṃ ca |
cakārāt pūrvavaditi |
bālasya garbhānnirgama 'kāle prāṇādivāyūnāṃ daśānāmutpādaḥ |
śiṣyāṇāṃ maṇḍala praveśakāle puṣpakṣepaḥ |
nagno jarāyucarmāmbaradharo bāla iti viśuddhayā devatāyāḥ tata indriyaprabodho jñānasattvapraveśo bālasya yathā tathā devatāyāṃ bhāvanīyā' yogibhiviṃśatyākārasaṃbodhilakṣaṇā |
evaṃ pañcākārābhisaṃbodhau sevāṅgaṃ kāyaniṣpattau, viṃśatyā kārasaṃbodhāvupasādhanaṃ vāniṣpattau evamutpattikramo dvidhā - eko jarāyujaḥ, dvitīyo'ṇḍajaḥ |
yo'ṇḍajaḥ sa lokadhātūtpādaḥ |
brahmāṇḍajamiti bhāṣayā |
yo jarāyujaḥ sa manuṣyotpādaḥ |
yo jhaṭitaḥ sa upapādukotpādaḥ |
' sattvānāṃ sa ekotpattikrama: |
jhaṭitākāreṇa sattvāśayenokto bhagavatā utpannakramaḥ punaḥ kalpanā- rahitaḥ |
gaganodbhavaḥ svayaṃbhūḥ prajñājñānānalo mahān |
vairocano mahādīptirjñānajyortivirocanaḥ ||
ityādipañcākārābhisambodhinā'vagantavyaḥ || 51 ||
idānīṃ jñānacakrākarṣaṇamucyate- nīlābhaṃ bhīmakāyaṃ prahasitavadanaṃ cārdhadaṃṣṭrākarālaṃ garjantaṃ sūryaṃ netraṃ dvayadhikajinakaraṃ vedavaktraṃ dvipādam |
prajñopāyo dbhavantaṃ praharaṇasahitaṃ preṣayed vajravegaṃ prajñopāyo dbhavantaṃ aṣṭāṅghrisyandanasthaṃ jinaripumathanaṃ jñānacakrasya hetoḥ || 52 ||
nīlābhamityādinā |
iha jātabālasya madhyamāprāṇanirgamo nīlābhaḥ, tasya nirgame- ne (ṇe) ndriyāṇāṃ prabodho bāhyaviṣayāṇāṃ ṣaḍvijñānānāṃ pravṛttiradhyātmani |
ato bāhya- devatāniṣpattau nīlābhaṃ prāṇaṃ madhyamāviśuddhayā vajjravegaṃ bhomakāyaṃ prahasitavadanaṃ cārdha- vaṃṣṭrākarālaṃ garjantaṃ dvādaśanetraṃ dvayadhikajinakaraṃ ṣaḍviṃśatibhujaṃ |
caturmukhaṃ dvicaraṇaṃ prajñopāyo dbhavantaṃ bhartṛvat praharaṇasahitaṃ gajacarmāmbaradharaṃ pañcamudrāvibhūṣitam akṣobhya- mukuṭinaṃ kapāla muṇḍamālādhāriṇaṃ sarpābharaṇaṃ hū~kāravajranirmāṇaṃ taṃ vajravegaṃ preṣayed ālayavijñānaṃ pravṛttivijñānamiti |
aṣṭāṅghrisyandanasthamiti |
śabdasparśarasa rūpagandha- sattvarajastamoguṇasthaṃ jinaripumathanaṃ mārakleśamathanam |
jñānacakrasya hetoḥ pañcaviṣaya- jñānanivṛttaya iti || 52 ||
nābhī hatvāṅkuśena sphuradamalakaraṃ jñānacakreśvaraṃ vai hasteṣvevaṃ prabaddhvā sakuliśaphaṇinā bhīṣayitvā svaśastraḥ |
sādhyaṃ kṛtvā samastaṃ vrajati punarasau cālayitvā sucakraṃ veśaṃ bandhaṃ ca toṣaṃ samarasakaraṇaṃ jambhakādiḥ karoti ||53||
tato nābhikamalāda 'bāhyanirgataḥ prāṇo bāhyabhāvānākṛṣya punanivartate |
'ato viśuddhayā hatvā nābhī vajrāṅkuśena sphuradamalakara jñānacakreśvaraṃ ve hasteṣu caturviṃśatiṣu bandhayitvā sakuliśa nāgapāśairbhīṣayitvā svaśastraiḥ |
evaṃ jñānacakraṃ sādhyaṃ kṛtvā vrajati punaḥ svasthānaṃ cālayitvā samastaṃ bhāvalakṣaṇaṃ viśvacakramiti |
tato veśaṃ jambhakaḥ karoti cakṣurindriyajanitamālayavijñānam |
bandhaṃ kāyendriyajanitaṃ kāyavijñānaṃ stambhakaḥ karoti |
toṣaṃ jihvendriyajanitaṃ jihvāvijñānaṃ mānakaḥ karoti |
samarasa- karaṇaṃ ghrāṇendriyajanitaṃ ghrāṇavijñānamatibalaḥ karoti |
evaṃ pañcaprakāraṃ jaḥkāreṇākṛṣṭam, hū~kāreṇa praviṣṭam, vaṃkāreṇa baddham, ho: kāreṇa toṣitam, hī: kāreṇa samarasīkṛtam |
vajrāṅkuśena vajreṇa vajrapāśena vajraghaṇṭayā vajradaṇḍeneti |
evaṃ jñānacakraṃ sampūjya pūrvavat samayacakraṃ samarasībhūtaṃ bhāvayediti niyamaḥ || 53 ||
mucyate- idānīmutpattikrameṇa pratyekasthāne jñānadevatānāṃ samayadevatābhiḥ sārdhamekatva- cittaṃ niṣpattiyoge bhavati sagaganaṃ garbhapadye'gnimūrdhni raktadīptā |
pūrve śrīkṛṣṇadīptā varakamaladale dakṣiṇe vāme śrīśvetadīptā bhavati kulavaśāt paścime pītadīptā dhūmāgneyyāṃ marīcidanujadiśi tathā dyotakeśapradeśe || 54 ||
'cittamityādinā |
iha niṣpattiyoge utpattikrame bhavati sagaganaṃ vajradhātvī- śvaryā sārdhaṃ vijñānaṃ 'garbhapadma mahāsukhe agnimūni candrasūrya rāhukālāgnimaṇḍalopari vimalaprabhāyāṃ samayasattvena jñānasattvasya samarasatvam |
evaṃ pūrvapatre kṛṣṇadīptā samarasā |
varakamala- dale dakṣiṇe raktadoptā, uttare śvetadoptā, paścime potadīptā bhavati kula- vaśād jñāna 'cakravaśāt |
evamāgneyyāṃ dhūmā |
nairṛtye marīciḥ |
khadyakota īśe || 54 ||
vāyavyāṃ śrīpradīpā maṇirapi ca tarurdharmagaṇḍī ca śaṅkho vahnau vāyau ca daitye haradiśi ca tathābhyantare koṇabhāge |
pūrve saṃskārapṛthvī khalu kamalagatau dakṣiṇe vedanāmbho vāme saṃjñā ca vahnirbhavati sa pavanaṃ rūpamevāpare ca ||55||
vāyavyāṃ pradīpā samarasā iti |
evamagnikoṇe cintāmaṇiḥ, nairṛtye dharmagaṇḍī, īśāne dharmaśaṅkhaḥ, vāyavye kalpavṛkṣa iti |
vajrāvalīpadmadalayo: koṇa- bhā[ ge'bhyantare |
evaṃ pūrvakamalāsane kaṇikāyāṃ sūrye saṃskārapṛthivyo dvo samarasau samayasattvābhyāṃ saha |
dakṣiṇe vedanā toyadhātuḥ, uttare saṃjñā tejodhātuḥ, paścime sapavanaṃ rūpaṃ samarasaṃ bhavatīti |
atropāyā nāyakāḥ || 55 ||
āgneyyāṃ vāyurūpe bhavati danupato vahnisaṃjñā dvayaṃ ca īśe'mbho vedanā vai maruti ca dharaṇī skandhasaṃskārayuktā |
devī buddhāntarāleṣvamṛtarasaghaṭāścāṣṭakakṣapradeśe ghrāṇo gandhaśca pūrve punaraparapuṭe dakṣiṇe netrarūpe || 56 ||
evamāgneyyāṃ vāyurūpe samarase bhavataḥ, nairṛtye tejodhātusaṃjñe, īśāne toyadhātu- vedane, vāyavye pṛthivīdhātusaṃskārau samarasāviti koṇadevyo nāyikyaḥ |
evaṃ devībuddhānāmantarāleṣvamṛtarasaghaṭā aṣṭakakṣapradeśe samarasā bhavantīti niyamaḥ |
tathāgatapuṭe tato bodhisattvapuḍhe ghrāṇo gandhaśca pūrvakamale samarasaḥ |
dakṣiṇe netraṃ rūpaviṣayaḥ || 56 ||
vāme jihvārasaḥ syād bhavati hi varuṇe sparśa kāyastathaiva pātāle śabdakarṇī bhavati kulavaśād dakṣiṇe dvāravāme |
cittaṃ ve dharmadhāturbhavati sagaganaṃ sarvato dvāravāme ādau copāyaṣaṭkaṃ bhavati jinavaśānmaṇḍalasyādhidevam ||57||
paṭale, ślo. ] prāṇadevatotpādamahoddeśaḥ uttare jihvārasaḥ, varuṇe 'sparśakāyendriyam, pātāle śabdakarṇī, dakṣiṇadvāra- pūrve vāme cittaṃ vai dharmadhāturbhavati sagaganaṃ pūrvadvārasya vāme |
evamādau copāyaṣaṭkaṃ bhavati |
tathāgatakulavaśād maṇḍalasyādhidaivam ||57||
paścāt prajñādiṣaṭkaṃ prakaṭamadhipatiḥ svasvapadmāsane ca pūrvaṃdvāre pracaṇḍastvasidhṛgatibala: stambhakī tasya mudrā |
savye jambhaśca mānī bhavati ca dhanade mānako jambhakī ca stambhaścānantavīryā bhavati ca varuṇe dvāramadhyasthapadmaṃ ||58||
paścāt prajñādiṣaṭkaṃ dharmadhātvādikamiti |
atrāgneyyāṃ sparśavajrā kāye- ndriyam,
pātāle[ śabdavajñā śrotram |
uttaradvāra' vāme dharmadhātuḥ, manaḥ paścimadvāravāme iti dvādaśāyatanāni |
bodhisattvapuṭe samarasīkaraṇaṃ jñānasattvasya svasvapadmāsane candra- mūrdhni |
idānīṃ krodharājānāṃ samarasatvamucyate - pūrvaṃdvāre pracaṇḍastva sidhūgatibalaḥ stambhako tasya mudrā, savyadvāre jambhakaśca mānī mudrā, uttaradvāre mānako jambhakī mudrā |
paścimadvāre ' stambho'nantavīryā tasya mudrā bhavati samarasā |
dvāramadhyasthapadma iti cittamaṇḍale sama rasakaraṇam |
nābhicakre hṛtkamale jātakasyeti niyamaḥ ||58||
mārīcī nīladaṇḍo'cala iti bhṛkuṭī śṛṅkhalā'nantavīrya- ṣṭakki cundārathasthā suradhanadapare dakṣiṇe dvāramadhye |
sumbho raudrekṣaṇādho bhavati nabhasi coṣṇīṣa evātinīlā pūrvadvārā parordhvo bhavati ca niyataṃ syandanaśca dvayośca || 59 ||

13. śrīvatrī viśvabhadro bhavati kulavaśād vajradhṛg vajrapāṇi- 14. bhūmigarbhaśca samyak
15. garbho'moghasiddhirvimalamaṇikaro 16. viṣkambhī vajrapāṇirbhavati kulavaśāllokanātho'mitābhaḥ 17. śrīmātā dharmadhātustvapi varakuliśā vajradhātvīśvarī ca || 86 ||
18. śrītārā sparśavatrā khalu rasakuliśā pāṇḍarā jātibhedād 19. rūpākhyā māmakī vai bhavati kulavaśāllocanā gandhavajrā
20. krodhendro vajravego bhavati jinapativiśvabhadraḥ sa eva 21. uṣṇīṣo'kṣobhya evātra punaratibalo'moghasiddhiḥ prasiddhaḥ ||87 ||
22. jambho vai ratnapāṇirbhavati kulavaśānmānakaścāmitābhaḥ 23. stambho vairocanaśca prabhavati balavān vajrapāṇiśca sumbhaḥ
24. vegarbho nīladaṇḍaḥ prakṛtiguṇavaśād bhūmigarbhaśca ṭakki - 25. viṣkambhī cātivīryo bhavati kulavaśāllokanātho'calaśca ||88 ||

28. jambhī mānī krameṇa prakaṭitaniyatā stambhakī ca prasiddhā 29. raudrākṣī śabdavatrā bhavati kulavaśācchṛṅkhalā sparśavatrā || 89 ||
30. mārīcī gandhavatrā prabhavati bhṛkuṭī caiva cundā prasiddhā 31. jñātavyā jātibhedāt khalu rasakuliśā rūpavajrā narendra
32. stambhaḥ kālāntako'traiva punaratibalo vighnaśatruḥ prasiddho 33. jambha: prajñāntako vai prabhavati ca tathā mānaka: padmaśatruḥ || 90 ||
34. cāmuṇḍā śūkarīśā marudanala jalakṣmāstathendrī caturthī 35. gandho rūpaṃ rasaḥ sparśa iti calahare daityavahnau sthitāśca
36. brahmā vairocano vai bhavati kulavaśāt sāgaraścāmitābho 37. vahniḥ śrīratnapāṇirbhavati hi pavano'moghasiddhistathaiva || 91 || 52. vimalaprabhāyāṃ 53. akṣobhyo daityaśatrurbhavati jinapatiḥ śaṅkaraśca prasiddho 54. viṣkambhī yaḥ sa śakro bhavati gaṇapatirlokanāthastathaiva
38. bhūgarbhaḥ ṣaṇmukhaḥ syād bhavati danupatiḥ śrīkhagarbhaḥ prasiddha: 56. kāla: śrīvajrapāṇirbhavati danupativiśvabhadraśca ṣaṣṭhaḥ || 92 ||
39. ṣaḍ vidyāḥ ṣaṭ na vajrāḥ prakṛtiguṇavaśāt svasvamudrāśca teṣāṃ 58. mudrā vidyādayo'rkāḥ suravarapatayaḥ śvetakṛṣṇāśca pūrṇāḥ
40. teṣāṃ yāḥ padmapatre va karatithayo māghamāsādayastāḥ 60. ṣaṇmāse pūrvaṣaṭkaṃ bhavati kulavaśāccāparaṃ devatīnām ||93||

42. khadyotolūkavaktrā khagapativadanā śrīpradīpā prasiddhā 64. śvānāsyā kṛṣṇadīptā suvikṛtavadanā śūkarāsyātidīptā || 14 ||
43. vyāghrāsyā śvetadīptā bhavati kulavaśājjambukī pītadīptā 66. evaṃ lāsyādisarvā: prakaṭadaśavidhā viśvamātāvaśeṣāḥ
idānīṃ janyajanakādisambandhaḥ śrīvatrī viśvabhadraḥ ( ) ityādisārdhanava- 68. vṛttāni viśvamātāvaśeṣā : ( ) iti paryantaṃ kulakulīnayoḥ sambandhaḥ ||| 86 - 943 ||
idānīṃ vatuḥ kāyapariśuddhirucyate - 70. divyā buddhāśca vidyāḥ satarusakalaśāḥ śuddhakāyo jinasya kāyamaṇḍale pravararathagatā 'mārīcyādayaḥ sūryadevāḥ prasiddhāḥ
nairṛtyādayo dvādaśa
aṣṭau nāgāḥ karkoṭakādayaḥ
pracaṇḍāḥ śvānāsyādayaḥ
ete devādayaḥ parijana- sahitāḥ
padmadale devatābhiḥ saha buddhanirmāṇakāyaḥ
evaṃ bhūyo dvibhedo bhavati jinatanurbāhyato'bhyantare ca
garbhotpattiryathaiva prabhavati niyatā maṇḍale tadvadeveti || 96 || śāstā divyādikumbhāḥ sahajajinatanurmaṇḍale garbhamadhye buddhādyā dharmakāyaḥ khalu rasakuliśādyāśca saṃbhogakāyaḥ
krodhā nirmāṇakāyo bhavati kulavaśānmaṇḍale garbhasaṃsthā- icāmuṇḍādyaṣṭadevyaḥ parijanasahitāḥ śuddhakāyo hi bāhye ||17|| iha garbhe yathā bālasya vijñānaṃ jñānaṃ śukrarajogarbhe śuddhakāya:, tathā śāstā bhagavān
divyā dhūmādayaḥ, ādiśabdena dharmaśaṅkhādayo'ṣṭakumbhā ete maṇḍalagarbha sahajakā iti
tathā bālasya skandhadhātūdbhavo dharmakāyastathā maṇḍale buddhācā iti
tato yathā bālasyāyatanodbhavaḥ saṃbhogakāyastathā maṇḍale'pi
khalu śabda- vajrādaya iti
tato yathā bālasya hastapādādikeśādisaṃbhavaḥ prasavana samayaśca nirmāṇakāya:, tathā yamāntakādayaścatuḥ krodhā iti kulavaśāmmaṇḍale garbha saṃsthā iti cittamaṇḍale cittakulavaśāditi garbhe caturdhā niyamaḥ
idānīṃ bāhye caturdhā ucyate - cāmuṇḍetyādi
iha yathotpannasya bālasya 'nābhicakrāt prāṇanirgamaḥ sahajakāyastathā bāhye vāṅmaṇḍale cāmuṇḍādyaṣṭadevyaḥ parijanasahitāścatuḥṣaṣṭi- yoginībhiḥ sahitā iti || 97 || vimalaprabhāyāṃ devādyā dharmaṃkāyaḥ sakalaphaṇikulaṃ cātra saṃbhogakāya- icaṇḍā nirmāṇakāyo bhavati narapate sarvasattvārthahetoḥ
yugmaṃ syāt kāyavajraṃ sahaja jinatanubimba niṣpattiheto- rvāgvajraṃ dharmakāyo bhavati ca yugalaṃ dharmatādeśanārtham ||98|| iha yathā bālasya hastapādādi saṃkucanamasphuṭavacanaṃ dharmakāyastathā devā dvādaśa
ādiśabdena rathasthāḥ ṣaḍ devya iti
iha yathā bālasya dantotthāne sphuṭavacane sati 'saṃbhoga kāyastathā maṇḍale sakala phaṇikulamiti
iha yathā bālasya dantapātāt punarutthānādāmaraṇāvadhau nirmāṇakāyastathā maṇḍale caṇḍā śvānāsyādayaḥ parijana- sahitāḥ sārdhatrikoṭibhūtaiḥ sahitā nirmāṇakāyo bhavati, narapate sarvasattvārthahetoriti 'bāhye catuḥ kāryāviśuddhiniyamaḥ
idānīṃ catuḥkāyacaturvajrāṇāṃ parasparaṃ yoga ucyate- yugmamityādi
iha bālasya bimbaniṣpattihetoḥ sahajakāyaścaturbhūtātmakaṃ kāyavajraṃ yugmaṃ syād yathā, tathā maṇḍale'pi || 98 || cittaṃ saṃbhogakāyo yugalamapi bhavet sarvasattvārthakartā jñānaṃ nirmāṇakāyo bhavati hi yugalaṃ prāṇināṃ mokṣadaṃ ve
prajñopāyāṅgabhāvaḥ samaviṣamakulairyoginā veditavyaṃ candrādityādikādyaistrividhabhavagatairjñānavijñānabhedaiḥ ||99|| evaṃ vāgvajraṃ dharmakāyo bālasya yugalamapi jalpanārthaṃ yathā tathā dharmadeśanārthaṃ maṇḍale'pi
iha bālasya bodhicittaṃ saṃbhogakāyaḥ sarvasattvārtha' kartā yugalam, tathā maṇḍale'pi
iha yathā bālasya cyavanakāle jñānamiti sukhaṃ nirmāṇakāya iti paripūrṇa- dhātutvaṃ ṣoḍaśavarṣāvadherbhavati hi yugalaṃ prāṇināṃ mokṣadaṃ vaṃ
mokṣo'tra bodhicittabindūnāṃ cyutikṣaṇaḥ
tad dadātoti mokṣadaṃ yugalaṃ jñānaṃ nirmāṇakāyalakṣaṇaṃ prāṇinām
tathā 'tadvaidharmyeṇa maṇḍale mokṣadaṃ dvādaśāṅgahetuphala ' nirodhata iti buddhaniyamaḥ
punareṣāṃ
catuḥkāyacaturvāṇāṃ prajñopāyāṅgabhāvaḥ samaviṣamakulairiti
samakule rajaudbhavadhātu- kulaiḥ, viṣamakulaiḥ śukrodbhūtadhātukulaiḥ

• prāṇadeva totpādamahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ kāyavākcittādhiṣṭhānamucyate- vajraḥ svāhānuyuktaḥ śirasi galahṛdornābhiguhye ca mūrdhni etaiścādhiṣṭhitāṅgaṃ paramajinapati snāpayed devatībhiḥ

vajrerityādi
yathotpannasya bālasya kāyavākcittādhiṣṭhānaṃ jāgratsvapnasuṣupta- lakṣaṇaṃ bhavati, lalāṭe kaṇṭhe hṛdaye nābhau guhye uṣṇīṣe oṃ āḥ hūṃ ho svāhā
etaiścā- dhiṣṭhitāṅgaṃ bālaṃ yathā snāpayanti mātaraḥ, tathā paramajinapata snāpayed devatobhi- yoginībhiḥ
atrādhiṣṭhāne lalāṭe akārapariṇataṃ candramaṇḍalam, tadupari oṅkāra- pariṇatamaṣṭāracakram, tatpariṇataṃ kāyavajraṃ śuklavarṇaṃ trimukhaṃ mūlaṃ śuklaṃ vāmaṃ raktaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ ṣaḍbhujaṃ dakṣiṇe cakravajrapadmadharaṃ vāme khaḍgaghaṇṭāmaṇidharaṃ, saprajñaṃ niṣpādya tato lalāṭānniścārya tenākāśadhātuṃ samantāt paripūrṇa vibhāvya kāyavajravaineyānāṃ sattvānāṃ dharmadeśanāṃ kṛtvā punarāgatyātmanaḥ purataḥ saṃsthāpyābhiṣekamanunāthayet
abhiṣiñcantu māṃ kāyavajradharā iti
tato'mṛtakalaśaiḥ kāyakuladevyo'bhiṣiñcayanti
tato'bhiṣeke sati adhiṣṭhānaṃ kārayet svalalāṭe candramaṇḍale kāyavatraṃ praveśyedamudīrayet - kāyavajradharaḥ śrīmān trivajrābhedyabhāvitaḥ
adhiṣṭānapadaṃ me'dya karotu kāyavajriṇaḥ ||
daśadiksaṃsthitā buddhāstrivajrābhedya bhāvitāḥ
adhiṣṭhānapadaṃ me'dya kurvantu kāyavajriṇaḥ ||
iti kāyādhiṣṭhānam
evaṃ kaṇṭhe rephapariṇataṃ sūryamaṇḍalam, tadupari āḥkārapariṇataṃ raktapadmamaṣṭadalaṃ tatpariṇataṃ vāgvajraṃ saprajñaṃ raktaṃ raktasitakṛṣṇavadanaṃ, dakṣiṇe padmavajracakradharaṃ vāme maṇighaṇṭākhaṅgadharaṃ niścāryākāśadhātuṃ tena paripūrṇa vibhāvya vāgvajravaineyānāṃ sattvānāṃ dharmadeśanāṃ kṛtvā punarātmano'grataḥ saṃsthāpyā'bhiṣeka manunāthayet - abhiṣiñcantu māṃ vāgvajriṇaḥ
tato vākkula 'devībhiramṛtaghaṭairabhiṣiñcyamānamātmānaṃ vibhāvya tato vāgadhiṣṭhānaṃ kuryāt, vāgvajraṃ sūryamaṇḍale viniveśya idamudīrayet - vāgvajradharaḥ śrīmān trivajrābhedyabhāvitaḥ
adhiṣṭhānapadaṃ me'dya karotu vāgvajriṇaḥ ||
dadādiksaṃsthitā buddhāstrivastrābhedya bhāvitāḥ
adhiṣṭhānapadaṃ me'dya kurvantu vāgvajriṇaḥ ||

[ sādhanā- cittādhiṣṭhāne hṛdaye rāhumaṇḍalaṃ nīlavarṇaṃ vibhāvya bindupariṇataṃ tadupari hu~kāra- pariṇataṃ vajraṃ pañcaśukaṃ tatpariṇataṃ cittavatraṃ saprajñaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ kṛṣṇa sitaraktavadanaṃ, dakṣiṇe vajracakrapadmadharaṃ, vāme ghaṇṭā maṇikhaḍgadharaṃ niścāryākāśadhātuṃ tena paripūrṇaṃ vibhāvya cittavajravaineyānāṃ sattvānāṃ dharmadeśanāṃ kṛtvā punarātmano'grataḥ saṃsthāpyābhiṣeka- manunāthayet
abhiṣiñcantu māṃ sarve cittavajriṇa ' iti
tataścittavajjra kuladevībhirabhi- ṣiñcayamānamātmānaṃ vibhāvyādhiṣṭhānaṃ kārayet

adhiṣṭhānapadaṃ me'dya karotu cittavajriṇaḥ ||
daśadiksaṃsthitā buddhāstrivajrābhedya bhāvitāḥ
adhiṣṭhānapadaṃ me'dya kurvantu cittavajriṇaḥ ||
iti
evaṃ cittādhiṣṭhānaṃ kṛtvā tata ekatvena oṃ sarvatathāgatakāyavākcittasvabhāvā- tmako'hamityahaṅkāramudvahed yogīti niyamaḥ
evaṃ prajñāyā nābhau hokāreṇa, guhye svākāreṇa, uṣṇīṣasthāne hākāreṇa, trikula upāyaḥ śukradharmataḥ

oṃ śūnyatājñānavajjra - svabhāvātmako'hamiti niyamaḥ

tatrāhaṅkāraḥ oṃ viśuddhadharmadhātu svabhāvātmako'hamiti niyamaḥ
evaṃ śūnye vai dharmadhātukāle trikuliśa- samaye

kamaladalagatā: sūrya lagne ghaṃṭībhi- cāmuṇḍādyaṣṭayā meḥ daityādyāḥ sūryamāseḥ kamaladalagatā nāḍikāśvāsasaṃkhyeḥ

daityādyā iti

teṣāṃ kamaladalagatāḥ ṣaṣṭyuttaratriśataṃ lāsyādiyuktaṃ nāḍikāśvāsa- saṃkhyaidinaiḥ ṣaṣṭayuttaratriśatadināvaraṇakṣayeṇa śuddhāḥ
anye te devā anyāstāḥ patradevya iti śuddhāḥ

paṭale, 106-109 ślo. ] prāṇa devatotpādamahoddeśaḥ 203 manaḥ idānīṃ catuṣkāyakṛtyamucyate- nirmāṇa bhogakartṛ prabhavati hi manaḥ kāyavāgindriyaiśca saṃbhoge'dṛṣṭacintāṃ vrajati guṇavaśād dharmakāye ca nidrām |
śuddhe saukhyaṃ prayātyatra dinaniśisamaye bindumokṣatrayānte tasmāt tadbhāvanīyaṃ pratidinasamaye yoginā cākṣarārtham || 108 ||
nirmāṇa ityādi |
iha saṃsāriṇāṃ nirmāṇe jāgradavasthāyāṃ bhogakartṛ prabhavati kāyavāgindriyaiḥ karaṇabhūtaiviṣayeṣu |
saṃbhoge svapnāvasthāyāmadṛṣṭaviṣayeṣvajaḍeṣu 'cintāṃ vrajati guṇavaśāditi viṣayavāsanāvaśāt |

śuddhe turyāvasthāyāṃ saukhyaṃ prayāti |
atra dinasamaye niśi samaye vā |
samaya iti kāla:, tasmin kāle maithune kṛte ekasmin samaye bindu- mokṣatrayānte sahajakṣaṇe mahāsukhaṃ prayātoti |
saṃvṛtyā tattvaṃ yasmāt tasmāt tadbhāvanīyamahaniśikāle yoginā cākṣarārthamityacyutakṣaṇārthaṃ vakṣyamāṇena ṣaḍaṅganeti niyamaḥ |
evaṃ sāvaraṇadharme niruddhe nirāvaraṇadharmo bhavatyutpādavyayarahita iti nyāyaḥ || 108 ||
idānīṃ pratyālīḍhapadādiviśuddhirucyate - vāme prāṇapracāraḥ prabhavati ca tathā''kuñcanaṃ dakṣiṇe yat pratyālīḍhaṃ padaṃ tatsamapadamaparālīḍhamagnyakaṃcārāt |
vaiśākhaṃ maṇḍalaṃ vai varalalitapadaṃ padmavajrāsanaṃ ca vyomādau ca pracāraḥ samaviṣamagatau pañcadhā prāṇavāyoḥ || 109 ||
vāmetyādi |

|| idānīṃ ṣaḍaṅgayoga ucyate- ( nā. saṃ. ) pratyāhāro jinendro bhavati daśavidho dhyānamakṣobhya eva prāṇāyāmaśca khaḍgī punarapi daśadhā dhāraṇā ratnapāṇiḥ
ḍombyāṃ cānusmṛtiḥ syādapi kamaladharaḥ śrīsamādhiśca cakrī ekaikaḥ pañcabhedaiḥ punarapi ca yato bhidyate hyādikādyaiḥ ||
|| pratyāhāra ityādinā
iha pratyāhāra ādikarmaṇi jinendra iti jñānaskandhaḥ
sa ca nimittabhedena daśavidho dhūmamarīcikhadyotadīpajvālācandrādityarāhukalābindu- darśanabhedenākalpito jñānaskandhaḥ
dhyānamakṣobhya eva daśavidho vijñāna skandho viṣaya- viṣayiṇāṃ daśānāmekatvaṃ viśvabimbe dhyānamiti
prāṇāyāmaśca daśavidhaḥ
khaṅgīti saṃskāraskandhaḥ, vāma dakṣiṇadaśamaṇḍalaika lolībhūtatvāditi
punarapi vaśadhā dhāraṇā ratnapāṇiriti vedanāskandhaḥ
prāṇasya dhāraṇā nābhihṛtkaṇṭhalalā ṭoṣṇīṣakamale gatāgatabhedena daśavidha iti
ḍombyāṃ cānusmṛtiḥ syādapi kamalaghara iti saṃjñāskandho daśavidhaḥ
sa cānusmṛtirḍombyāṃ madhyanāḍyāṃ daśakāmāvasthābhedata iti
śrīsamā - ṣiśca cakrīti vairocano daśavidhaḥ

|| ādikādyairiti svaravyañjanaiḥ vāmadakṣiṇaprāṇasañcāra- idānīṃ pratyāhārādilakṣaṇamucyate- pratyāhāro daśānāṃ viṣayaviṣayiṇāmapravṛttiḥ śarīre prajñā tarko vicāro ratiracalasukhaṃ dhyānamapyekacittam

|| pratyetyādi
iha pratyāhāro nāma śarīre viṣayaviṣayiṇāṃ daśānāṃ sambandhenā- pravṛttivijñānasya śūnyabimbe, viṣayeṣu pravṛttiranyaiścakṣurādibhiḥ pañcavidhairiti

ratiriti bimbāsaktiḥ

skhalanam nirodho madhyame mārge praveśaḥ, sa ca daśavidho daśamaṇḍala- rodhataḥ

prajñopāyā- tmakeneti jñeyajñānaikalolībhūtena

sāpi daśavidhā prāṇādīnāmabhāvata iti
evaṃ ṣaḍaṅgayogasādhanam

trividhagativaśāditi

] utpannakramasādhanamahoddeśaḥ niḥspandagatiradhimātreti evaṃ karmamudrākṣarasukhadāyinī, jñānamudrā spandasukhadāyinī, mahāmudrā niḥspandasukhadāyinī evaṃ trimudrābhāvanā ṣaḍaṅgayoge bhagavatoktā iti ṣaḍaṅgayogo bhāvanīyo yoginā buddhatvāyeti || 117 ||
idānīṃ pratyāhārādiphalamucyate- pratyāhāreṇa yogī viṣayavirahito'dhiṣṭhyate sarvamantraḥ pañcābhijñānalābho bhavati narapate dhyānayogena śuddhaḥ
prāṇāyāmena śuddhaḥ śaśiravirahitaḥ pūjyate bodhisattva- marikleśādināśaṃ viśati daśabalaṃ dhāraṇāyā balena || 118 ||
pratyetyādi iha pratyāhāreṇa yogoṃ yadā viśuddho bhavati bimbena sthirobhūtena, tadā sarvamantrairadhiṣṭhaghate, vacasā varadānādikaṃ dadāti pañcābhijñānalābhī bhavati narapate dhyānayogena śuddha iti iha yadā'nimiṣitacakṣurbhavati tadā divyacakṣurbhavati evaṃ divya śrotro dhyānena śuddho bhavati prāṇāyāmena śuddha iti iha yadā raviśaśimārga- rahito yogī bhavati sadā madhyamāvāhakaḥ, tadā prāṇāyāmena śuddhaḥ san pūjyate bodhisattvaḥ, praśaṃsyata ityarthaḥ mārakleśādināśaṃ viśati daśabalamiti śūnyatā- bimbam iha grāhyagrāhakacittaṃ viśati dhāraṇāyā baleneti prāṇasya gatāgatakṣayeṇa ekalolībhavati || 118 ||
saṃśuddho'nusmṛteḥ syād vimalamapi prabhāmaṇḍalaṃ jñānabimbāt tasmācchuddhaḥ samādhau katipayadivasaiḥ siddhayate jñānadehaḥ
pratyāhārādibhirvai yadi bhavati na sā mantriṇāmiṣṭasiddhi-
rnādābhyāsāddhaṭhenābjagakuliśamaṇī sādhayed bindurodhāt || 119 ||
saṃśuddho'nusmṛteriti ihānusmṛtibimbāliṅganaṃ cittasya sarvavikalparahitatvam, tasmācchuddho yadā tadā vimalaṃ prabhāmaṇḍalaṃ bhavati api ca śabdād romakūpāt spharanti pañcaraśmayo niścaranti jñānavimbācnyavimbāditi tasmācchuddhaḥ samādhāviti iha grāhyagrāhakacittayorekatvena yadakṣarasukhaṃ bhavati, tatsukhaṃ samādhi- rucyate tasmāt samādhiśuddho vaimalyaṃ gataḥ katipayadivasaistrivarṣatripakṣa divasaiḥ siddhaghate jñānadeha iti daśavaśitādikaṃ prāpto bodhisattvo bhavatīti pratyāhārādi- niyamaḥ kha.
ga. cha. bho. eṣāṃ ca yadā yogī ga. ca. bho. miṣaca ca. bho. 'iha' nāsti cha. dinaiḥ |
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ sādhanā- idānīṃ haṭhayoga ucyate iha yadā pratyāhārāvibhirbimbe dṛṣṭe satyakṣarakṣaṇaṃ' notpadyate, ayantrita prāṇatayā, tadā nādābhyāsād vakṣyamāṇād haṭhena 'prāṇaṃ madhyamāyāṃ vāhayitvā prajñā'bjagatakuliśamaṇau bodhicittabindu nirodhādakṣarakṣaṇaṃ sādhayennispande- neti haṭhayogaḥ || 119 ||
idānīṃ śūnyatābimbasādhanāya dṛṣṭirucyate- sevāyāmādiyogo nabhasi daśavidhaścakriṇaḥ krodhadṛṣṭyā dṛṣṭyā vighnāntakasyāmṛtapathagatayā copasādhye ṣaḍaṅgaḥ |
prajñā sṛṣṭendubindorapi kuliśamaṇī tryakṣaraḥ sādhane syāt sokhyā'naṣṭe kaśāntaḥ sahaja iha mahāsādhane jñānayogaḥ || 120 ||
sevetyādi iha sevetyādidhūmādinimittabhāvanā, tasyāṃ sevāyāmādiyogo dhūmādi- nimittagrahaṇaṃ cittasyeti sa ca daśavidho dhūmādinā sādhaṃ pratyayo bhavati tena daśavidhaḥ sa ca cakriṇa ityuṣṇīṣasya krodhadṛṣṭyā iti ūrdhvadṛṣṭyā'nimiṣayā nimittaṃ bhavati rātriyogena catuvidham divāyogena ṣaḍvidham tato bimbaparyantaṃ sevāṅgaṃ bhavati pratyāhāreṇa dhyāneneti dṛṣṭyā vighnāntakasyeti vighnāntako mṛta- kuṇḍalī, tasya dṛṣṭiramṛtasthānagatā lalāṭagatā, tayā dṛṣṭyā vighnāntakasyāmṛtapathagasyā copasādhane ṣaḍaṅgaḥ cakārāt prāṇāyāmo dhāraṇā kartavyā prāṇasya bimbe dṛṣṭe sati upasādhanam prajñāsṛṣṭendubindoriti iha prajñārāgeṇa sṛṣṭaścāsāvindubinduḥ sṛṣṭendu- bindu:, tasya prajñāsṛṣṭabodhicittavindorapi kuliśamaṇau gatasya yastrayakṣaro 'yogo bhavati guhye nābhau hṛdaye, sa sādhane syāditi sādhanāṅke tṛtīye bhavati evaṃ sādhanāṅgaṃ kartavyam saukhyā'naṣṭaikaśānta iti iha saukhyenānaṣṭena bodhicittasya ya ekakṣaṇaḥ, sa śānta ityucyate sahaja iha mahāsādhane jñānayoga iti cittasyākṣarasukhena sahaika- tvamiti mahāsādhanāṅgaṃ caturtham evaṃ jñānasādhane caturaṅgam devatāsādhana utpatti- krameṇa pūrvoktaṃ laukikam, lokottaratattvasādhanamutpannakrameṇa tathā hi- satyadvayaṃ samāśritya buddhānāṃ dharmadeśanā |
lokasaṃvṛtisatyena satyena paramārthataḥ ||
iti bhagavato niyamaḥ punaḥ- kramadvayaṃ samāśritya deśanā vajriṇo mama |
utpattikrameṇaikā utpannakramato'parā ||
ka. kha. ga. cha. kṣaṇo bho, 'prāṇa' nāsti ga. niroghakṣaṇarakṣaṇaṃ ka. kha. ga. bho. itaḥ paraṃ 'bimbamevam' ityadhikaḥ pāṭhaḥ ga. 'dṛṣṭyā 'kasyeti' nāsti bho. ‘yogo' nāsti ca. nāṅge bho. De bşin Du gSuis Pa ( tathāha ) ga. ' puna: ' nāsti |
paṭale ilo utpannakramasādhanamahoddeśaḥ evaṃ devatāsādhane vikalpabhāvanā, tattvasādhane vikalparahitaiścaturaṅgeriti nyāyaḥ || 120 ||
idānīṃ prāṇāyāmalakṣaṇamucyate- prāṇāyāmaḥ samantāt samasukhaphalado mastake yāvadiṣṭa: tasmādūdhvaṃ hyaniṣṭo maraṇabhayakaraḥ skandhanirnāśahetuḥ |
uṣṇīṣaṃ bhedayitvā paramasukhapade yojanīyo vrajan ve skandhā'bhāve'pi yogī vrajati samasukhaṃ kintu loke'prasiddhiḥ || 121 ||
prāṇetyādi iha śūnyatā bimbe dṛṣṭe sati yaḥ prāṇāyāmo yoginā kartavyaḥ, sa yāvanmastake iti śirovyathāṃ na karoti sa ca sa masukhaphalado bhavati tasmādūrdhvamiti śirovyathāntādaniṣṭo maraṇabhayakaro bhavati, skandhanirnāśahetubhūto bhavati |
atha yogabalenoṣṇīṣaṃ bhedayitvā prāṇo vrajan yoginā paramasukhapade śūnyatābimbe yojanīyo vai ekāntam |
evaṃ skandhā'bhāve'pi yogī vrajati samasukhaṃ buddhabimbamiti yogīti yogacittaṃ vrajati |
kintu loke'prasiddhiriti yogī mṛto'yamiti prāṇāyāma - niyamaḥ || 121 ||
madhye prāṇapraveśo viṣayavirahitāliṅganaṃ viśvamātuḥ padmāviṣṭaṃ svavajraspharaṇamapi tathendvarkamadhye praveśaḥ |
saukhyaṃ bījāprapāte surataratigataṃ yogināṃ yogameta- nmudrāsiddhayarthahetoḥ paramamapi vibhoḥ śrorahasyād rahasyam ||122||
aparaṃ vṛttaṃ subodham || 122 ||
idānīṃ devatāvisarjanamucyate- uṣṇīṣe pañcaśukaṃ bhavati hi kuliśaṃ bāhyaśūkaṃ dviguṇyaṃ vajraṃ syād dharmacakre dviguṇitamaparaṃ tasya cānyad dviguṇyam |
tasyāpyanyad dviguṇyaṃ bhavati sahajasaṃbhoganirmāṇacakre tadgarbhe'pyekaśūkaṃ samasukhaphalada guhyapadmodarastham || 123||
uṣṇīṣa ityādi |
iha maṇḍalarājāgrī karmarājāgrī binduyogaṃ sūkṣmayogaṃ bhāvayitvā pūjāṃ stutiṃ kṛtvā namaste varadavacāgra ityādinā, vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ sādhanā- tato maṇḍaladevatānāṃ visarjanāyoṣṇīṣe pañcaśūkaṃ vajraṃ bhāvayet |
tasya varaṭake mahāsukha- cakraṃ visarjayet |
tasya bāhyaśukaṃ dviguṇyamaṣṭaśukaṃ madhyaśūkena sārdhaṃ navaśūkaṃ vajra syāddharmacakre hṛdaye |
tasya varaṭake dhūmādikān visarjayet |
dviguṇitamaparaṃ ṣoḍaśaśūkaṃ madhyaśakena sārdhaṃ saptadaśaśūkaṃ sahaje lalāṭe |
tasya varaṭake skandhadhātudevatā visarjayet |
tasya cānyad dviguṇyamiti dvātriṃśacchukaṃ madhyaśukena sārdhaṃ trayastriṃśacchukaṃ saṃbhoge kaṇṭhe |
tasya varaṭake dvādaśāyatanacatuḥ krodhadevatā visarjayet |
tasyāpyanyad dviguṇyamiti catuḥṣaṣṭiśūkaṃ madhyaśūkena sārdhaṃ pañcaṣaṣṭiśakaṃ vajraṃ nirmāṇe nābhicakre |
tasya varaṭake cacikādayo bhīmādayaścatuḥ ṣaṣṭimaṇḍalavāhinyaścatasraḥ śūnyakulikāḥ |
evaṃ dvāsaptati visarjayettā iti |
tadgarbhe'pyekaśūkamiti |
teṣāṃ garbhe prāguktamekaśūkaṃ samasukha- phaladaṃ guhyapadyodarastham |
tatrāpi dvātriṃśacchūkaṃ dalasaṃkhyam |
tasya varaṭake mārīcyādayo yāstā visarjayet |
evaṃ dvādaśadevāḥ saparivārāḥ karmacakrāṇi vajrāṇi kṛtvā teṣāṃ varaṭakeṣu visarjayet tāniti |
nāgāśca śvānāsyādayaḥ kriyācakaṃṣu |
evaṃ visarjanaṃ kṛtvā tato valiṃ dadyāt trisandhyaṃ prāguktavidhinā |
pañcāmṛtaṃ pūrvavidhinā śodhayitvā tenātmānaṃ prīṇayet |
evaṃ sādhanaṃ kālacakrasyotpattikrameṇotpannakrameṇoktaṃ bhagavatā mañjuśriyeti || 123 ||
idānīṃ yogacaryocyate- yogī prāṇātipātaṃ dinaniśi kurute prāṇanāśaḥ sa ukto yaḥ śabdo vaktrahīnaḥ prabhavati hṛdaye'sau mṛṣāvāda eva |
sarvajñajñānabhūmergrahaṇamapi ca yad yoginaḥ steyamuktaṃ saukhyaṃ bindvaprapāte bhavati ca paradārasya sevā'virāgāt || 124 ||
yogītyādi |
iha yogināṃ yogacaryā dvidhā -- ekā bāhyā, dvitīyā'dhyātmikī |
tatra yā bāhyā sā laukika phala hetoḥ |
yā cādhyātmikī sā lokottaraphala hetoryoginā kartavyeti |
iha yogī yat prāṇātipātaṃ dinaniśi kurute tat svadehe prāṇanāśa uktaḥ |
sarvajñapadalābhāya na bāhye prāṇātipātaḥ |
iha bāhye yaḥ prāṇātipāta ukto durdānta-
damanāya sa teṣāṃ prāṇo yogabalenākṛṣṭaḥ punastasminneva kāye praveśanīyo yogineti
evaṃ durdāntadamako bhavati
na durdāntāntako 'bhavatīti siddhaḥ
yaḥ śabdo vaktrahīna iti
iha sarvajñasya vacanaṃ sarvarutaṃ yat sarvasattvānāṃ hṛdaye bhavati svasvabhāṣāntareṇa, tadevā- pratiṣṭhitaṃ sarvasattvarutatvādapratiṣṭhitatvānmṛṣāvāda ityuktaḥ
bāhye punaḥ sattvārthaṃ prati mahāmāyā mahāraudrā bhūtasaṃhārakāriṇī ityādivacanaṃ sattva- vaineyārtham
tathā— utpannakrama sādhanamahoddeśaḥ sukhaṃ dvīndriyajaṃ tattvaṃ buddhatvaphaladāyakam
narā vajradharākārā yoṣito vajrayoṣitaḥ ||
ityādivacanaṃ mṛṣāvādaḥ, na punaḥ sattvānāṃ vañcanāya visaṃvādakaṃ vacanaṃ bauddhayogināmiti siddhaye
iha śarīre nirāvaraṇe jāte sati sarvajñasya dvādaśabhūmīnāṃ yad grahaṇaṃ yoginastat steyagrahaṇamuktam
bāhye punaḥ sattvopakārato nidhānādika- mutpāṭanīyaṃ nidhirakṣakāṇāṃ durgatimocanārthamiti
saukhyaṃ yat śukrabindora- prapātād bhavati sā paradārasya sevā
paradārā prajñāpāramitā saṃsārapāraṃ gatā, paro vajrasattvaḥ saṃsārapāraṃ gataḥ, tasya dārā paradāreti, tasyāḥ sevā'virāgato'kṣarasukhato yoginām
bāhye punaḥ sekādikāle dātrā svabhāryādikā dattā yā tasyāḥ sevā paradārasya sevā'virāgāditi
yathātmasamayināṃ virāgo na bhavati samayabheda ityarthaḥ || 124 ||
prāṇāyāmānalena dravamapi śaśinaḥ pānakaṃ madyapānaṃ uṣṇīṣe'ṅguṣṭhaparvād vrajati tithivaśāt pūrṇimānte svacittam
uṣṇīṣādaṅgulīṣu vrajati punaridaṃ kṛṣṇapakṣāvasānaṃ sā caryā yogino ve pratidinasamaye tviṣṭasiddhipradā yā || 125 ||
prāṇāyāmānaleneti
iha prāṇanirodhena yā caṇḍālī jvalitā, sā prāṇāyāmānala ityucyate, tena prāṇāyāmānalena dravamapi śaśina iti bodhicittasya drutasya dravaṃ bindurūpaṃ pānakaṃ kuliśamukhenordhvato yattat sahajānandajanakaṃ madyapānaṃ yogināmuktamiti
bāhye punaḥ 'sekādikāle bāhyadevatānāṃ valyarthamuktamiti
uṣṇīṣe'ṅguṣṭhaparvāditi
iha kāmaśāstre śrūyate - iha śuklapakṣe vāmapādāṅguṣṭhāt pratipadādivṛddhayā candrakalāvṛddhayā pūrṇimānte uṣṇīṣe svacittamiti bodhicittaṃ vrajati tithivaśāditi
punaruṣṇoṣāda- dakṣiṇapādāṅgulīṣu vrajati punaridaṃ kṛṣṇapakṣatithivaśād yāvat kṛṣṇapakṣāvasānam amāntam
evaṃ kṛṣṇapakṣāvasāne bodhicittaṃ pādāṅguṣṭhe veditavyam, punaraparamāse śuklapakṣe vāmāṅguṣṭhe pūrvavaditi
tatrāha - prathamā tithiḥ prathamāṅgalīparve, dvitīyā dvitīye, tṛtīyā tṛtīye, caturthī vāmapādasandhau pañcamī jānusandhau, ṣaṣṭhī kaṭyarundhau, saptamī vāmakarāṅguliprathamaparvasandhī, aṣṭamī madhyama' sandhau navamī tṛtīyasandhau, daśamī karamandhī, ekādaśī bāhusandhau dvādaśī skandhabāhusandho, trayodaśī hṛdaye, caturdaśī kaṇṭhe, pūrṇā lalāṭe, pūrṇāntamuṣṇīṣe śukrasya bhavati
punaḥ kṛṣṇaprati- pallalāṭe, dvitīyā kaṇṭhe, tṛtīyā hṛdaye, caturthī dakṣiṇaskandhabāhusandhau
śeṣaṃ vāmavadvilomena dakṣiṇapādāṅgulīnakhāntaṃ yāvadamāntaṃ bodhicittasya sā caryā yogino vai pratidinasamaye iṣṭasiddhipradā yeti
iha bodhicittasya vāmadakṣiṇanāḍīpravāhavaśena vāmadakṣiṇena gatasya sarvakālaṃ madhyamāpravāhena ṣaṭsu guhyādikamaleṣvadhogamanādūrdhva- ga

vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ sādhanā- gamanaṃ nāma caryā, sā iṣṭasiddhirmahāmudrā siddhistasyā: prakarṣeṇa 'dātrīṣṭapradeti siddham
bāhye punaḥ pañcatathāgatakulanārīṇāṃ grahaṇaṃ nārīcaryā, tāsu nārīcaryāsu manthānaṃ brahmacaryam
tathā nena yoginā kartavyamiti
tathā ko'sau bodhicittasya nāḍīsaṃcāra ityūrdhvaretaso gamanaṃ kartavyamiti niyamo mūlatantre
iti nārīcaryāniyamaḥ || 125 ||
cintākāṅkṣā jvaro'ṅge varamukhakamale śuṣkadravyāpravṛttiḥ kamponmādaśca ghūrmā prabhavati manaso vibhramastīvramūrcchā
dhūmādyā vajriṇastāḥ prakaṭadaśavidhāḥ prāṇino'ṅgeṣvavasthā loke tā manmathasya prakaṭitaniyatā ko jinaḥ kaḥ sa kāmaḥ || 126 ||
yā bindoḥ śvetadhārā patati dinaniśaṃ māmakī sāsurā no gokvādyaṃ cakṣurādeḥ sphuraṇamanudinaṃ nānyamāṃsaṃ kadācit
sevā pañcāmṛtānāṃ svakulabhuvigatairdevateḥ śuddhikāye śūnye cittapraveśāt samarasakaraṇaṃ maithunaṃ tanna yonau || 127 ||
[290b] dānaṃ tyāgo dhanasyācyutirapi manasaḥ strīprasaṅgācca śīlaṃ kṣāntiḥ śabdādyaveśo hyubhayagativināśo'nilasyaiva vīryam
dhyānaṃ prajñā ca cittaṃ sahajasukhagataṃ sarvagā sarvabhāṣā tasyāḥ sattvārthamṛddhirbhavanidhana majaprāptiranyāścatasraḥ || 128 ||
ekāṅge śaktiyukte navapadasahite pañcaviṃśātmakādye dhyāte mudrādayo vai katipayadivaseḥ siddhayaḥ saṃbhavanti
stambhaṃ śānti ca vaśyaṃ bhuvananidhanatāṃ vaktrabhedeḥ karoti bhūtānāṃ maṇḍalastho danubhujagakulaṃ sādhayed bhāvito'sī || 129 ||
iha pañcaviṃśatyadhikaśatavṛttāt vṛttacatuṣṭayaṃ subodham

] utpanna kramasādhana mahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ śāntyādyarthaṃ devatābhāvanocyate- śvetaḥ śānti ca puṣṭi svamanasi kurute rakta ākṛṣṭivaśyaṃ pītaḥ stambhaṃ ca mohaṃ kaṣaṇaghananibhaṃ māraṇoccāṭanaṃ ca
dhyātaṃ japtaṃ tathaiva svamanasi kurute kāyavākcittavajraṃ bhūtānāṃ maṇḍalasthaṃ tribhuvananilaye sādhayet karmabhedaiḥ || 130 ||
śveta ityādi
iha kālacako bhagavānekavīro vā prajñopāyātmako vā pañcā 'tma- ko vā vaktrādibhedaiḥ śāntyādikaṃ bhavati
yadā śānti karoti yogī, tadā yoginā kāyavaktranāyakaṃ kṛtvā śuklavarṇo bhāvanīyaścandramaṇḍale lalāṭasthaḥ
śvetaḥ śānti puṣṭi ca karoti
rakta ākṛṣṭi vaśyaṃ ca karoti vāgvajranāyakaḥ sūryamaṇḍale kaṇṭhastho manasi dhyātaḥ san
pītaḥ stambhanaṃ mohanaṃ ca karoti kālāgnimaṇḍale nābhistho dhyāto jñānavajranāyaka iti
kṛṣṇo māraṇamuccāṭanaṃ 'ca karoti 'vidveṣaṃ ca karoti hṛdaye rāhumaṇḍale cittavantranāyaka iti
evaṃ bhūtānāṃ maṇḍalastho danubhujagakulaṃ sādhayed bhāvito'sāviti
detyabhujagānāṃ kulamaṣṭavidhaṃ tadeva kulaṃ sādhayedvakṣyamāṇaṃ gāruḍe nātra vistāritamiti
dhyātaṃ japtaṃ tathaiva svamanasi kuruta iti
[ 2912 ] ekamukhadvibhujadevatā kāyādivarṇabhedena bhāvitā vakṣyamāṇakrameṇa śāntyādikaṃ karoti
evaṃ bhūtānāṃ maṇḍalastha- miti toyādimaṇḍalasthaṃ vajracatuṣṭayam, 'sādhayet tribhuvananilaye karmabhedairanekairiti nānāvidhānairityarthaḥ || 130 ||
idānīṃ durdāntadamanāya gajacarmapaṭārdraghṛgbhāvanocyate- pakṣādhikyodbhavābhyāṃ maṇikanakanibhābhyāṃ ca savyetarābhyāṃ skandhāriṣṭebhacarmoddhṛtamapi sakalaṃ pāṭayitvāṅghriyugmāt

mṛtyumārāsyahasta iti

parārthamiti durdāntaveneyārthamiti niyamaḥ || ||

tasmāt kāyaprabhederbhavati jinapatirviśvamātā tathaiva prajñopāyāṅgabhāvaḥ samasukhaphaladeścandrasūryapracāreḥ || ||
māturityādi
iha kāyabhedena sūryaḥ prajñā, candra upāyaḥ
sa ca candraḥ sūryacārād dvādaśādhikyamekaṃ cāraṃ yāvaccarati māsaṃ pratitrayodaśarāśīṃścarati

idānīṃ viśvarūpabhāvanocyate- ekādyānantavaktro bahukaracaraṇo'nekavarṇastamo'nte prajñopāyātmako vai dadati samasukhaṃ nāḍikendvarkaṃrodhāt

• paṭale, ślo. ] nānā sādhanamahoddeśaḥ bhūmyādīnāṃ samantādamalamaṇinibho bhedakaḥ śūnya eko nādyo nānto na madhyastvaviṣayaviṣayaḥ sādhitaḥ kālacakraḥ || ||
eketyādi
ihaikavaktro vā ādiśabdāt 'trimukho vā catuḥpañcādyanantamukho vā bahukaracaraṇo'nekakaracaraṇo'nekāstradharaḥ
anekavarṇo'nekasaṃsthānaḥ “viśvamāyādharo rājā buddhavidyādharo mahān ( nā0 saṃ0 ) śūnyatākṣaradharo bhagavān prajñopāyātmakaḥ
tamo'nte niśākāle niśāyogena divākāle divāyogena yaḥ prajñāpāramitāyāṃ yogaṃ paśyati, sa ākāśe paśyati niśāyāmabhyavakāśe paśyati divāyām
evaṃ vibhāvito bimbaparyantam
tato nāḍikendvarka rodhāditi vāmadakṣiṇaprāṇa rodhāt dadapti samasukhamiti paramākṣarasukhaṃ dadāti
bhūmyādīnāmiti pṛthivyādīnāṃ dhātūnām
amalamaṇinibho bhedaka iti
ihāmalamaṇiryathā sparśamātreṇa pāṣāṇādikaṃ dhātukaṃ ratnaṃ karoti na bhedako vedhaka iti
tathā śūnya eko vimalo bhūmyādīnāṃ śarīradhātūnāṃ samantād vedhaka iti
sa śūnyatārūpī nādyo nānto na madhyo'viṣayaviṣaya iti
viṣayaivinā viṣaya- pratibhāso māyāsvapna pratisenopamaḥ
sādhitaḥ kālacakraḥ samasukhaṃ dadātīti niyama iti śrīmadādibuddhasādhanamutpannakrameṇoktam, asya vistaro jñānapaṭale vaktavya iti || ||
iti śrīmūlatantrānusāriṇyāṃ laghukālacakratantrarājaṭīkāyāṃ ( ) nānā sādhana mahoddeśaḥ dvādaśasāhasrikāyāṃ vimala prabhāyāṃ utpannakramasādhanamahoddeśaścaturthaḥ
vajravegaṃ namaskṛtya viśvavajradharaṃ prabhum
nāyakaṃ krodharājānāṃ nānāsādhanamucyate ||
krodhendraṃ vajravegaṃ dvayadhikajinakaraṃ vedavaktraṃ dvipādaṃ piṅgākṣaṃ piṅgakeśaṃ jinapatimukuṭaṃ tīkṣṇadaṃṣṭrākarālam
sarpālaṃ vyāghracarmapravaranivasanaṃ bhartṛvacchastrahastaṃ mūrdhno mālānibaddhaṃ sakala jinakuleḥ pañcavarṇaiḥ kapālaiḥ || ||
krodhendramityādinā
iha krodhendraṃ vajravegaṃ hu~kāravajraniṣpannaṃ pūrvoktasādhanavidhi- nā
dvadyadhikajinakaramiti ṣaḍviṃśatibhujaṃ gajacarmapaṭadhāriṇam
vevavaktra- miti caturmukham dvipādaṃ piṅgākṣaṃ piṅgakeśaṃ jinapatimukuṭamityakṣobhyamukuṭaṃ tīkṣṇa- vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ sādhanā- daṃṣṭrākarālam
sarpālamiti sarpabhūṣaṇam
vyāghracamaṃ pravaranivasanam
bhartṛvacchastrahastaṃ kālacakravaditi
mūrdhna mālānibaddhaṃ sakalajinakuleviśuddheḥ
pañcavarṇaiḥ kapālaiḥ || ||
viśvābje sūryamūni sphuradamalakaraṃ maṇḍale viśvavarṇe pādābhyāṃ bhūtanāthākramitamatibalāt saṃsthitālīḍhapādam
bhūtādīṃstrāsayantaṃ hyamuraphaṇisurān jñānasattvekabhūtaṃ dhyāyannevaikamāsaṃ citibhuvanagataṃ sādhayed bhūtavṛndam || ||
itthaṃbhūtaṃ viśvānje sūryamaṇḍalopari sphuradamalakaraṃ svacchaṃ maṇḍalagṛhe viśvavarṇe ekavīram madhye caturdvāreṣu vajrāṅkuśa' vajravajrapāśa' vajravajraghaṇṭā yathānukrameṇa dattvā pādābhyāṃ bhūtanāthamaparājitapreta nāthamākramitamatibalātsaṃsthitālīḍhapādaṃ bhūtāvīṃstrāsa- yantaṃ gajacarmadhṛtaṃ karatarjanībhyām asura phaṇisurāṃstrāsayantamiti
jñānasattveka- bhūtam
ebhirmantrapadaiḥ jaḥ hū~ va~ ho dhyāyan yogī, evaikamāsaṃ citibhuvanagataṃ śmaśānabhūmigataṃ sādhayed bhūtavṛndamiti bhūtādīnāṃ yo nāyakaḥ, sa tayā mūrtyā pādatale pātitaḥ san saparivāraḥ siddhiṃ gacchati
preto vā rākṣasādika iti bhūtādisādhana- niyamaḥ || 135 ||
idānīṃ meghavarṣāpaṇāya nāgarājasādhanamucyate- nāgānabjāṣṭapatreṣvapi jayavijayau pātayitvā'rkamūrdhni pādābhyāṃ stambhayitvā phaṇipatimithunaṃ padmapatre sthitānām
lāṅgūlāgraṃ ca sarvaṃ ghanakulamudarān muñcato vai samantād dhyātaḥ krodhendra evaṃ katipaya divasaiḥ sādhayenmeghavṛndam || 136 ||
nāgānityādi
iha sa eva vajravegaḥ krodhendro dhyātaḥ san sādhayenmeghavṛndam
katipayadivasairiti māsadinerevamityanena vidhinā
nāgānabjāṣṭapatreṣviti
abjapūrva- patre karkoṭaḥ, agnau padmaḥ, dakṣiṇe vāsukiḥ, nairṛtye śaṅkhapālaḥ, uttare anantaḥ, īśāne kulikaḥ, paścime takṣakaḥ, vāyavye mahāpadmaḥ pūrvāgnau kṛṣṇo, dakṣiṇe nairṛtye raktau, uttareśāne śuklau, paścimavāyavye potau, api jayavijayau haritanīlau nāgarājānau pātayitvā'rkamūni arkamaṇḍale vāmadakṣiṇapādatale nābhyadhvaṃ puruṣākārāvadhaḥ sarpākārī śira upari saptaphaṇacakravāhau mahāmaṇibhiḥ sphurantāvuttānako pātayitvā 'apare- nāgarājān pātayitvā teṣāṃ lāṅgūlāgraṃ pratyekaṃ jayopari pūrvottarāṇām, vijayopari dakṣiṇapaścimānām
lāṅgūlāgraṃ ca sarvam
evaṃ pādābhyāṃ stambhayitvā phaṇipatimithunaṃ paṭale, 134- 139 ślo. ] nānāsādhanamahoddeśaḥ padmapatre sthitānāṃ lāṅgūlāgraṃ ca sarvamiti
evamau nāgarājāḥ pañca phaṇino ghanakulaṃ meghavṛndamudarānmuñcato vai samantāt
evaṃ krodhendro dhyātaḥ smaśānabhūmyāṃ māsadinairmegha- vṛndaṃ sādhayet
tato yathābhirucitakāle varṣāpayati, visarjanena vidhārayati
iti nāgarājasādhananiyamaḥ || 136 ||
idānīṃ karmabhedairdevatāsādhanamucyate-
ityādyaṃ devatānāṃ bhavati narapate sādhanaṃ devatīnāṃ pratyekaṃ maṇḍale'smin svajinakulavaśāt karmabhedaiḥ samasteḥ
stambhe śāntī ca vaśye paradhanaharaṇe māraṇoccāṭanādye ṣaṭtriṃśadyoginīnāṃ bhavati khalu punarjāpahomaṃ svabījeḥ ||137||
ityādyamityādi
iha maṇḍale uktādyadaparaṃ devatā daivatānāṃ sādhanaṃ bhavati narapate pratyekaṃ maṇḍale'smin kavīraiḥ svajina kulavaśād vairocanādikulavaśāt, karmabhedaiḥ samastaiḥ sādhanaṃ bhavati
stambhe śāntau vaśye paradhanaharaṇe māraṇoccāṭanāce vakṣyamāṇasādhanaṃ ṣatriṃśadyoginīnāmanyāsāṃ śmaśānaparyantānāṃ bhavati khalu punarjāpahomaṃ ca sva-sva ' mantrabījairbhavati ||137||
idānīṃ śāntyādidhyānamucyate-
śāntau dhyānaṃ ca śāntaṃ śaśadharadhavalā devatā śāntarūpā raudre dhyānaṃ ca raudraṃ kaṣaṇaghananibhā devatā raudrarūpā
vaśye dhyānaṃ sarāgaṃ dinakaravapuṣā devatā rāgamūrtiḥ stambhe dhyānaṃ samūḍhaṃ varakanakanibhā devatā stabdharūpā || 138 ||
śāntāvityādi
iha śāntau dhyānaṃ ca śāntaṃ śaśadharadhavalā devatā śāntarūpā dhyātavyeti
raudre māraṇādye dhyānaṃ raudraṃ kṛṣṇavarṇā devatā raudramūrtiḥ
vaśye dhyānaṃ sarāgaṃ devatā raktavarṇā rāgamūrtiḥ
stambhane dhyānaṃ mūḍhaṃ devatā pītavarṇā stabdharūpeti
yathā śāntau tathā puṣṭau jvaropaśamane viṣāpaharaṇe ca bhavati
yathā māraṇe tathoccāṭane vidveṣe jvarasaṃkrāmaṇe ceti
yathā vaśye tathākṛṣṭau stobhane jvarotpādane ca
yathā stambhane tathā mohane kīlane ceti niyamaḥ || 138 ||
idānīṃ gaṇakule: śāntyādisiddhirucyate-
śāntiḥ puṣṭiśca rājan sasutajinakule: siddhyate devatībhi- vidveṣoccāṭanaṃ ca prakṛtiguṇavaśāt siddhayate krodhajābhiḥ
vaśyādyaṃ bhūtajābhiḥ prakaṭadanukule kīlanaṃ cāsurībhi- matṛbhyāṃ sarvakarmāṇyubhayapavikule māraṇaṃ jīvanaṃ ca || 139 ||
śāntirityādi
iha śāntiḥ puṣṭiśca sasutajinakulairiti
iha rūpa - vedanā- saṃjñā-saṃskārā etāni catvāri jinakulāni sasutānīti
viṣkambha - kṣiti- garbha- lokeśvara-khagarbha etāni bodhisattvakulāni
evaṃ locanā- pāṇḍarā māmakī- tārā jinakulāni
gandhavajrā-rasavajrā-rūpavajrā - sparśavatrā bodhisattvakulāni
ebhiḥ kulaiḥ śuklavarṇairbhāvitaiḥ pratyekaikaiścandramaṇḍale lalāṭe padmāsane upāyaiḥ sitapadmavarada- hastaiḥ, prajñābhiḥ sitotpalābhayahastābhiḥ śāntiḥ puṣṭiśca siddhayate
rājannityāmantraṇam
cakārānnirviṣatvaṃ jvaropaśamanaṃ ceti
vidveṣoccāṭanaṃ cakārānmāraṇaṃ viṣasaṃkrāmaṇaṃ ca
prakṛtiguṇavaśāditi krodhaprakṛtiguṇavaśāt, siddhayate krodhajābhiriti
krodhajā hū~kāravajrajā daśa krodhāḥ, phreṃkārakartijā daśa krodhabhāryāḥ, hṛdaye rāhumaṇḍale krodharāloḍha- pādairvajrapāśahastairmāraṇaṃ siddhyati
khaṅgaśṛṅkhalāhastaiḥ satarjanyavidveṣādyaṃ' kṛṣṇavarṇaiḥ krūrairiti
devībhiḥ kartikapālahastābhiḥ pratyāloḍhābhiḥ khaḍgapāśahastābhiriti siddhayati
vaśyādyaṃ bhūtajābhiriti
iha carcikādibhiraṣṭadevībhiḥ sūryamaṇḍale kaṇṭhe viśākhapadābhī raktavarṇābhirdhanurbāṇahastābhirvaśyaṃ siddhayati
ākṛṣṭādyaṃ pāśāṅkuśahastābhiḥ siddhyatīti
prakaṭadanukule kolanaṃ cāsurobhiriti
iha śvānāsyādyaṣṭadevībhiḥ pītavarṇābhiḥ, nābhau kālāgnimaṇḍale pīte maṇḍalapadābhiścakraparvatahastābhiḥ stambhanaṃ siddhayati
mudgarakīlaka hastābhiḥ kīlanaṃ siddhyati
triśūla nāgahastābhiḥ mohanaṃ siddhayatīti
mātṛbhyāmiti vajradhātvīśvaryā prajñāpāramitayā vā guhyakamale sarvakarmāṇi siddhayanti
udakādimaṇḍalabhedena sitādivarṇena pūrvoktena pratyekaikacihnena padena ca māraṇaṃ jīvanaṃ ca siddhyati
vajrāsanena bindumadhye sānandā devatā jīvanaṃ bhavati, yogabalena prāṇānākṛṣya cyutena bindunā viraktā māraṇaṃ karoti, punaḥ pratyujjīvanaṃ nāsti sādhyasya
tena tatsādhanaṃ bauddhayoginā na kartavyam, yatra sādhyasya prāṇe ākṛṣṭe sati śukranirgamo bhavatīti niyamaḥ sarvakarmasu || 139 ||
idānīṃ sarvakarmasādhanānāmādikāraṇamucyate-
ādau śrīkālacakrastribhuvanajananī yatnataḥ sādhanīyau paścāt karmāṇi sādhyāni ca bhuvinilaye śāntikādīni yāni
mātrā pitrā vihīno nahi bhavati sutaḥ sarvadā lokasiddha- stasmād dvau sādhanīyo samasukhaphalado nānyathā karmasiddhiḥ || 140 ||
Lhamo nānāsāghanamahoddeśaḥ ādāvityādi
ihādau yoginā yatnata iti gurūpadeśataḥ sādhanīyaḥ śrīkālacakra iti prāṇavāyurmadhyamāyāṃ praveśitavyaḥ sadā
tribhuvanajananīti śūnyatābimbam
tau dvau bimbaprāṇau yatnataḥ sādhanīyau
paścāduktāni sarvakarmāṇi sādhyāni bhavanti bhuvitala- nilaye śāntikādīni yāni
atra dṛṣṭāntaḥ - mātrā pitrā vihono nahi bhavati sutaḥ sarvadā lokasiddhaḥ
tasmād dvau sādhanīyau bimbaprāṇau samasukhaphaladau nānyathā karmasiddhirasti, bimbena prāṇenāsādhiteneti niyamaḥ || 140 ||
idānīṃ śāntyādisādhanāya ādibhāvanocyate-
bhartuhṛtpadmamadhye śaśiraviśikhini sthāpayenmūni vajraṃ hū~kāraṃ jñānajātaṃ pralayaghananibhaṃ pañcaśūkaṃ saraśmi
tanmadhye jo'ṅkuśasya tribhuvanasakalaṃ raśmibhiḥ pūrayitvā ākṛṣya jñānacakraṃ trividhabhavagataṃ vajramārge praveśya || 141 ||
sarvaṃ candradravābhaṃ svakuliśavadanādutsṛjenmātṛpadma tasmin sūrye praviṣṭaṃ bhavati samarasaṃ cādikādiprayuktam
tanmadhye jñānabījaṃ bhavati kulavaśāt karmaṇaḥ śāntikāde- stenotpannā ca devī bhavati hi phaladā yogino devatā vā || 142 ||
bharturityādi
iha yadā yogī bimbaṃ vispaṣṭamavadhūtyāṃ prāṇagataṃ paśyati, tadā tadvimbaṃ yādṛśaṃ vikalpayet tādṛśaṃ paśyati, tadvimbaṃ bharturiti
kālacakraṃ pūrvoktaṃ niṣpādyaṃ tatastasya hṛtpadmamadhye kaṇikāyāṃ śaśiraviśikhinīti candrasūrya rāhuyoga grahamaṇḍale tryātmake, adhyātmani lalanārasanā'vadhūtyekalolībhūte hutkamale
tatra sthāpayed muni vajraṃ hū~kārapariṇataṃ pañcaśūkaṃ pralayaghananibhaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇami iti saraśmi paścaraśmi spharaditi
tanmadhya iti tasya vajrasya madhyavaraṭake jaḥkārapariṇataṃ vajrāṅkuśaṃ bhāvayet
tatastasyāṅkaśasya raśmibhirvajrāṅkuśākārastribhuvanamiti tridhātukaṃ sakalaṃ pūrayitvā tairvajrāśaistribhavākāraṃ svacchaṃ jñānacakramākṛṣya trividhabhavagataṃ vyāpakatvena yat tadavadhūtīdvāreṇoṣṇīṣalalāṭakaṇṭhahṛdayanābhiguhyamārge praveśya
sarvamiti sarvākāraṃ yattaccandradravābhamiti bodhicittalakṣaṇam, svakuliśavadanādutsṛjenmātṛpadma iti svavastra - mukhādyathā puruṣaḥ strīkamale bodhicittamānanditaṃ kṣipet tathā devatāyogena devyāḥ padma utsṛjet
mātriti vakṣyamāṇānāṃ jananī yathā garbhajānāṃ tathaiva
tasmin sūrye praviṣṭa- miti
iha yathā strīyonau rakta praviṣṭaṃ bāṃdhicittaṃ samarasaṃ raktena saha bhavati, tathā sūryamadhye praviṣṭaṃ candraṃ samarasaṃ mātṛpadme bhavati
ādiyuktaṃ candradravaṃ kāviyuktaṃ sūryarajaḥ, prāṇāpānayuktam
tanmadhye prāṇāpānamadhye jñānabojamālayavijñānalakṣaṇaṃ bhavati
kulavaśāditi pañcaskandhavāsanāvaśāt
sattvānāṃ vijñānaṃ bhavati
evaṃ karmaṇaḥ śānti- kāverjñānabījaṃ bhavati
tena bojena utpannā yathā kumārī vā kumāro vā, bhavati hi T vimalaprabhāyāṃ phalado dvādaśavarṣāvadheḥ ṣoḍaśavarṣāvadheḥ, tathā devī devatā upāyo vā yoginā bhāviteti niyamaḥ
ato dvādaśavarṣairdevī varadā bhavati bhāvitā, devaśca ṣoḍaṣavarṣairvarado bhavati
tataḥ sarvakarmāṇi sarvasiddhayaḥ sarvasaukhyāni yoginaḥ siddhayanti
anyathā kleśa: kevala eveti sarvatantrāntare kālaniyamo vīryavatāmahaniśi bhāvitātmanām, nānyeṣāṃ śatāvadheriti siddhiniyamaḥ || 141-142||
idānīṃ cihnotpādāya jñānabījānyucyante-
ja: hū~ va~ ho : krameṇāṅkuśa iti kuliśaṃ vajrapāśaśca ghaṇṭā oṃ āḥ hū~ hostathoktaṃ śaśiravikuliśaṃ cākṣaraṃ tadvadeva
ī ṛ ū lu tathaiva prakaṭayaravalā vāyuvahnayambupṛthvyo
haḥ huṃ haṃ phreṃ tathoktaṃ ravirapi kuliśaṃ candramā kartikā ca || 143 ||
'ja ityādi |
iha jaḥ hū~ va~ hoḥ krameṇeti jaḥkāreṇa vajrāṅkuśo bhavati, tena pariṇatena vajrāśahastā devī vā devo vā bhavati |
evaṃ hū~kāreṇa vajram, tena vajrahastā bhavati |
va~kāreṇa pāśahastena pāśahastā bhavati |
ho: kāreṇa ghaṇṭā, tayā ghaṇṭāhastā bhavati |
oṃ āḥ hū~ hoḥ tathoktamiti |
tatheti krameṇa pūrvavat |
oṃkāreṇa candramaṇḍalaṃ śaśīti |
āḥkāreṇa sūryamaṇḍalaṃ ravīti |
hu~kāreṇa rāhumaṇḍalaṃ kuliśamiti |
ho:kāreṇa kālāgnimaṇḍalamakṣaraṃ tadvadeveti |
ī ṝ ū lu tathaiveti |
yathākrameṇa īkārapariṇataḥ khaṅgaḥ, tena pariniṣpannā devatā khaḍgahastā devī vā |
evaṃ ṛkāreṇa maṇirbāṇo vā, tena tejodevatā maṇihastā bāṇahastā vā devī |
ūkāreṇa padmam tena toyadevatā padmahastā utpalahastā vā devī |
lṛkāreṇa cakram |
cakreṇa pṛthivodevatā cakrahastā devī vā |
evaṃ yaravalā api yathākrameṇa vāyvagnitoyapṛthivīdevatā iti |
tathā haḥ iti ravimaṇḍalam |
hū~ iti ravi - mūrdhni vajraṃ nāyakasya |
hamiti candramaṇḍalam |
iti candramaṇḍalopari phartikā |
nāyikā cihna niyamaḥ |
tathoktamiti || 143 ||
rucyate- idānīṃ devatāyāṃ sādhitāyāṃ satyāṃ śāntyādikarmakaraṇāya devatāsamādhi- dhyātvā candrārkamadhye tvalikalisahite toyabījātmakābjaṃ tenotpannekavaktrāṃ yamakarakamalāṃ devatīṃ candravarṇām |
ārūḍhāṃ śvetanāgaṃ sitajalajakarāṃ cābhayāṃ śvetavastrāṃ śvetālaṅkārayuktāṃ prahasitavadanāṃ preṣayet sādhyaveśma || 144 ||
nānā sādhana mahoddeśaḥ dhyātvetyādi |
iha pūrvoktamātṛguhyapadye candrārkamadhye āvikādisahite toyabījātma kābja 'miti vakārapariṇataṃ śuklaṃ padmam, tenotpannaikavaktrā dvibhujā devatā candravarṇā |
ārūḍhā śvetanāgamiti airāvatamārūḍhā |
sitajalajakareti śvetapadmahastā devatā devī śvetotpalahastā |
abhayā dakṣiṇe'bhayahastā |
śvetavastrā śvetālaṅkārayuktā muktāphalābharaṇā prahasitavadanā bhāvyā |
tāṃ ca preṣayet sādhya veśmani || 144 ||
tasmāt sādhyaṃ gṛhītvā punarapi ca vibhormaṇḍale saṃpraviṣṭāṃ bhartuścājñāṃ pralabdhvā punaramṛtaghaṭerlocanādyāḥ prahṛṣṭāḥ
taṃ sādhyaṃ snāpayanti pravaradaśavidhāḥ śaktayaḥ pūjayanti rūpādyā: poṣayanti prakaṭadaśabhirlāsyādayastoṣayanti || 145 ||
bhūtākhyāścābhayante pravaradaśavidhāḥ krodhajāḥ pālayanti nāginyaścumbayanti tvamarayuvatayo dvādaśāliṅgayanti |
caṇḍāḥ kurvanti rakṣāṃ sakalabhuvitale śāntipuṣṭyarthaṃ heto- revaṃ sādhyasya sarvaṃ paramasukhakaraṃ yoginā bhāvanīyam || 146 ||
aparavṛttadvayenoktaṃ subodham |
tasmādityādinā evaṃ sādhyasya savaṃ paramasukhakaraṃ yoginā bhāvanīyamiti paryantam || 145-146 ||
kuvalaya kalikābāṇamevekṣucāpaṃ hṛīṃ candrādityagarbhe tenotpannārkabhāsobhayakaradhanuṣā pūritākarṇabāṇā |
pratyālīḍhaṃ ca rūḍhā kamalaśaśadharā preṣayet sādhyaveśma sādhyaṃ hṛnnābhiguhye śirasi ca vadane tāḍayitvā śareṇa || 147 ||
kaṇṭhe pāśena baddhvā kṣubhitamapi tayā maṇḍale nīyamānaṃ caṇḍābhirvastrahīnaṃ kṛtamapi niyataṃ veṣṭitaṃ nāginībhiḥ
devībhirbhartsyamānaṃ salaguḍamuṣalaistāḍitaṃ krodhajābhi- bhūtābhirbhīṣyamānaṃ kharanakhanihitaṃ caiva lāsyādibhiśca || 148 ||
vajrābhirnaṣṭabuddhi kṣitijalahutabhugvātajābhiśca baddha bhartuḥ pāde vivastraṃ sakalamadahataṃ pātitaṃ śaktibhiśca
evaṃ kṛtvā tu vaśyaṃ punarapi ca vibhustoṣayet tatra sādhyaṃ [ sādhanā- tadvat pāśāṅkuśābhyāṃ bhavati bahuvidhākṛṣṭikarma tridhā || 149 ||
tathā hnīṃ candrādityagarbhe ityādinā tadvat pāśāṅkuśābhyāṃ bhavati bahuvidhā- kṛṣṭikarma trighātau iti paryantaṃ vaśyākṛṣṭo vṛttatrayaṃ subodham || 147 - 149 ||
| dhyātvā sūryendumadhye kaṣaṇaghananibhaṃ dīrghahū~kā rajāsiṃ tenotpannā vivarṇā tvasikarakamalā tarjanīpāśahastā |
pratyālīḍhoṣṭramūrdhni prakupitavadanā preritā sādhyaveśma sādhyaṃ pāśena baddhvā kupitavadanayā maṇḍaladvāranītam || 150 ||
uṣṭre yaḥ kārajāte varapavanagato bhartṛvākyena sādhyaṃ tatrārūḍhaṃ prakṛtyā śikhicalavalayaṃ prerayed yāvadeva
evamuccāṭanaṃ vai bhavati surapateḥ kiṃ punarmānuṣasya vidveṣe'pyuṣṭra hono bahukṛtakalaho savyavāme ca neyau || 151 ||
tathā vidveṣoccāṭane dhyātvā sūryendugarbhe kaṣaṇadhamanibhaṃ dīrghakāranāsim ityādinā vṛttadvayaṃ subodham || 150 - 151 ||
dhyātvā sūryendugarbhe la iti pariṇataṃ pītavarṇaṃ sucakraṃ tenotpannekavaktrā varakanakanibhā śṛṅkhalācakrahastā |
kūrme daityāsanasthā tvati mṛdugamanā preritā sādhyaveśma sādhyaṃ cakreṇa bheṣyaṃ prapatitamavano śṛṅkhalābaddhapādam ||152||
ānītaṃ maṇḍale ve jinapativacasā pātayitvā dharaṇyāṃ merustanmūrdhni deyo varakanakamayaḥ stambhane sādhyakāye |
ṣaṭsandho kīlanārthaṃ tvapi kuliśamayeḥ kīlakaiḥ kīlanīyaḥ sarvaiḥ sandaṃśyamānaḥ patita iha maho mohane bhāvanīyaḥ || 153 ||
dhyātvā sūryendugarbhela iti pariṇataṃ pītavarṇaṃ sucakram ityādi stambhana - kīlana- mohane vṛttadvayaṃ subodham || 152 - 153 ||
dhyātvā sūryendugarbhe taḍidanalanibhāṃ kartikāṃ phreṃsvabhāvāṃ tenotpannā pracaṇḍā pralayaghananibhā kartikā śuktihastā |
nānāsādhanamahoddeśaḥ pratyālīḍhā vivastrā hyupari hariripoḥ preritā sādhyaveśma sādhyaṃ keśeṣu śīghraṃ dhṛtamapi ca tayā maṇḍale vastrahīnam ||154 ||
ānītaṃ śrīśmaśāne jinapativacasā gṛdhrakākaiḥ śṛgālaḥ sarvāṅgāt pītaraktaṃ palalamapi tathā bhakṣitaṃ sarvadhātum |
sādhyasyaivaṃ samastaṃ pravarabhuvitale māraṇe bhāvanīyaṃ dhyānenānena śakro vrajati yamapuraṃ kiṃ punardhyātvā sūryendugarbhe taḍidanalanibhāṃ kartikā punargarbhajātaḥ || 155 ||
svabhāvām ityādi māraṇe vṛttadvayaṃ subodham |
evaṃ vaśyādinavavṛttāni subodhāni tena na likhi (vyākhyā) tānīti || 154-155 |
idānīṃ śāntāvaparaṃ dhyānamucyate- śānto puṣṭau ca śuklaṃ bhavati kulavaśād dhyānamapyambubījād vaśyākṛṣṭo ca raktaṃ tvapi tanudahanaṃ vahnibījātmakaṃ ca |
vidveṣoccāṭane ca pralayaghananibhaṃ vāyubījasvabhāvaṃ saṃstambhe kīlanādye varakanakanibhaṃ bhūmibījātmakaṃ ca || 156 ||
śāntāvityādi |
iha prathamaṃ tāvadekavīramātmānaṃ kālacakraṃ bhāvayeccaturviṃśa- tibhujaṃ śāntyādivaśyādikarmaṇi, māraṇādistambhanādikarmaṇi ṣaḍviṃśatibhujam |
tato jhaṭityākāreṇa śāntau puṣṭāviti |
iha kālacakrasya hṛdaye toyamaṇḍale toyabīje- notpannā devatā toyātmikā śuklā |
kulavaśādukā rakulavaśāt |
tasyā dhyānaṃ śukladhyānamapyambubījāt śāntī puṣṭau ca bhavati |
tathā vaśyākṛṣṭau ca raktam |
api tanuvahanaṃ vahnibījātmakaṃ kaṇṭhe vahnimaṇḍale ṛkārakulavaśāditi |
ca kṛṣṇaṃ vāyubojasvabhāvaṃ lalāṭe vāyumaṇḍale prāṇasya ikārakulavaśāt |
kīlanādye pītaṃ bhūmibojātmakaṃ nābhau pṛthivīmaṇḍale lakārakulavaśāditi || 156 ||
nīlābhaṃ śūnyabījād bhavati hi haritaṃ māraṇe jīvane ca pṛthvīkṛtsnaṃ samantājjalanidhigamane vāyukṛtsnaṃ ca vṛṣṭeḥ
nāśārthaṃ vahnikṛtsnaṃ tvapi mahivalayaṃ naśārthaṃ toyakṛtsnaṃ bhavati khagamane drāvaṇārthaṃ ca vahne - śūnyakṛtsnaṃ tvadṛśye ||157||
a vimala prabhāyāṃ sādhanā- 'nīlābhaṃ śūnyabījāda guhye jñānamaṇḍale aṃkārakulavaśānmāraṇe
uṣṇīṣe śūnya- maṇḍale haritamakāra kulavaśājjīvane ca
evaṃ ṣaṭsthāneṣu ṣaṭkulavaśāt prāṇa saṃyamāt karmasiddhirbhagavatoktā
idānīṃ pṛthivyādikṛtsnabhāvanocyate pṛthvītyādi
iha yadā yogināṃ devatā siddhā bhavati, tadā nābhau pṛthivīmaṇḍalāt pṛthvīkṛtsnaṃ samudropari setubandhavanniścārya bhāvayet
jalanidhigamane samudropari gacchati, yathā sthale tathā jale pṛthvī kṛtsnadhyāneneti
evaṃ vāyukṛtsnaṃ cātivṛṣṭevināśārthamiti
lalāṭe vāyu- maṇḍalānniścārya vāyukṛtsnaṃ meghopari bhāvayettena meghavṛṣṭi vināśayati
atha pañca- dhātvātmakaṃ kūṭāgāra mātmana upari bhāvayet
tena dhyānena yogī jalena na spṛśyate kūṭasomāparyantam
na meghavṛṣṭiḥ praviśati varṣamāṇāpīti mūlatantre proktam
evaṃ vāyukṛtsnaṃ niścāryāgnimūni vṛṣṭevināśārthamiti vahnikṛtsnamiti
iha kaṇṭhe vahnimaṇḍalā dagnibījapariṇatā jvālā pṛthivyupari bhāvayet
niścārya tābhirvālā- bhirmahivalayaṃ dravati drutakanakavat
evaṃ bhūmidrāvaṇārthaṃ vahnikṛtsnaṃ bhāvanīyam
evaṃ varnāśārthaṃ toyakṛtsnamiti
iha devatāhṛdaye toyamaṇḍalāt toyabījajanitaṃ toyakṛtsnaṃ niścāryāgnimūrdhni bhāvayet
tenāgniḥ śītalo bhavati, na dahanakṣama iti
bhavati khagamane śūnya kṛtsnamiti
uṣṇīṣe ākāśamaṇḍale ākāśakṛtsnaṃ dravyarahitaṃ bhāvayet, tenā- kāśagamanaṃ bhavatīti
tathā caurādyupadrave'dṛśyo bhavati tenaiva dhyāneneti niyamaḥ || 157 ||
idānīṃ tiryagupadravaśamanāya dhyānamucyate- dhyānaṃ pañcānanaṃ vai bhavati gajapaterbhaṅga evāgnibījāt tākṣyaṃ nāgendrabhaṅge bhavati hi dhavalaṃ toyabījātmakaṃ ca
aṣṭāṅghri khaḍgasiṃhe pralayaghananibhaṃ vāyubījātmakaṃ syāt khaḍgākhyaṃ vājiśatroravanikulavaśāt krodhajaṃ daityabhaṅge || 158 ||
dhyānamityādi
iha yadā gajapaterbhayaṃ bhavati, tadā kaṇṭhe agnibījāditi rephādutpannaṃ pañcānanaṃ bhāvayet
tat pañcānanadhyānaṃ bhavati gajapatebhaṃṅgaviṣaye
evaṃ tākṣyaṃ nāgendrabhaṅge hṛdaye toyamaṇḍale toyabījātmakaṃ tadvad dhavalaṃ bhavati
aṣṭāṅghrimiti aṣṭapadam
khaṅgibhaye siṃhabhaye kṛtsnaṃ lalāṭe vāyumaṇḍale vāyu- bojātmakaṃ ceti
khaṅgākhyaṃ vājiśatroriti mahiṣabhaye
avanikulavaśāditi pītaṃ nābhau pṛthivīmaṇḍale lakārabījāditi
krodhajaṃ daityabhaṅge uṣṇīṣe śūnyamaṇḍale śyāme
nīle guhye jñānamaṇḍale vā hū~kārabījātmakaṃ 'kṣaṃkārabījātmakaṃ datyādīnāṃ bhaṅgaviṣaye krodhajaṃ sukhakaraṃ yogināṃ bhavatīti dhyānaniyamastiryagbhaṅgāya || 158 ||
nāsti
paṭale, ślo. ] nānāsādhanamahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ karmasādhanāyādiniyama ucyate- śrīmantraṃ buddhabimbaṃ prathamamapi vibhoryoginā sādhanīyaṃ paścāt siddhyanti karmāṇyaparimitaguṇānyarkabhede : sthitāni
mantre bimbe tvasiddhe tribhuvananilaye siddhayate naiva kiñcit tasmād rājan svacitte vyapagata kaluṣe sādhayenmantrabimbam ||159||
śrīmantramityādi
iha yogināṃ karmasiddhaye prathamaṃ sādhanīyaṃ śrīmantramiti
oṃ āḥ hū~ iti sādhanīyaṃ vakṣyamāṇajāpahoma vidhinā'paramantra siddhaye
evaṃ buddhabimbamiti śūnyatābimbaṃ pratyakṣaṃ karaṇīyamaparadhyānasiddhaye
evaṃ śrīmantraṃ buddhabimbaṃ prathamamapi vibho- yoginā sādhanīyaṃ paścāt siddhayanti karmāṇi, aparimitaguṇānyarka bhedairiti dvādaśabhedaiḥ sthitāni
mantre bimbe tvasiddhe tribhuvananilaye siddhayate naiva kiñcit tasmād rājan svacittaṃ vyapagata kaluṣe sādhayenmantrabimbamādau vibhoriti niyamaḥ || 159 ||
idānīṃ khaṅgādisiddhayarthamasurendrasādhanamucyate- śūraḥ saṃgrāmabhūmau patita iti tathā lambitastaskaro vā aṣṭamyāṃ bhūtarātro nṛpa citibhuvane snāpayedaṣṭakumbhaiḥ
gandherdhūpaiḥ pradīpairbahuvidhaca ruke raktapuṣpaiḥ prapūjya vajranyāsaṃ prakṛtyā śirasi ca hṛdaye mūrdhni nābhau ca kaṇṭhe || 160 ||
śūra ityādinā
iha saṃgrāmabhūmau śūro rājaputra ekanārācaprahāreṇa patito'nyo vā yodhaḥ, tathā 'vṛkṣe lambitastaskaro vā śūraḥ
aṣṭamyāṃ vā bhūtarātrau caturdaśyāṃ vā kṛṣṇapakṣe
nṛpetyāmantraṇam
citibhuvane śmaśāne snāpayet taṃ śavam
aṣṭakumbhairvaśya- karmaṇyuktairjayavijayābhyāṃ ca
tato gandhairdhūpaiḥ pradīpairbahuvidhacaruke raktapuṣpaiḥ prapūjya rakta- vastreṇa paridhānaṃ kṛtvā
vajranyāsaṃ prakṛtyā śirasi ca hṛdaye mūni nābhau ca kaṇṭhe iti
lalāṭe oṃ, hṛdaye hū~, uṣṇīṣe haṃ, nābhau 'ho, kaṇṭhe āḥ, guhyekṣaḥ
evaṃ pūrvoktaṃ hṛdayaṃ śiraḥ śikhā kavacaṃ netramastraṃ ceti ṣaḍaṅganyāsaṃ kṛtvā śatrasyātma- śarīrasyāpi rakṣāṃ kṛtvā devatāyogena || 160 ||
kṛtvā kuṇḍe trikoṇe yadaruṇarajasā garbhapadmaṃ sacihnaṃ patre cihnaṃ jinānāṃ diśi vidiśi tathā devatīnāṃ svacihnam
nāsti
bāhye rekhātraye vai daśadiśi valaye krodhacihnāni tadvat pretaṃ tasyāvasavye tvasikarakamalaṃ maṇḍalāt savyapādam || 161 ||
tatastrikoṇe kuṇḍe pūrvokte 'yadaruṇarajasā garbhapadmaṃ raktaṃ tat sacihnamiti bāṇacihnaṃ kaṇikāyām, athavā sarvakarmaṇi vajram iti vacanāt raktavañcam
patre cihnaṃ jinānāmiti
pūrve patre khaṅgaḥ, dakṣiṇe ratnam, uttare padmam, paścime cakramiti
diśi vidiśi tatheti
devatīnāṃ svacihnamiti
