yadyadatraṃ sādhayet sa tena cihnena tattat kulavidyādharo bhavati kālacakrājñayeti
atha devatāniyamena siddharasavat saptāvarta milati, tadā sādhanaṃ vinā khecarāḥ siddhayo bhavanti, iti mūlatantre niyamaḥ |
iti gulikādisādhanavidhiḥ || 170 ||
jīve dūte sajīve gaganadiśi gate dūta: praśno'samo yo bahusugvaphalado mṛtyumāpnoti daṣṭo mṛtyudoktaḥ samo yaḥ |
dūtaḥ sarpādināma pravadati hi tato mṛtyumāpnoti daṣṭaḥ pṛcchā prāṇapraveśe yadi bhavati śubhā nirgame sā'śubhā syāt || 171 ||
dūto vāmāgrapāda: kathayati yuvatīṃ dakṣiṇāmro naraṃ ca svāṅga hastena yatra spṛśati sa manujo daṣṭamatra pradeśe |
protphullaṃ netravaktraṃ kathayati maraṇaṃ karṇamūle ca kṛṣṇaḥ śabdo hṛtpuṇḍarīke yadi bhavati manāk saṃgrahaṃ tatra kuryāt || 172 ||
ādau rakṣāvidhānaṃ bhavati sukhakaraṃ daṣṭakasyātmanaśca pṛthvītoyā'gnivātā gaganamapi tathā'ṅguṣṭhakādau niyojya |
lādyā hāntāḥ krameṇorujaṭharahṛdaye vaktramadhye lalāṭe hū~kṣū hyahya| dināgā daśaviṣamasamā hrasvadīrghaprabhedeḥ ||173||
vāmāṅga hrasvabījaṃ śravaṇagalagataṃ kakṣa kukṣorudeśe savyāṅga dīrghameva prabhavati phaṇināṃ sṛṣṭisaṃhārayogaiḥ |
rakṣāṃ kṛtvā jinākhyāṃ guḻasusamaśareṇāhibījānvitena huṃkṣūṃyuktena śīghraṃ sunihatahṛdayaḥ stobhamāyāti daṣṭaḥ || 174 |
pṛthvībīje lalāṭe caraṇagatakhaje stambhamāyāti śīghraṃ toye mūrdhni praviṣṭe śikhini ca jaṭhare nirviṣatvaṃ prayāti |
vāyorbīje lalāṭe śikhini ca hṛdaye saṃkramo vai viṣasya śūnye mūrdhni praviṣṭe caraṇagatamaho chedanaṃ vai viṣasya || 175 ||
śveto bindurlalāṭe trividhamapi viṣaṃ nirviṣaṃ ve karoti raktaḥ stobhaṃ praveśaṃ kaṣaṇaghananibhaḥ stambhanaṃ pītavarṇaḥ vati prāṇapravāhe vimalaprabhāyāṃ trikaṭukalulitāṃ yojayenni viṣatve [ sādhanā- aṅgulyā lambikāyāṃ vikasitavadane ṭaṅgaṇaṃ yojayed vā || 176 ||
vajrī jāti: kumārī trikaṭukalavaṇaṃ lāṅgalī devadālī brāhmī cāro'śvagandhā dinakarasahitā vandhyakarkoṭakī ca |
viṇmāṃsaṃ śukraraktaṃ samamapi gulikā kāritā'kṣobhyapiṣṭā bhūtaṃ bhūtajvaraṃ vā sthiramuragaviṣaṃ ghrāṇadattā nihanti || 177||
sūryādī saptavāre dinaniśisamaye saptabhāgāvasāne śūnyā mandārkamadhye pravahati kulikā mṛtyurūpā'rdhanāḍī |
nāgakrīḍāṃ na kuryāt trividhamapi viṣaṃ bhakṣaṇīyaṃ na tatra tasyāmevāhidaṣṭo vrajati yamapuraṃ madhyāhne cārdharātre dina niśisamaye bhūtalabdho'strabhinnaḥ || 178 ||
nityavāraprabhedāt śūnyāndho sandhimadhye pravahati kulikā kāladaṣṭekanāḍī |
pratyūṣe'staṅgate'rke punarapi ca tathā kālanāḍī ca mṛtyo- retānyāsyāni rāhoḥ pratidinasamaye veditavyāni samyak || 179 ||
āditye'nantabhogo dinaniśisamaye cādibhāge dinasya paścāccheṣoragāṇāmudaya iha bhavet saptavāraprabhedāt |
khartuḥ khacchidrakheṣuḥ khayugakhavasavaḥ khādrikhāgniśca nāḍyo bhogāḥ sūryādivārādapi vasuphaṇināṃ bhuktibhedād viṣaṃ syāt || 180 ||
vipro'nanto himābhaḥ kulika iti nṛpo vāsukiḥ śaṅkhapālo rakto vaiśyo mahābjo varakana kanibhastakṣakastadvadeva |
karkoṭako'bjaḥ kaṣaṇaghananibhaścāntyajau viśvavarṇau janmasthānaṃ ca teṣāṃ jalaśikhidharaṇīmārutākāśadhātuḥ || 181 ||
pādāt kaṭayantapīto garuḍa iti tathā nābhisīmno himābha ākaṇṭhād raktavarṇaḥ kaṣaṇaghananibho bhrūlatāṃ yāvadeva |
tasmādvai viśvavarṇaḥ phaṇikulasahito mudritaḥ pañcatattve - rdhyātastanmudrayā ve harati phaṇiviṣaṃ bhūtarogādikaṃ ca || 182 ||
kāraṃ pakṣināthaṃ svahṛdayakamale bhāvayet sūryamūni nāgālaṅkārayuktaṃ sakalakulavaśāt pañcavarṇaṃ sphurantam |
pakṣisvāhāntamādi praṇavamapi tataḥ pakṣināthasya mantraṃ japtvā taṃ koṭimekaṃ phaṇikulasahitaṃ sādhayet pakṣinātham || 183||
tāyeṃ siddhe phaṇīndrāḥ phaṇipatitanayāḥ kiṅkaratvaṃ prayānti bhūtā yakṣā grahāśca pravarabhuvitale ḍākinīmātaraśca |
mantrākṛṣṭi prayānti grahagaṇasakalaṃ jalpate kāladaṣṭaḥ tasmāt sattvārthahetoḥ prathamamapi nareḥ sādhanīyaḥ khagendraḥ || 184 ||
tata ekasaptatyadhikaśatavṛttād' gāruḍavṛttāni subodhāni |
tenātra na likhi- ( vyākhyā) tānīti || 171-184 ||
idānīṃ śāntyādo yantrāṇyucyante- vedeṣvaṣṭau daleṣveva nṛpatiṣu radeṣvabdhiṣaṭsu dvijeṣu garbhe sādhya: svadikṣu prathamamapi yugaṃ yādayo'ṣṭo daleṣu |
eyādyāḥ ṣo ḍaśeṣu triguṇitadaśakāḥ kādihakṣā dvijeṣu sandhyāpatreṣu sādhyastithiguṇitayugeṣveva lāntā: samātrāḥ || 185 ||
vedeṣvityādi |
ihābhiṣekapaṭaloktanyagrodhapatrādike śrīkhaṇḍādinā śītādilekhanyā yantrāṇi lekhyāni śāntyādīni |
tatrāyaṃ kramaḥ - prathamaparimaṇḍale caturdalāni, dvitīye'ṣṭī, tṛtīye ṣoḍaśa, caturthe dvātriṃśat pañcame catuḥṣaṣṭhiḥ, ṣaṣṭhe dvātriṃśaditi |
yathā śarīre uṣṇīṣe hṛdaye lalāṭe kaṇṭhe nābhau guhye ṣaṭcakrāṇi tathā yantralikhane ṣaṭ parimaṇḍa- lānīti |
tatra caturdalamadhye sādhyanāma |
vedeṣviti caturdaleṣu dikṣu prathamam a aṃ yugmamiti |
a pūrve aṃ uttare |
ā paścime |
aḥ dakṣiṇe |
iti prathamaparimaṇḍale |
aṣṭābja- patreṣviti aṣṭadaleṣu yādayaḥ |
i ī pūrve'gnau |
ṛṛ yāmye nairṛtye |
u ū uttareśāne |
la la paścime vāyavye |
iti dvitīyaparimaṇḍale |
evaṃ nṛpatiṣviti tṛtīyaparimaṇḍale ṣoḍaśadaleṣu eyādyā iti pūrvādicaturdaleṣu e ai ya yā, dakṣiṇadaleṣu, ar ār ra rā uttaradaleṣu o au va vā, paścimadaleṣu al āl la lā |
iti tṛtīye parimaṇḍale |
radeṣviti dvātriṃśaddaleṣu triguṇitadaśakā iti triṃśat kādayo hamā iti dvijeṣviti
tatra pūrvādipañcadaleṣu ca cha ja jha ña, dakṣiṇapañcadaleṣu ṭa ṭha ḍa ḍha ṇa, uttare pa pha ba bha ma, paścime ta tha da dha na, evamīśānamārabhya pūrvapatre makārākṣaramārabhya patratraye ka kha ga vimalaprabhāyāṃ iti
'āgneyādārabhya dakṣiṇe ñakārādārabhya patratraye gha ṅa cha iti
nairṛtyādārabhya paścime ṇakārādārabhya patratraye sa paṣa iti
vāyavyādārabhya uttare nakārādārabhya patratrayeśa kaha iti
evaṃ dvātriṃśadda leṣviti caturthaparimaṇḍale
abdhiṣaṭ ṣviti catuḥṣaṣṭidaleṣviti sādhyaḥ
patreṣu cartaṣu pūrve dakṣiṇe paścime uttare sādhyanāma yathā, tathā madhye
evaṃ pañcasthāneṣu sādhya nāma
tithiguṇitayugeṣviti ṣaṣṭidaleṣu yathāsaṃkhyaṃ lāntā: samātrā iti
hayaravalā: samātrā dvādaśamātrāsahitāḥ ṣaṣṭiḥ ṣaṣṭida- leṣu tataḥ sādhyanāma pūrvādau dakṣiṇāvartena ya yā yi yī yū yṛ yu yū yūla ylu yaṃ yaḥ iti dvādaśadaleṣu tato dakṣiṇe sādhyanāmno ra rāri rī ru rū la la raṃ raḥ iti dvādaśadaleṣu
uttare sādhyanāmno va vā vi vī vṛ vṛ vu vū vla vlu vaṃ vaḥ iti dvādaśadaleṣu
paścime sādhyanāmno lalā lilī ḷ ḹ lu lū lḷ llū laṃ la: iti dvādaśadaleṣviti
tataḥ pūrve sādhyasya aparapatre ha, paścime hā, uttare haiṃ, dakṣiṇe haḥ, evaṃ vāmāvartena hi hī īśānāntam, hu ha, agnyantam, huhū vāyavyantam, ha la hala nairṛtyāntamiti
sādhyasya nāmādyakṣaramiti kaṇikāyām dvitīyaṃ pūrve sandhyāpatre, tṛtīyaṃ dakṣiṇe, caturthamuttare, pañcamaṃ paścime
te' hū~ āḥ oṃ ho iti cittavākkāyajñānākṣarāṇi nāmādyakṣarasahitāni lekhyāni || 185 ||
ādyekaikasvarābhyāṃ makaraghaṭavaśād dīrghahasvāśca pañca dvātriṃśad bāhyapatreṣvapi samahṛdayā vajratīkṣṇādivarṇāḥ
bāhye śāntyādikarmaṇyapi ca vayaralā maṇḍalānyeva teṣāṃ varṇā garbhottamāṅgāḥ śikhicalacaraṇā vaśya ākarṣaṇe ca ||186||
athādyekaikasvarābhyāṃ makaraghaṭavaśād doghaṃhasvāśca pañceti
iha makarādi- lagneṣvadhidevā: kā khā gā ghāṅā ityādayaḥ
pūrve sādhyacittākṣarasya dakṣiṇāvartena cā chā jā jhātrā mone, tathā tra jhaja cha ceti meṣe, evaṃ daśapatreṣu
tathā dakṣiṇe vāgvajrasya ṭādayo daśa, uttare kāyavajrasya yādayo daśa, paścime jñānavajrasya tādayo daśa, śeṣadaleṣu viṃśatiṣu paścime pañcadaleṣu kādayaḥ pañca dīrghāḥ, pūrve ṅādaya pañca hasvāḥ, dakṣiṇe sādayaḥ pañca dīrghāḥ, uttare kādayaḥ pañca hrasvāḥ, evaṃ ṣaṣṭivarṇāḥ pañcame parimaṇḍale
dvātriṃśad bāhyapatreṣviti
iha guhyakamale dvātriṃśaddala viśuddhayā dvātriṃśatpatre jg ṣaṣṭhe parimaṇḍale'pi samahṛdayā vajratīkṣṇādivarṇā iti
tadyathā 'vajratīkṣṇa duḥkhacheda' iti īśānamārabhyāgneyaparyantam, tato dakṣiṇe 'prajñājñānamūrtaye' iti, tathā paścime kāyavāgīśvara a iti
tathā uttare 'arapacanāya te namaḥ' iti vajratīkṣṇādivarṇāḥ
evaṃ ṣaṭcakrātmakaṃ yantraṃ likhitvā abhiṣekapaṭaloktavidhinā bāhye śāntyādikarmaṇyapi ca vayaralā maṇḍalānyeva teṣāmiti
idaṃ yantraṃ śāntipuṣṭau jvarāpaharaṇe nirviṣīkaraṇe
udakamaṇḍalena yantraṃ veṣṭayet, vakāreṇa vā
tataścandramaṇḍalamadhye kṣipet, hastimadhye vā
evaṃ māraṇādye vāyumaṇḍalena, vaśyādye tejomaṇḍalena, stambhanādye pṛthivīmaṇḍalena veṣṭayet
śeṣamabhiṣekapaṭaloktaṃ kartavyamiti
iha yantre varṇā garbhottamāṅgā iti garbhaśiraso lekhyā:
śikhicalacaraṇā iti dakṣiṇacaraṇāḥ
sarveṣāṃ meruruttarasthaḥ
garbhakaṇikā iti vaśye ākarṣaṇe ca || 186||
śāntyādau garbhapādāḥ śikhicalaśiraso mantriṇā lekhanīyā: pūrvokta: śāntipuṣṭi bhuvana nidhanatoccāṭanā kṛṣṭivaśyam
sastambhaṃ mohanaṃ ca tribhuvananilaye cakrametat karoti jāpairhomaiśca sādhyaḥ prathamamiha mahāvajratīkṣṇādimantraḥ || 187||
tathā śāntyādau garbhapādā iti uttarapādā mevaṃbhimukhāḥ
śikhicalaśirasa iti dakṣiṇaśirasaḥ
evaṃ māraṇādye pūrvacaraṇāḥ paścimaśirasaḥ, stambhanādye pūrvaśirasaḥ paścimacaraṇā iti
pratyeka patre lekhanīyā mantriṇā pūrvoktaṃrityādi subodha iti ṣaṭcakrayantra niyamaḥ || 187 ||
idānīṃ yamāntakayantramucyate- aṣṭāre dvādaśāre diśividiśigataṃ ṣoḍaśāre'ntare ca sādhya: koṇeṣu madhye prabhavati yamarājāsadomeruṇādyo
tasmād garbhāramadhyād bhavati danirayakṣecca tasmānnirante oṃ hoḥ ṣṭrīḥ tasya bāhye bhavati ca vikṛtādānanād hū~ dvidhā phaṭ || 188 ||
aṣṭāra ityādi
iha nyagrodha' patrādau yantraṃ lekhanīyam
prathamaparimaṇḍalamaṣṭāraṃ dvitīyaṃ dvādaśāraṃ tṛtīyaṃ ṣoḍaśāramiti
tatrāṣṭāreṣu viśividiśigatamiti diśi yamādyakṣaraṃ gatam, vidiśi patre sādhyanāmākṣaraṃ gatam, ṣoḍaśāre cāntarāntara- daleṣvaṣṭasviti
evaṃ sādhyaḥ koṇeṣu
prathamaparimaṇḍale madhye kaṇikāyāṃ prabhavati ya pūrve, ma dvādaśāre pūrve rā, dvitīye jā, tṛtīye sa, caturthe patre do prathamāṣṭāre
dakṣiṇe me
punardvādaśāre
pañcame patre ru ṣaṣṭhe ṇa, saptame yo punaraṣṭāre
paścimapatre da
punardvādaśāre
aṣṭame ni, navame ra, daśame ya
punaraṣṭāre uttare kṣe
punardvādaśāre ekādaśe cca, dvādaśe ni
tathāha - yama rājā sa do me ya ya me do ru ṇa yo da ya
yada yo ni ra ya kṣe ya ya kṣe ya cca nirāmaya || iti mūlatantre
ekādhipatinā ṣoḍaśākṣarāṇi ṣoḍaśadaleṣu
evaṃ madhye prabhavati yamarājāsadomeruṇādyo tasmād garbhāramadhyād bhavati danirayakṣenca tasmānniranta iti
aṣṭāre dvādaśāre niyamaḥ
tataḥ ṣoḍaśāre sādhyanāmāntarāntare patre idaṃ mantraṃ likhet — oṃ hrīḥ ṣṭrīḥ vikṛtānana huṃ huṃ phaṭ iti || 188 ||
- evaṃ kakṣāntarāle bhavati narapate sādhyanāmeṣa mantro vidveṣe mṛtyuvaśye prabhavati ya ma rā kṣeda me do sa cādyāḥ
stambhākṛṣṭau ca mohe'pi ca balakaraṇe śāntikoccāṭane ca garbhāt tasmin yakāro vrajati guṇavaśāt pūrvavadvāhya sarvam || 189 ||
evaṃ kakṣāntarāle bhavati narapate sādhyanāmaiṣa mantro vidveṣe mṛtyuvaśye iti
iha madhye'dhidevo vidveṣe ya, mṛtyo ma, vaśye rā prabhavati
tathā kṣe da me do sa cādyā iti
iha stambhane kṣe, ākṛṣṭau da, mohane me, balakaraṇe do, śāntau sa, uccāṭane cca, jvara- karaṇe ṇa, stobhane rū, jaye jā, santāpaśamane yo, śatrunivāraṇe ni iti
garbhāt tasmin yakāro vrajati guṇavaśāviti
iha karmaṇaḥ svabhāvāt yo varṇo garbhe'dhipatirbhavati, tasya sthāne yakāro likhyate
pūrvavadvāhye sarvamiti yamāntakayantra niyamaḥ || 189 ||
idānīṃ mañjuśrīyantramucyate- varṇānāmuttamāṅgāt prabhavati purato maḥ svarāliṅgitaśca varṇairvagantavarṇaḥ kuliśakulavaśāt pañcamo'haṃ sa uktaḥ
prajñā bindudvayena svaraparamapuṭe syādiyaṃ me'pyukāro maṃ muḥ haṃ huśca saṃsuḥ kamalavasudale mañjurevāpatre || 190||
varṇānāmityādi
iha varṇānāmuttamāṅgāditi varṇānāṃ śirasi binduḥ
tasmādutta- māṅgāt prabhavati purato maḥ svarāliṅgitaśceti
akārasvareṇāliṅgito'nusvāro makāro bhavati
anyacca varṇaiḥ kakārādyairāliṅgito vargānta iti ṅa ñaṇa mana no bhavanti
kuliśakulavaśāt pañcamo'haṃ sa uktaḥ
ato binduraham
prajñā bindudvayena visargeṇa svaraparamapuṭe'kāradvayamadhye syādiyaṃ me'pyukāraḥ
evaṃ maṃ ityupāyo muriti prajñā, evaṃ haṃ huḥ saṃ suḥ mañjurityaṣṭākṣarāṇi daleṣvaṣṭasu
dvitīye dvādaśāre parimaṇḍale evārkapatra iti || 190 ||
bāhye śrīvajraghoṣaḥ prabhavati sayuto mantabhadro'pi hū~ phaṭ sādhyo'smin kaṇikāyāṃ amukamapi kuru codanaṃ śrīsamādeḥ
evaṃ pūrvoktacakreṣvapi bhavati sadā lekhanaṃ sādhyanāmnaḥ etatsavaṃ narāṇāṃ jinapativacasā siddhayate me prasādāt || 191 ||
bāhye prathamadale śrī, dvitīye va, evaṃ krameṇa jra ghoṣa samantabhadra hū~ phaḍiti dvādaśākṣarāṇi śeṣaṃ pūrvavat
sarvamiti
sādhyo'smin kaṇikāyāmiti
sādhyanāmādyakṣaraṃ kaṇikāyām
tato jāpakāle covanaṃ śrīsamāderiti
oṃ śrīvajraghoṣa samantabhadra amukasya śānti kuru kuru namaḥ
evaṃ puṣṭyādike svāhā 'hū~ phaṭ vauṣaṭ phaḍiti ante dātavyam, yantralikhane'pyanti me patre
evaṃ pūrvoktacakreṣvapi bhavati sadā lekhanaṃ sādhya- nāmnaḥ
etatsarvaṃ narāṇāṃ jinapativacasā siddhayate me prasādāditi yantra-
likhanavidhiḥ || 191 ||
yaḥ śabdo hṛtpradeśe bhavati varanṛṇāṃ śrūyate śrotrarandhre- stasmiścittaṃ narasya vrajati samarasaṃ yojitaṃ caikabhūtam |
yaṃ śabdaṃ jīvaloke vadati ca bhavajastattadeva śṛṇoti vijñānaṃ caiva dūrācchravaṇamapi vibhoryoginā bhāvanīyam ||192||
kṛtvā paryaṃṅkabandhaṃ vikasitavadano'nyonyadantaṃ spṛśenna ākṛṣṭo bāhyavātastadamṛtasahito nābhimadhye praviṣṭaḥ |
santāpaṃ kṣutpipāsāṃ harati varatanau sanniruddho viṣaṃ ca śveto bindulalāṭe svaraparikarito muñcamāno'mṛtaṃ vā || 193||

svacittamiti

mudrāsaṅgena tasmāditi

iha sarvasmastantrarāje samājādike upāyatantre, cakrasaṃvarā- dike prajñātantre khalu kuliśapadaṃ pūrvoktamakṣarasukhaṃ yogināmiti
taduktaṃ bhagavatā - tadyathā bhagavān buddhaḥ saṃbuddho'kārasambhavaḥ
akāraḥ sarvavarṇāgyo mahārthaḥ paramākṣaraḥ ||

kiṃ tat ?
jinavarajanakā'nāhataṃ kālacakraṃ taditi niyamo bhagavataḥ sarvatantrāntare yogibhiravagantavyaḥ saṃbuddhapadalābhāyeti
iti śrīmūlatantrānusāriṇyāṃ laghukālacakratantrarājaṭīkāyāṃ vimalaprabhāyāṃ dvādaśasāhasrikāyāṃ nānāsāghana mahoddeśaḥ pañcamaḥ || sādhanāpaṭalasya ṭīkā samāptā
[ 'tanmaṇḍalatritayabodhitaśe karatnarājaprabodhitabhuvaḥ paramādbhūtārthāḥ
uktastu sādhayitumiṣṭatamaḥ parārthaṃ nānārthasādhanavidhiḥ sa ṣaḍaṅgayogaḥ || prāptaṃ mayā kuśalamāvukadattakena saṃlekhya sādhanavidheḥ paṭalaṃ hi tena
lokottarādvayasukhākararatnamūrdhnā jñānaikacakṣuramalaḥ sakalo'stu lokaḥ || ]
